# Towards Update-Dependent Analysis of Query Maintenance

# 查询维护的更新依赖性分析探索

XIAO HU, University of Waterloo, Canada

胡晓（XIAO HU），加拿大滑铁卢大学

QICHEN WANG, Hong Kong Baptist University, China

王启晨（QICHEN WANG），中国香港浸会大学

This paper studies the hardness of maintaining self-join-free conjunctive queries over a dynamic database, where tuples can be inserted or deleted. The worst-case complexity of this problem under arbitrary updates has been well understood. It is known that most practical queries require $\Omega \left( \sqrt{\left| D\right| }\right)$ maintenance time for each update to ensure $O\left( 1\right)$ -delay enumeration,barring a very restricted class of queries (known as "q-hierarchical" queries). Nonetheless, most real-world update sequences are not arbitrary, far away from the worst-case scenario; instead, they are so "nice" that queries can greatly benefit from their inherent structure in query maintenance. In this paper, we aim to understand the hardness of query maintenance under different update sequences, in particular, the insertion-only (or deletion-only), first-in-first-out (FIFO), arbitrarily worse sequences, as well as their "mixed" sequences. We first provide a comprehensive characterization of queries that can be maintained in $O\left( 1\right)$ time for $O\left( 1\right)$ -delay enumeration over FIFO sequences. Then,we address mixed sequences, which may exhibit insertion-only or FIFO patterns on subqueries but lack a specific pattern in totality, and introduce a structural dichotomy for determining whether the input query can be maintained in $O\left( 1\right)$ time for $O\left( 1\right)$ -delay enumeration over mixed sequences.

本文研究动态数据库中无自连接合取查询的维护难度，其中元组可被插入或删除。任意更新下该问题的最坏情况复杂度已被充分理解。已知大多数实际查询需要$\Omega \left( \sqrt{\left| D\right| }\right)$维护时间以确保$O\left( 1\right)$延迟枚举，仅极少数受限查询类别（称为"q-层次化"查询）例外。然而现实世界的更新序列通常并非任意，远优于最坏情况；它们往往具有特定内在结构，使查询维护能从中极大获益。本文旨在探究不同更新序列下的查询维护难度，特别是仅插入（或仅删除）、先进先出（FIFO）、任意恶化序列及其混合序列。我们首先全面刻画了能在FIFO序列上以$O\left( 1\right)$时间维护并实现$O\left( 1\right)$延迟枚举的查询特征。随后针对混合序列——其子查询可能呈现仅插入或FIFO模式但整体缺乏特定模式——提出了结构二分法，用以判定输入查询能否在混合序列上以$O\left( 1\right)$时间维护并实现$O\left( 1\right)$延迟枚举。

## CCS Concepts: - Theory of computation $\rightarrow$ Database query processing and optimization (theory).

## CCS概念：- 计算理论$\rightarrow$数据库查询处理与优化（理论）

Additional Key Words and Phrases: conjunctive query, insertion-only updates, FIFO updates, mixed updates

附加关键词：合取查询，仅插入更新，FIFO更新，混合更新

## 1 INTRODUCTION

## 1 引言

Dynamic query processing is a challenging problem that has been extensively investigated $\lbrack 4,7,8$ , ${11},{13},{16},{18} - {20},{22},{23},{25},{26}\rbrack$ ,which studies how to maintain the query results over a dynamic database, where tuples can be inserted or deleted. Instead of computing query results from scratch each time, a common approach is to design a data structure that can be updated efficiently while the query answers can be retrieved with a delay guarantee. The worst-case complexity of this problem has been well understood $\left\lbrack  {7,{16}}\right\rbrack$ ,but only a limited class of queries can admit an efficient index, and the large remaining queries only have a very expensive index. More importantly, the worst-case update sequence rarely occurs in practice. These observations motivate us to investigate more instance-dependent complexity of this problem, such as insertion-only and FIFO sequences.

动态查询处理是经广泛研究的挑战性问题$\lbrack 4,7,8$${11},{13},{16},{18} - {20},{22},{23},{25},{26}\rbrack$，其核心在于如何维护动态数据库（支持元组增删）上的查询结果。常见方法是设计可高效更新的数据结构，而非每次都从头计算查询结果，同时保证查询应答的延迟可控。该问题的最坏情况复杂度已明确$\left\lbrack  {7,{16}}\right\rbrack$，但仅有有限查询类别支持高效索引，多数查询仍需昂贵索引。更重要的是，最坏更新序列在实践中极少出现。这些观察促使我们探究该问题更多实例相关的复杂度特性，如仅插入序列和FIFO序列。

Furthermore, practical update sequences usually exhibit patterns (insertion-only, FIFO, or arbitrary) over subqueries but lack a specific pattern in total. Let's look at the TPC-H benchmark [1], which has broad industry-wide relevance. There are 8 individual relations in the schema - NATION and REGION are public as well as static, which can be modeled as a special insertion-only sequence before any update from other relations; PART, SUPPLIER and CUSTOMER are insertion-only, as these entities are not deleted from database once inserted; in contrast, LINEITEM, ORDER, and PARTSUPP are subject to frequent updates, which could be FIFO or even arbitrary. Consider a simplified query in this benchmark that finds all parts and supplier information:

实际更新序列通常在子查询上呈现特定模式（仅插入、FIFO或任意），但整体缺乏统一模式。以具有广泛行业相关性的TPC-H基准[1]为例：模式中含8个独立关系——NATION和REGION是公开静态的，可建模为其他关系更新前的特殊仅插入序列；PART、SUPPLIER和CUSTOMER是仅插入的，因这些实体入库后不会被删除；而LINEITEM、ORDER和PARTSUPP则频繁更新，可能是FIFO甚至任意模式。考虑该基准中查找零件与供应商信息的简化查询：

$$
\text{PART(PK)} \bowtie  \text{PARTSUPP(PK,SK)} \bowtie  \text{SUPPLIER(SK)}
$$

From our previous knowledge [7], this query is very costly to maintain as it is non-q-hierarchical. However, if updates in PART and SUPPLIER are insertion-only, this query becomes easy to maintain, even if updates in PARTSUPP are arbitrary [25]. To bridge the gap between existing theory and practical applications, we aim for a more fine-grained analysis of maintaining CQs over mixed sequences that display specific patterns (insertion-only or FIFO) on sub-queries.

根据已有研究[7]，该查询因非q-层次化而维护成本极高。但若PART和SUPPLIER的更新是仅插入的，即使PARTSUPP的更新是任意的[25]，该查询也变得易于维护。为弥合理论与实践的鸿沟，我们旨在对混合序列（子查询呈现仅插入或FIFO等特定模式）上的合取查询维护进行更细粒度分析。

### 1.1 Problem Definition

### 1.1 问题定义

Conjunctive Query. Let $\mathbb{R}$ be a database schema with $m$ relations ${R}_{1},{R}_{2},\cdots ,{R}_{m}$ and $n$ attributes $\mathcal{V} = \left\{  {{x}_{1},{x}_{2},\cdots ,{x}_{n}}\right\}$ . Each relation ${R}_{i}$ is defined on a subset of attributes ${e}_{i} \subseteq  \mathcal{V}$ . Let $\operatorname{dom}\left( x\right)$ be the domain of attribute $x$ . Let $\operatorname{dom}\left( X\right)  = \mathop{\prod }\limits_{{x \in  X}}\operatorname{dom}\left( x\right)$ be the domain of a subset of attributes $X$ . Let $D$ be a given instance of $\mathbb{R}$ ,and the corresponding instances of ${R}_{1},\cdots ,{R}_{m}$ be ${R}_{1}^{D},\cdots ,{R}_{m}^{D}$ , where ${R}_{i}^{D}$ is a finite set of tuples from $\operatorname{dom}\left( {e}_{i}\right)$ . When the context is clear,we drop the superscript and use ${R}_{i}$ for relation and instance.

合取查询(Conjunctive Query)。设$\mathbb{R}$为具有$m$个关系${R}_{1},{R}_{2},\cdots ,{R}_{m}$和$n$个属性$\mathcal{V} = \left\{  {{x}_{1},{x}_{2},\cdots ,{x}_{n}}\right\}$的数据库模式。每个关系${R}_{i}$定义在属性子集${e}_{i} \subseteq  \mathcal{V}$上。设$\operatorname{dom}\left( x\right)$为属性$x$的域，$\operatorname{dom}\left( X\right)  = \mathop{\prod }\limits_{{x \in  X}}\operatorname{dom}\left( x\right)$为属性子集$X$的域。设$D$为$\mathbb{R}$的给定实例，其对应${R}_{1},\cdots ,{R}_{m}$的实例为${R}_{1}^{D},\cdots ,{R}_{m}^{D}$，其中${R}_{i}^{D}$是来自$\operatorname{dom}\left( {e}_{i}\right)$的有限元组集合。当上下文明确时，省略上标并用${R}_{i}$表示关系及其实例。

In this paper,we consider the class of self-join-free conjunctive queries (CQs) formally defined as ${}^{1}$

本文研究无自连接的合取查询类(CQs)，其形式化定义为${}^{1}$

$$
Q \mathrel{\text{:=}} {\pi }_{\mathbf{y}}\left( {{R}_{1}\left( {e}_{1}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{2}\left( {e}_{2}\right)  \bowtie  \cdots  \bowtie  {R}_{m}\left( {e}_{m}\right) }\right) ,
$$

where $\mathrm{y} \subseteq  \mathcal{V}$ denotes the output attributes. Let $\overline{\mathrm{y}} = \mathcal{V} - \mathrm{y}$ denote the non-output attributes. Each ${R}_{i}$ in $Q$ is distinct,i.e.,the CQ does not have a self-join. If $\mathbf{y} = \left\{  {{x}_{1},{x}_{2},\cdots ,{x}_{n}}\right\}$ ,such a CQ is a full join, i.e., the natural join of underlying relations. For simplicity, we omit ${\pi }_{\mathbf{y}}$ for a full join. If $\mathbf{y} = \varnothing$ ,such a CQ is known as a Boolean ${CQ}$ ,which indicates whether there exists any result of the underlying join query. We also use a triple(V,E,y)to represent $Q$ ,where $\mathcal{E} = \left\{  {{e}_{1},{e}_{2},\cdots ,{e}_{m}}\right\}$ .

其中$\mathrm{y} \subseteq  \mathcal{V}$表示输出属性，$\overline{\mathrm{y}} = \mathcal{V} - \mathrm{y}$表示非输出属性。$Q$中每个${R}_{i}$互不相同，即该CQ不含自连接。若$\mathbf{y} = \left\{  {{x}_{1},{x}_{2},\cdots ,{x}_{n}}\right\}$，此类CQ称为完全连接，即底层关系的自然连接。为简化表述，完全连接中省略${\pi }_{\mathbf{y}}$。若$\mathbf{y} = \varnothing$，则称为布尔型${CQ}$，用于判定底层连接查询是否存在结果。我们亦用三元组(V,E,y)表示$Q$，其中$\mathcal{E} = \left\{  {{e}_{1},{e}_{2},\cdots ,{e}_{m}}\right\}$。

图1. CQs分类体系

We use Figure 1 to illustrate the relationship between different classes of CQs to be discussed throughout the paper and defer their formal definitions to Section 2.

通过图1展示本文讨论的各类CQ间关系，其形式化定义将在第2节详述。

Updates and Update Sequence. In the dynamic setting, we model each update as a quadruple $\left( {t,s, + /-,{R}_{i}}\right)$ for $s \in  \mathbb{Z}$ ,indicating that tuple $t$ is inserted into (+) or deleted from (-) relation ${R}_{i}$ at timestamp $s$ . Let $S$ be a sequence of updates ordered by their timestamp,where only a single update occurs at any given timestamp. An enumeration procedure may be invoked after any timestamp. Rather than specifying a separate preprocessing step, the initial database state may be simulated by using an insertion-only update sequence concatenated as the prefix of an arbitrary update sequence. Suppose the initial database contains $n$ tuples and that the current timestamp is 0 . Each tuple $t \in  {R}_{i}$ will be assigned a distinct timestamp $s$ within the range $\left\lbrack  {-n, - 1}\right\rbrack$ . Consequently,for each tuple $t$ , the update sequence includes the quadruple $\left( {t,s,+,{R}_{i}}\right)$ for each tuple $t$ . For the remainder of this discussion, we assume the initial database is (logically) empty, with all pre-existing tuples inserted at negative timestamps.

更新与更新序列。在动态场景中，每个更新建模为四元组$\left( {t,s, + /-,{R}_{i}}\right)$（针对$s \in  \mathbb{Z}$），表示在时间戳$s$向关系${R}_{i}$插入(+)或删除(-)元组$t$。设$S$为按时间戳排序的更新序列，任一时刻仅发生单次更新。枚举过程可在任意时间戳后触发。初始数据库状态可通过在任意更新序列前拼接纯插入更新序列来模拟，无需单独预处理步骤。假设初始数据库含$n$个元组且当前时间戳为0，每个元组$t \in  {R}_{i}$将被分配$\left\lbrack  {-n, - 1}\right\rbrack$范围内的唯一时间戳$s$。因此对于每个元组$t$，更新序列包含四元组$\left( {t,s,+,{R}_{i}}\right)$。下文讨论中，我们假定初始数据库(逻辑上)为空，所有既有元组均在负时间戳插入。

Under set semantics,inserting $t$ into ${R}_{i}$ has no effect if ${R}_{i}$ already contains $t$ ,and likewise,deleting $t$ has no effect if $t$ is not present in ${R}_{i}$ . For a given update sequence $S$ ,the lifespan of a tuple $t$ is an interval $\left\lbrack  {{t}^{ + },{t}^{ - }}\right\rbrack$ ,where ${t}^{ + }$ denotes the timestamp when $t$ is inserted,and ${t}^{ - }$ denotes the timestamp when $t$ is deleted. For any tuple $t$ not deleted by the end of $S$ ,we set ${t}^{ - } =  + \infty$ to indicate its persistence beyond the scope of the considered timestamp. Moreover,we treat two $+ \infty$ values as incomparable, thereby ensuring that the single-update assumption is maintained. It is possible for the same tuple to be inserted and subsequently deleted multiple times. For simplicity, these instances are regarded as logically distinct tuples, with each one possessing a unique lifespan. These lifespans, corresponding to the same physical tuple, are assumed to be disjoint under set semantics. The dynamic database $D$ is defined by the update sequence $S$ in such a way that for every timestamp $s \in  \mathbb{Z}$ ,the set of tuples $t$ for which $s \in  \left\lbrack  {{t}^{ + },{t}^{ - }}\right\rbrack$ constitutes a snapshot of $D$ . The size of the dynamic database $D$ is then defined as the maximum number of tuples that coexist at any given timestamp.

在集合语义下，若${R}_{i}$已包含$t$，则插入$t$的操作无效；同理，若${R}_{i}$中不存在$t$，删除操作亦无效。对于给定更新序列$S$，元组$t$的生命周期为区间$\left\lbrack  {{t}^{ + },{t}^{ - }}\right\rbrack$，其中${t}^{ + }$表示插入时间戳，${t}^{ - }$表示删除时间戳。对于在$S$结束时未被删除的元组$t$，我们设定${t}^{ - } =  + \infty$以表明其持续存在于考虑的时间戳范围之外。此外，我们将两个$+ \infty$值视为不可比较，从而确保单次更新假设成立。同一元组可能被多次插入和删除，为简化处理，这些实例被视为逻辑上不同的元组，各自拥有独立生命周期。在集合语义下，这些对应于同一物理元组的生命周期被假定为互不相交。动态数据库$D$由更新序列$S$定义，使得对于每个时间戳$s \in  \mathbb{Z}$，满足$s \in  \left\lbrack  {{t}^{ + },{t}^{ - }}\right\rbrack$的元组$t$集合构成$D$的快照。动态数据库$D$的大小定义为任意时间戳下共存元组的最大数量。

${}^{1}$因此，本文后续将"无自连接CQ"简称为"CQ"。

We are particularly interested in two classes of update sequences that are practically important:

我们特别关注两类具有实际重要性的更新序列：

- First-in-first-out (FIFO). An update sequence $S$ is FIFO if,for any pair of tuples ${t}_{1},{t}_{2}$ ,if ${t}_{1}^{ + } < {t}_{2}^{ + }$ , then ${t}_{1}^{ - } < {t}_{2}^{ - }$ or ${t}_{2}^{ - } =  + \infty$ ,and not FIFO otherwise. FIFO sequences are commonly used in sliding-window or tumbling-window models applied to streaming data.

- 先进先出(FIFO)。若对于任意元组对${t}_{1},{t}_{2}$，当${t}_{1}^{ + } < {t}_{2}^{ + }$时满足${t}_{1}^{ - } < {t}_{2}^{ - }$或${t}_{2}^{ - } =  + \infty$，则更新序列$S$为FIFO，否则非FIFO。FIFO序列常用于流数据处理中的滑动窗口或滚动窗口模型。

- Insertion-only or Deletion-only. An update sequence $S$ is insertion-only if for every tuple $t$ , ${t}^{ - } =  + \infty$ ,and not insertion-only otherwise. By definition,insertion-only sequences are inherently FIFO. Symmetrically,an update sequence $S$ is deletion-only if for every tuple $t,{t}^{ + } < 0$ ,and not deletion-only otherwise. In subsequent discussions, we will focus on the insertion-only case, whereas an analysis of the deletion-only case is deferred to Appendix C.

- 纯插入或纯删除。若对每个元组$t$满足${t}^{ - } =  + \infty$，则更新序列$S$为纯插入，否则非纯插入。根据定义，纯插入序列本质上是FIFO的。对称地，若对每个元组$t,{t}^{ + } < 0$满足删除条件，则更新序列$S$为纯删除，否则非纯删除。后续讨论将聚焦纯插入情形，纯删除情形的分析详见附录C。

For a $\mathrm{{CQ}}\mathcal{Q} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E},\mathbf{y}}\right)$ and an update sequence $S$ ,the projection of $S$ to a subset ${\mathcal{E}}_{1} \subseteq  \mathcal{E}$ of relations is defined as ${\pi }_{{\mathcal{E}}_{1}}S = \left\{  {\left( {*,*,+/-,{R}_{e}}\right)  \in  S : e \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{1}}\right\}$ ,that is,it consists solely of those updates in $S$ that pertain to relations from ${\mathcal{E}}_{1}$ . We define a pattern $\sigma  : {2}^{\mathcal{E}} \rightarrow  \{$ insertion-only,FIFO,arbitrary $\}$ to characterize update sequences for $Q$ . An update sequence $S$ is said to be consistent with the pattern $\sigma$ ,if for every subset ${\mathcal{E}}_{1} \subseteq  \mathcal{E}$ of relations,the projection ${\pi }_{{\mathcal{E}}_{1}}S$ adheres to the update behavior specified by $\sigma \left( {\mathcal{E}}_{1}\right)$ . Furthermore, $S$ is called a $\sigma$ -sequence if it is consistent with $\sigma$ . The projection of the pattern $\sigma$ onto a subset ${\mathcal{E}}_{1} \subseteq  \mathcal{E}$ of relations is defined as $\left( {{\pi }_{{\mathcal{E}}_{1}}\sigma }\right)  : {2}^{{\mathcal{E}}_{1}} \rightarrow$ \{insertion-only,FIFO,arbitrary\},such that for every subset ${\mathcal{E}}_{2} \subseteq  {\mathcal{E}}_{1},\left( {{\pi }_{{\mathcal{E}}_{1}}\sigma }\right) \left( {\mathcal{E}}_{2}\right)  = \sigma \left( {\mathcal{E}}_{2}\right)$ .

对于$\mathrm{{CQ}}\mathcal{Q} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E},\mathbf{y}}\right)$和更新序列$S$，将$S$投影到关系子集${\mathcal{E}}_{1} \subseteq  \mathcal{E}$上的操作定义为${\pi }_{{\mathcal{E}}_{1}}S = \left\{  {\left( {*,*,+/-,{R}_{e}}\right)  \in  S : e \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{1}}\right\}$，即该投影仅包含$S$中与${\mathcal{E}}_{1}$相关的更新操作。我们定义了模式$\sigma  : {2}^{\mathcal{E}} \rightarrow  \{${仅插入、先进先出、任意}$\}$来描述$Q$的更新序列特征。若对于每个关系子集${\mathcal{E}}_{1} \subseteq  \mathcal{E}$，其投影${\pi }_{{\mathcal{E}}_{1}}S$都符合$\sigma \left( {\mathcal{E}}_{1}\right)$指定的更新行为，则称更新序列$S$与模式$\sigma$一致。进一步地，若$S$与$\sigma$一致，则称其为$\sigma$序列。将模式$\sigma$投影到关系子集${\mathcal{E}}_{1} \subseteq  \mathcal{E}$上定义为$\left( {{\pi }_{{\mathcal{E}}_{1}}\sigma }\right)  : {2}^{{\mathcal{E}}_{1}} \rightarrow${仅插入、先进先出、任意}，使得对于每个子集${\mathcal{E}}_{2} \subseteq  {\mathcal{E}}_{1},\left( {{\pi }_{{\mathcal{E}}_{1}}\sigma }\right) \left( {\mathcal{E}}_{2}\right)  = \sigma \left( {\mathcal{E}}_{2}\right)$都成立。

Enumeration. We focus on $O\left( 1\right)$ -delay enumeration,such that the time from the start of the enumeration to the first result, the time between any consecutive pair of results, and the time from the last result to the termination of the enumeration process,are $O\left( 1\right)$ . In this paper,we aim to understand the maintenance complexity of CQs over update sequences consistent to a given pattern $\sigma$ ,if targeting $O\left( 1\right)$ -delay enumeration. For simplicity," $Q$ can (resp. cannot) be maintained in $\alpha$ time over a set $\mathcal{S}$ of update sequences" should be translated as "there exists an index (resp. there doesn’t exist an index) that can be maintained over an arbitrary update sequence $S \in  \mathcal{S}$ in amortized $\alpha$ time per update,and support $O\left( 1\right)$ -delay enumeration for $Q$ whenever needed".

枚举。我们关注$O\left( 1\right)$延迟枚举，要求从枚举开始到首个结果的时间、任意两个连续结果之间的时间、以及最后一个结果到枚举终止的时间都控制在$O\left( 1\right)$内。本文旨在理解针对$O\left( 1\right)$延迟枚举时，符合给定模式$\sigma$的更新序列上CQs的维护复杂度。简言之，"$Q$能在（或不能在）$\alpha$时间内维护于更新序列集合$\mathcal{S}$上"应理解为"存在（或不存在）一种索引，可在任意更新序列$S \in  \mathcal{S}$上以每次更新摊还$\alpha$时间进行维护，并在需要时支持$Q$的$O\left( 1\right)$延迟枚举"。

Model of Computation. We use the standard RAM model under the uniform cost measure. For an input of size $N$ ,every register is assumed to have a length $O\left( {\log N}\right)$ . Any arithmetic operation - such as addition, subtraction, multiplication, division - as well as the concatenation of the values of two registers,can be performed in $O\left( 1\right)$ time. Moreover,retrieving the content of any register by its unique address is also achievable in $O\left( 1\right)$ time.

计算模型。我们采用均匀成本度量下的标准RAM模型。对于大小为$N$的输入，假定每个寄存器长度为$O\left( {\log N}\right)$。任何算术操作——包括加减乘除及寄存器值拼接——都可在$O\left( 1\right)$时间内完成。此外，通过唯一地址获取寄存器内容同样可在$O\left( 1\right)$时间内实现。

### 1.2 Previous Results

### 1.2 先前研究成果

Maintaining CQs under arbitrary updates. In 2017, two independent papers [7, 16] investigated the worst-case complexity of dynamically maintaining CQs under arbitrary updates. Their results establish the following dichotomy: Any $q$ -hierarchical CQ can be maintained in $O\left( 1\right)$ time under arbitrary updates, while for non-q-hierarchical CQs, a polynominal lower bound on the update time has been demonstrated, assuming the OMv and OuMv conjectures.

任意更新下的CQ维护。2017年，两篇独立论文[7,16]研究了任意更新下动态维护CQ的最坏情况复杂度。其成果建立了如下二分定理：任何$q$层次化CQ可在$O\left( 1\right)$时间内维护于任意更新下，而对于非q层次化CQ，在OMv和OuMv猜想成立时，已证明存在多项式时间复杂度的更新下限。

THEOREM 1.1 ([7,16]). A CQ Q can be maintained in $O\left( 1\right)$ time over arbitrary updates if $Q$ is q-hierarchical,and cannot be maintained in $O\left( {\sqrt{\left| D\right| }}^{1 - \epsilon }\right)$ time for arbitrary $\epsilon  > 0$ otherwise,assuming the OMv and OuMv conjectures.

定理1.1（文献[7,16]）。若$Q$满足q-分层性，则CQ查询Q可在$O\left( 1\right)$时间内维护任意更新；否则在OMv与OuMv猜想成立的前提下，对于任意$\epsilon  > 0$无法在$O\left( {\sqrt{\left| D\right| }}^{1 - \epsilon }\right)$时间内完成维护。

CONJECTURE 1.2 (OMV CONJECTURE [15]). The following problem cannot be solved in $O\left( {n}^{3 - \epsilon }\right)$ time for any constant $\epsilon  > 0$ : Given an $n \times  n$ Boolean matrix $M$ and a sequence of $n$ -dimensional Boolean vectors ${v}_{1},{v}_{2},\cdots ,{v}_{n}$ ,it is required to output $M{v}_{i}$ before seeing ${v}_{i + 1}$ ,for every $i \in  \left\lbrack  n\right\rbrack$ .

猜想1.2（OMv猜想[15]）。对于任意常数$\epsilon  > 0$，下列问题无法在$O\left( {n}^{3 - \epsilon }\right)$时间内解决：给定$n \times  n$布尔矩阵$M$和$n$维布尔向量序列${v}_{1},{v}_{2},\cdots ,{v}_{n}$，需在见到${v}_{i + 1}$前输出$M{v}_{i}$（对每个$i \in  \left\lbrack  n\right\rbrack$）。

CONJECTURE 1.3 (OUMv CONJECTURE [15]). The following problem cannot be solved in $O\left( {n}^{3 - \epsilon }\right)$ time for any constant $\epsilon  > 0$ : Given an $n \times  n$ Boolean matrix $M$ and a sequence of n-dimensional Boolean vector pairs $\left( {{u}_{1},{v}_{1}}\right) ,\left( {{u}_{2},{v}_{2}}\right) ,\cdots ,\left( {{u}_{n},{v}_{n}}\right)$ ,it is required to output ${u}_{i}^{T}M{v}_{i}$ before seeing $\left( {{u}_{i + 1},{v}_{i + 1}}\right)$ ,for every $i \in  \left\lbrack  n\right\rbrack$ .

猜想1.3（OuMv猜想[15]）。对于任意常数$\epsilon  > 0$，下列问题无法在$O\left( {n}^{3 - \epsilon }\right)$时间内解决：给定$n \times  n$布尔矩阵$M$和n维布尔向量对序列$\left( {{u}_{1},{v}_{1}}\right) ,\left( {{u}_{2},{v}_{2}}\right) ,\cdots ,\left( {{u}_{n},{v}_{n}}\right)$，需在见到$\left( {{u}_{i + 1},{v}_{i + 1}}\right)$前输出${u}_{i}^{T}M{v}_{i}$（对每个$i \in  \left\lbrack  n\right\rbrack$）。

Later,this $\Omega \left( \sqrt{\left| D\right| }\right)$ lower bound was matched for certain specific non-q-hierarchical CQs,such as the triangle [18] and the length-4 cycle [14]. Moreover, any free-connex CQ can be maintained in $O\left( \left| D\right| \right)$ time under arbitrary updates [16,25]. Consequently,a $\sqrt{\left| D\right| }$ -gap exists between the lower and upper bounds for general non-q-hierarchical CQs. In addition, the tradeoff between update time and enumeration delay has also been investigated for specific queries, including the triangle query [18] and hierarchical CQs [19]. However, the derived theoretical lower bounds typically require only the identification of a single hard instance among all possible update sequences, which results in particularly loose lower bounds for many simple update sequences. Recently, people have investigated the instance-dependent complexity of maintaining CQs based on the enclosureness of update sequences [26] for foreign-key acyclic joins. Building on this idea, Wang et al. [25] recently extended the notion of enclosureness to free-connex CQs, and demonstrated that such CQs can be maintained in time proportional to the enclosureness of the update sequence. The formal definition of enclosureness will be presented in Section 2. This concept constitutes a significant aspect of our dichotomy results, as it helps establish the optimality of our lower bounds.

后续研究针对特定非q-分层CQ（如三角形查询[18]和长度4环查询[14]）实现了该$\Omega \left( \sqrt{\left| D\right| }\right)$下界。此外，任意自由连接CQ在任意更新下可保持$O\left( \left| D\right| \right)$时间维护[16,25]，导致一般非q-分层CQ的上下界存在$\sqrt{\left| D\right| }$间隙。针对三角形查询[18]和分层CQ[19]等特定查询，学者们还探究了更新时间与枚举延迟的权衡关系。然而现有理论下界通常只需在全部更新序列中找出单个困难实例，导致对多数简单更新序列的下界约束过于宽松。近期研究基于外键无环连接的更新序列封闭性[26]，探讨了CQ维护的实例相关复杂度。王等人[25]将封闭性概念扩展至自由连接CQ，证明此类查询的维护时间可与更新序列封闭性成正比。第2节将给出封闭性的形式化定义，该概念是我们二分结果的重要基础，有助于确立下界的最优性。

LEMMA 1.4 ([25],LEMMA 6.6). For a free-connex ${CQQ}$ with a free-connex join tree $\mathcal{T}$ ,there is an index that can be updated in $O\left( \lambda \right)$ amortized time over an update sequence of enclosureness $\lambda$ under $\mathcal{T}$ ,while supporting $O\left( 1\right)$ -delay enumeration for $\mathcal{Q}$ .

引理1.4（文献[25]引理6.6）。对于具有自由连接树$\mathcal{T}$的自由连接${CQQ}$，存在索引可在$\mathcal{T}$下以$O\left( \lambda \right)$摊还时间维护封闭性为$\lambda$的更新序列，同时支持$\mathcal{Q}$的$O\left( 1\right)$延迟枚举。

Enumerating CQs over static databases. Enumerating CQs over static databases can be simulated by an insertion-only sequence as follows: (i) the initial database is assumed to be empty; (ii) input tuples are inserted sequentially, one by one; and (iii) after all tuples have been inserted into the database, an enumeration procedure is invoked to generate the query results.

静态数据库上的CQ枚举。静态数据库的CQ枚举可通过仅插入序列模拟：(i)初始数据库为空；(ii)逐个插入输入元组；(iii)所有元组插入完成后调用枚举程序生成查询结果。

LEMMA 1.5 ([16]). For any ${CQQ}$ ,if $Q$ can be maintained in $\alpha$ time over insertion-only sequences, then for an arbitrary database $D$ ,an index can be built in $O\left( {\alpha  \cdot  \left| D\right| }\right)$ preprocessing time,from which all query results of $\mathcal{Q}\left( D\right)$ can be enumerated with $O\left( 1\right)$ delay.

引理1.5（文献[16]）。对于任意${CQQ}$，若能在仅插入序列上以$\alpha$时间维护$Q$，则可为任意数据库$D$在$O\left( {\alpha  \cdot  \left| D\right| }\right)$预处理时间内构建索引，使得$\mathcal{Q}\left( D\right)$的所有查询结果都能以$O\left( 1\right)$延迟枚举。

From Lemma 1.4, Wang et al. [25] also obtained the following results:

根据引理1.4，Wang等人[25]还得出以下结论：

Corollary 1.6 ([25],THEOREM 6.11). A free-connex ${CQQ}$ can be maintained in $O\left( 1\right)$ time over insertion-only update sequences.

推论1.6（文献[25]定理6.11）。自由连接${CQQ}$可在仅插入更新序列上以$O\left( 1\right)$时间维护。

On the other hand, any lower bound for enumerating CQs over static databases implies a lower bound for maintaining CQs over insertion-only sequences. Bagan et al. [5] and Brault-Baron [9] showed that after $O\left( \left| D\right| \right)$ preprocessing time,a CQ $Q$ over any static database $D$ can be enumerated with $O\left( 1\right)$ delay,if and only if $Q$ is free-connex. Putting everything together,we have:

另一方面，静态数据库上枚举CQ的任何下界都意味着仅插入序列维护CQ的下界。Bagan等人[5]和Brault-Baron[9]证明：经过$O\left( \left| D\right| \right)$预处理时间后，当且仅当CQ$Q$是自由连接时，才能在任意静态数据库$D$上以$O\left( 1\right)$延迟枚举该查询。综合这些结果可得：

THEOREM 1.7. A CQQ can be maintained in $O\left( 1\right)$ time over insertion-only updates if $\mathcal{Q}$ is free-connex and cannot be maintained in $O\left( 1\right)$ time otherwise,assuming the Boolean Matrix Multiplication, Triangle Detection, and HyperClique conjectures.

定理1.7。在布尔矩阵乘法猜想、三角形检测猜想和超团猜想成立的条件下，当CQ$\mathcal{Q}$为自由连接时，可在仅插入更新中以$O\left( 1\right)$时间维护；否则无法在$O\left( 1\right)$时间内维护。

CONJECTURE 1.8 (TRIANGLE DETECTION CONJECTURE [2]). Given a graph with $m$ edges,no algorithm can decide whether a triangle exists in $O\left( m\right)$ time.

猜想1.8（三角形检测猜想[2]）。给定具有$m$条边的图，不存在算法能在$O\left( m\right)$时间内判定三角形是否存在。

Conjecture 1.9 (Boolean Matrix Multiplication [24] Conjecture). Given two Boolean matrices of size $n \times  n$ ,no algorithm can compute their product in $O\left( {n}^{2}\right)$ time.

猜想1.9（布尔矩阵乘法猜想[24]）。给定两个$n \times  n$阶布尔矩阵，不存在算法能在$O\left( {n}^{2}\right)$时间内计算其乘积。

CONJECTURE 1.10 (HYPERCLIQUE CONJECTURE [21]). Given a k-uniform hypergraph (for $k \geq  3$ ), no algorithm can decide whether a hyper-clique of size $k + 1$ exists or not in $O\left( m\right)$ time,i.e.,a set of $k + 1$ vertices where every subset of size $k$ forms a hyperedge,where $m$ is the number of edges in the hypergraph.

猜想1.10（超团猜想[21]）。给定k-均匀超图（当$k \geq  3$时），不存在算法能在$O\left( m\right)$时间内判定是否存在$k + 1$阶超团——即$k + 1$个顶点的集合，其中每个$k$顶点子集都构成超边，此处$m$表示超图的边数。

Enumerating CQs under other updates. In an independent work, Kara et al. [17] studied the enumeration of CQs over databases where some relations remain static while others undergo arbitrary updates. Their goal is to characterize the class of CQs for which an index can be maintained with $O\left( 1\right)$ amortized update time while supporting $O\left( 1\right)$ -delay enumeration. They further refine this characterization by considering three cases based on whether the preprocessing time is restricted to linear, polynomial, or exponential. In contrast, our work investigates a more general setting where updates in each relation - or even a subset of relations - may follow different patterns, including insertion-only, FIFO, or arbitrary. However, we implicitly restrict the preprocessing time to be linear complexity. As the settings considered in these other works differ fundamentally from our framework, direct comparisons are not feasible. Meanwhile, Abo Khamis et al. [3] investigate the difference in the amortized update time for maintaining general queries under insertion-only versus arbitrary update sequences, an investigation that diverges from our focus on characterizing queries maintainable within which are different from our target for characterizing $O\left( 1\right)$ amortized update time.

其他更新模式下的CQ枚举。Kara等人[17]在独立研究中探讨了部分关系静态、部分关系任意更新的数据库CQ枚举问题，旨在刻画能以$O\left( 1\right)$摊销更新时间维护索引并支持$O\left( 1\right)$延迟枚举的CQ类。他们通过预处理时间分为线性、多项式或指数三种情况进行细化表征。本研究则探讨更通用的场景：各关系（甚至关系子集）可遵循仅插入、FIFO或任意等不同更新模式，但隐含要求预处理时间为线性复杂度。由于这些研究的基本框架差异，无法直接比较。同时，Abo Khamis等人[3]研究了仅插入序列与任意更新序列下维护通用查询的摊销更新时间差异，这与我们聚焦$O\left( 1\right)$摊销更新时间内的可维护查询表征目标不同。

### 1.3 Our Results

### 1.3 研究成果

Due to the inherent difficulty of maintaining non-free-connex CQs even over insertion-only sequences, we concentrate our attention in this paper to self-join-free free-connex CQs. Our lower bound results are derived under well-known conjectures, including the OMv and OuMv conjectures [15]. We discuss the optimality of our lower bounds, each of which is either an implication or an adaption of Lemma 1.4.

鉴于非自由连接CQ即使在仅插入序列上也难以维护，本文聚焦无自连接的自由连接CQ研究。我们的下界结果基于OMv和OuMv猜想[15]等著名猜想得出，并通过引理1.4的推论或改编来论证各下界的最优性。

Maintaining CQs over FIFO sequences. We first address the problem of maintaining CQs over FIFO sequences. In this context, we identify a class of queries, termed weak-q-hierarchical CQs, which lies strictly between free-connex queries and q-hierarchical queries (see Figure 1). Our first main result is a structural dichotomy that determines the hardness of maintaining CQs over FIFO sequences.

在FIFO序列上维护CQ问题研究。我们首先探讨在FIFO序列上维护CQ的问题。在此背景下，我们识别出一类介于自由连接查询(free-connex queries)和q层次查询(q-hierarchical queries)之间的查询类型（见图1），称为弱q层次CQ(weak-q-hierarchical CQs)。首个重要成果是揭示FIFO序列上CQ维护难度的结构二分性。

THEOREM 1.11. Afree-connex ${CQQ}$ can be maintained in $O\left( 1\right)$ time over FIFO sequences if $Q$ is weak-q-hierarchical and cannot be maintained in $O\left( {\sqrt{\left| D\right| }}^{1 - \epsilon }\right)$ time for any $\epsilon  > 0$ otherwise,assuming the OMv and OuMv conjectures.

定理1.11。假设OMv和OuMv猜想成立，当${CQQ}$是弱q层次查询时，自由连接${CQQ}$可在$O\left( 1\right)$时间内维护于FIFO序列上；否则，对于任意$\epsilon  > 0$都无法在$O\left( {\sqrt{\left| D\right| }}^{1 - \epsilon }\right)$时间内完成维护。

Maintaining CQs over mixed update sequences. We next consider mixed update sequences, in which a specific pattern - either insertion-only or FIFO - is exhibited by a subset of relations. To characterize the complexity of maintaining CQs under such mixed sequences, we introduce a procedural dichotomy formalized in the procedureMIxDETECT (see Algorithm 2). Notably, this result perfectly unifies all the previously known results for arbitrary and insertion-only update sequences together with our new findings for FIFO sequences.

混合更新序列上的CQ维护。接着我们研究由插入操作或FIFO模式构成的关系子集所展现的混合更新序列。为刻画此类混合序列下CQ维护的复杂度，我们提出了通过MIxDETECT算法（见算法2）形式化的过程二分法。值得注意的是，该结果完美统一了先前已知的任意更新序列、纯插入序列的研究成果，以及我们对FIFO序列的新发现。

THEOREM 1.12. For a free-connex ${CQQ} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E},\mathbf{y}}\right)$ and a pattern $\sigma$ of update sequences, $Q$ can be maintained in $O\left( 1\right)$ time over any $\sigma$ -sequence if MIxDETECT returns true,and cannot be maintained in $O\left( {\sqrt{\left| D\right| }}^{1 - \epsilon }\right)$ time for any $\epsilon  > 0$ otherwise,assuming the ${OMv}$ and ${OuMv}$ sconjectures.

定理1.12。对于自由连接${CQQ} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E},\mathbf{y}}\right)$和更新序列模式$\sigma$，若MIxDETECT返回真值，则$Q$可在$O\left( 1\right)$时间内维护于任何$\sigma$序列上；否则在假设${OMv}$和${OuMv}$猜想成立的前提下，对于任意$\epsilon  > 0$都无法在$O\left( {\sqrt{\left| D\right| }}^{1 - \epsilon }\right)$时间内完成维护。

### 1.4 Organization of This Paper

### 1.4 本文结构

This paper is organized as follows. In Section 2, we introduce the preliminary concepts essential for understanding both upper and lower bounds. In Section 3, we focus on maintaining CQs over FIFO sequences. We then extend our discussion to maintaining CQs over mixed sequences in Section 4. Finally, we conclude this paper and outline potential directions for future research in Section 5.

本文结构如下：第2节介绍理解上下界所需的预备知识；第3节重点讨论FIFO序列上的CQ维护；第4节将讨论延伸至混合序列上的CQ维护；最后第5节总结全文并展望未来研究方向。

## 2 PRELIMINARIES

## 2 预备知识

### 2.1 Classification of CQs

### 2.1 CQ分类体系

We introduce several important classes of CQs that are widely studied in the literature. We start with some commonly used terminology. In a CQ $\mathcal{Q} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E},\mathbf{y}}\right)$ ,let ${\mathcal{E}}_{x} = \{ e \in  \mathcal{E} : x \in  e\}$ be the set of relations containing attribute $x \in  \mathcal{V}$ . An attribute $x \in  \mathcal{V}$ is unique if it only appears in one relation,i.e., $\left| {\mathcal{E}}_{x}\right|  = 1$ . Let ${\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  }$ be the set of unique attributes in $\mathcal{Q}$ . For a subset ${\mathcal{E}}^{\prime } \subseteq  \mathcal{E}$ of relations, the ${\mathcal{E}}^{\prime }$ -induced CQ refers to $\mathcal{Q}\left\lbrack  {\mathcal{E}}^{\prime }\right\rbrack   \mathrel{\text{:=}} \left( {\mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{e \in  {\mathcal{E}}^{\prime }}}e,{\mathcal{E}}^{\prime },\mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{e \in  {\mathcal{E}}^{\prime }}}\left( {e \cap  \mathbf{y}}\right) }\right)$ .

我们介绍文献中广泛研究的几类重要CQ。首先定义常用术语：在CQ$\mathcal{Q} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E},\mathbf{y}}\right)$中，令${\mathcal{E}}_{x} = \{ e \in  \mathcal{E} : x \in  e\}$为包含属性$x \in  \mathcal{V}$的关系集合。若属性$x \in  \mathcal{V}$仅出现在单个关系中（即$\left| {\mathcal{E}}_{x}\right|  = 1$），则称其唯一。设${\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  }$表示$\mathcal{Q}$中的唯一属性集合。对于关系子集${\mathcal{E}}^{\prime } \subseteq  \mathcal{E}$，其${\mathcal{E}}^{\prime }$诱导CQ指代$\mathcal{Q}\left\lbrack  {\mathcal{E}}^{\prime }\right\rbrack   \mathrel{\text{:=}} \left( {\mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{e \in  {\mathcal{E}}^{\prime }}}e,{\mathcal{E}}^{\prime },\mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{e \in  {\mathcal{E}}^{\prime }}}\left( {e \cap  \mathbf{y}}\right) }\right)$。

Acyclic CQ [6, 12, 16]. There are some equivalent definitions of acyclic CQs, and we adopt one based on a generalized join tree as it naturally captures different classes of queries with the height of the tree. A generalized relation ${R}_{e}$ is defined on a subset $e \subseteq  \mathcal{V}$ of attributes such that there exists some input relation ${e}^{\prime } \in  \mathcal{E}$ with $e \subseteq  {e}^{\prime }$ ,and is distinguished from the input relations. A CQ $Q = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E},\mathbf{y}}\right)$ is acyclic if there exists a tree $\mathcal{T}$ in which each node corresponds to a distinct input relation in $\mathcal{E}$ or generalized relation while satisfying the following properties:

无环连接查询(CQ)[6,12,16]。关于无环CQ存在若干等价定义，我们采用基于广义连接树的定义，因其能通过树高自然刻画不同查询类别。广义关系${R}_{e}$定义在属性子集$e \subseteq  \mathcal{V}$上，要求存在某个输入关系${e}^{\prime } \in  \mathcal{E}$满足$e \subseteq  {e}^{\prime }$，且需与输入关系相区分。若存在树结构$\mathcal{T}$满足下列性质，则称CQ$Q = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E},\mathbf{y}}\right)$为无环查询：树中每个节点对应$\mathcal{E}$中的不同输入关系或广义关系。

- (cover property) each input relation corresponds to a node in $\mathcal{T}$ ; and each leaf node of $\mathcal{T}$ corresponds to an input relation;

- (覆盖性质)每个输入关系对应$\mathcal{T}$中一个节点；且$\mathcal{T}$的每个叶节点对应一个输入关系；

Figure 2. A free-connex join tree for ${Q}_{1}.\left\lbrack  \cdot \right\rbrack$ is a generalized relation.

图2. ${Q}_{1}.\left\lbrack  \cdot \right\rbrack$的自由连接树结构表示一个广义关系。

<img src="https://cdn.noedgeai.com/01962f99-7720-7ca8-a8dc-a597e361e6b4_5.jpg?x=1038&y=1702&w=345&h=168&r=0"/>

Figure 3. A height-1 free-connex join tree for ${Q}_{2}.\left\lbrack  \cdot \right\rbrack$ denotes a generalized relation.

图3. 高度为1的自由连接树中${Q}_{2}.\left\lbrack  \cdot \right\rbrack$表示广义关系。

<!-- Media -->

- (connect property) for each attribute $x \in  \mathcal{V}$ ,all nodes of $\mathcal{T}$ containing $x$ form a connected subtree of $\mathcal{T}$ ;

- (连通性质)对于每个属性$x \in  \mathcal{V}$，包含$x$的所有$\mathcal{T}$节点须构成$\mathcal{T}$的连通子树；

$\mathcal{T}$ is called a generalized join tree of $\mathcal{Q}$ . If all nodes in $\mathcal{T}$ correspond to input relations in $\mathcal{E},\mathcal{T}$ is called a traditional join tree.

$\mathcal{T}$称为$\mathcal{Q}$的广义连接树。若$\mathcal{T}$中所有节点对应$\mathcal{E},\mathcal{T}$的输入关系，则称为传统连接树。

Free-connex CQ [5,25]. An acyclic CQ $Q = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E},\mathbf{y}}\right)$ is free-connex if the derived CQ $\left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}\cup \{ \mathbf{y}\} ,\mathbf{y}}\right)$ is also acyclic. Equivalently,an acyclic $\mathrm{{CQ}}\mathcal{Q} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E},\mathbf{y}}\right)$ is free-connex if there exists a tree $\mathcal{T}$ in which each node corresponds to a distinct input relation or generalized relation while satisfying the following properties (in addition to the cover and connect property for acyclic CQs):

自由连接CQ[5,25]。若派生CQ$\left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}\cup \{ \mathbf{y}\} ,\mathbf{y}}\right)$也是无环查询，则称无环CQ$Q = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E},\mathbf{y}}\right)$为自由连接查询。等价表述为：存在树结构$\mathcal{T}$满足下列性质时(除需满足无环CQ的覆盖与连通性质外)，无环查询$\mathrm{{CQ}}\mathcal{Q} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E},\mathbf{y}}\right)$即为自由连接查询：树中每个节点对应不同输入关系或广义关系。

- (above property) no node corresponding to an input relation is an ancestor of a node corresponding to a generalized relation;

- (上层性质)对应输入关系的节点不得作为对应广义关系节点的祖先；

- (guard property) if node $e$ corresponds to a generalized relation, $e \subseteq  {e}^{\prime }$ holds for every child node ${e}^{\prime }$ of $e$ ;

- (守护性质)若节点$e$对应广义关系，则对其每个子节点${e}^{\prime }$都满足$e \subseteq  {e}^{\prime }$；

- (connex property) a subset ${\mathcal{E}}_{\text{con }}$ of nodes exist such that (i) ${\mathcal{E}}_{\text{con }}$ forms a connected subtree including the root of $\mathcal{T}$ ; (ii) for any $e \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{\text{con }}$ with its parent ${e}^{\prime },e \cap  {e}^{\prime } \subseteq  \mathbf{y}$ ; (iii) $\mathbf{y} \subseteq  \mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{e \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{\text{con }}}}e$ .

- (连通子集性质)存在节点子集${\mathcal{E}}_{\text{con }}$满足：(i)${\mathcal{E}}_{\text{con }}$构成包含$\mathcal{T}$根节点的连通子树；(ii)对任意$e \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{\text{con }}$及其父节点${e}^{\prime },e \cap  {e}^{\prime } \subseteq  \mathbf{y}$；(iii)$\mathbf{y} \subseteq  \mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{e \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{\text{con }}}}e$。

$\mathcal{T}$ is called a free-connex join tree of $\mathcal{Q}$ ,and ${\mathcal{T}}_{e}$ is the subtree of $\mathcal{T}$ rooted at $e$ . The height of $\mathcal{T}$ is the maximum number of relations on any leaf-to-root path, without counting generalized relations.

$\mathcal{T}$称为$\mathcal{Q}$的自由连接树，${\mathcal{T}}_{e}$是以$e$为根的$\mathcal{T}$子树。该树高度指任意叶节点到根路径上的最大关系数(不计广义关系)。

Example 2.1. Figure 2 shows a free-connex join tree for $\mathrm{{CQ}}{\mathcal{Q}}_{1} = {R}_{1}\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{2},{x}_{3}}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{2}\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{2}}\right)  \bowtie$ ${R}_{3}\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{4}}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{4}\left( {{x}_{4},{x}_{5}}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{5}\left( {{x}_{2},{x}_{3}}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{6}\left( {{x}_{2},{x}_{6}}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{7}\left( {{x}_{3},{x}_{7}}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{8}\left( {{x}_{3},{x}_{8}}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{9}\left( {{x}_{3},{x}_{9}}\right)$ . It has height as 3 with the path $\left\lbrack  {x}_{3}\right\rbrack   - \left\lbrack  {{x}_{2},{x}_{3}}\right\rbrack   - {R}_{1} - {R}_{3} - {R}_{4}$ .

示例2.1 图2展示了$\mathrm{{CQ}}{\mathcal{Q}}_{1} = {R}_{1}\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{2},{x}_{3}}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{2}\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{2}}\right)  \bowtie$${R}_{3}\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{4}}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{4}\left( {{x}_{4},{x}_{5}}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{5}\left( {{x}_{2},{x}_{3}}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{6}\left( {{x}_{2},{x}_{6}}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{7}\left( {{x}_{3},{x}_{7}}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{8}\left( {{x}_{3},{x}_{8}}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{9}\left( {{x}_{3},{x}_{9}}\right)$的自由连接树（free-connex join tree），其高度为3，路径为$\left\lbrack  {x}_{3}\right\rbrack   - \left\lbrack  {{x}_{2},{x}_{3}}\right\rbrack   - {R}_{1} - {R}_{3} - {R}_{4}$。

q-hierarchical CQ [7]. A CQ $\mathcal{Q} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E},\mathbf{y}}\right)$ is q-hierarchical if for any pair of attributes ${x}_{1},{x}_{2} \in  \mathcal{V}$ , either ${\mathcal{E}}_{{x}_{1}} \subseteq  {\mathcal{E}}_{{x}_{2}}$ or ${\mathcal{E}}_{{x}_{2}} \subseteq  {\mathcal{E}}_{{x}_{1}}$ or ${\mathcal{E}}_{{x}_{1}} \cap  {\mathcal{E}}_{{x}_{2}} = \varnothing$ ; and if ${x}_{1} \in  \mathbf{y}$ and ${\mathcal{E}}_{{x}_{1}} \subsetneq  {\mathcal{E}}_{{x}_{2}}$ ,then ${x}_{2} \in  \mathbf{y}$ . We mention two important structural properties of q-hierarchical CQs:

q-层次化CQ[7]。若对任意属性对${x}_{1},{x}_{2} \in  \mathcal{V}$满足${\mathcal{E}}_{{x}_{1}} \subseteq  {\mathcal{E}}_{{x}_{2}}$或${\mathcal{E}}_{{x}_{2}} \subseteq  {\mathcal{E}}_{{x}_{1}}$或${\mathcal{E}}_{{x}_{1}} \cap  {\mathcal{E}}_{{x}_{2}} = \varnothing$，且当${x}_{1} \in  \mathbf{y}$与${\mathcal{E}}_{{x}_{1}} \subsetneq  {\mathcal{E}}_{{x}_{2}}$时必有${x}_{2} \in  \mathbf{y}$，则CQ$\mathcal{Q} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E},\mathbf{y}}\right)$称为q-层次化CQ。我们指出q-层次化CQ的两个重要结构特性：

LEMMA 2.2 ([25]). A CQ is q-hierarchical if and only if it has a height-1 free-connex join tree.

引理2.2([25])。当且仅当存在高度为1的自由连接树时，CQ具有q-层次性。

LEMMA 2.3. In a $q$ -hierarchical ${CQQ} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E},\mathbf{y}}\right)$ with ${\mathcal{E}}^{\prime } \subseteq  \mathcal{E},Q\left\lbrack  {\mathcal{E}}^{\prime }\right\rbrack$ is also $q$ -hierarchical.

引理2.3 在$q$层次化的${CQQ} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E},\mathbf{y}}\right)$中，若${\mathcal{E}}^{\prime } \subseteq  \mathcal{E},Q\left\lbrack  {\mathcal{E}}^{\prime }\right\rbrack$则其同样具有$q$层次性。

Proof of Lemma 2.3. Let $\mathcal{T}$ be a height-1 free-connex join tree of the q-hierarchical CQ $\mathcal{Q}$ . For any subset ${\mathcal{E}}^{\prime } \subseteq  \mathcal{E}$ of relations,we remove all leaf nodes corresponding to relations in $\mathcal{E} - {\mathcal{E}}^{\prime }$ . We also recursively remove any generalized relation that is a leaf node. The resulting tree is a height-1 free-connex join tree for the ${\mathcal{E}}^{\prime }$ -induced CQ. Hence,the ${\mathcal{E}}^{\prime }$ -induced CQ is q-hierarchical.

引理2.3证明。设$\mathcal{T}$是q-层次化CQ$\mathcal{Q}$的高度1自由连接树。对于关系子集${\mathcal{E}}^{\prime } \subseteq  \mathcal{E}$，我们移除$\mathcal{E} - {\mathcal{E}}^{\prime }$对应的所有叶节点，并递归移除所有作为叶节点的广义关系。所得树即为${\mathcal{E}}^{\prime }$诱导CQ的高度1自由连接树，故该诱导CQ具有q-层次性。

Example 2.4. Consider a q-hierarchical CQ ${\mathcal{Q}}_{2} \mathrel{\text{:=}} {\pi }_{{x}_{2},{x}_{3}}{R}_{1}\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{2},{x}_{3}}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{2}\left( {{x}_{2},{x}_{3},{x}_{4}}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{3}\left( {{x}_{3},{x}_{5}}\right)$ with its height-1 free-connex join tree with ${\mathcal{E}}_{\text{con }} = \left\{  {\left\lbrack  {x}_{3}\right\rbrack  ,\left\lbrack  {{x}_{2},{x}_{3}}\right\rbrack  }\right\}$ in Figure 3.

例2.4. 考虑一个q-层次结构查询${\mathcal{Q}}_{2} \mathrel{\text{:=}} {\pi }_{{x}_{2},{x}_{3}}{R}_{1}\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{2},{x}_{3}}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{2}\left( {{x}_{2},{x}_{3},{x}_{4}}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{3}\left( {{x}_{3},{x}_{5}}\right)$，其高度为1的自由连接树如图3所示${\mathcal{E}}_{\text{con }} = \left\{  {\left\lbrack  {x}_{3}\right\rbrack  ,\left\lbrack  {{x}_{2},{x}_{3}}\right\rbrack  }\right\}$。

### 2.2 Upper Bounds

### 2.2 上界分析

Due to the high time and space costs of the classic change propagation framework [11] for maintaining queries, Wang et al. [25] proposed an improved approach that eliminates the join operator in change propagation [11]. Given a free-connex CQ, the method first organizes each input relation at the leaf level, connecting them via relational operators at all internal nodes. Rather than maintaining subqueries directly,it decomposes each subquery into a semi-join view $\left( {V}_{s}\right)$ and a projection view $\left( {V}_{p}\right)$ of linear size. Intuitively,the semi-join view of a relation $R$ contains all tuples in $R$ that can successfully join with tuples in its descendant nodes, while the projection views facilitate the efficient maintenance of these semi-join views. These two views are defined recursively over the join tree. Example 2.5 is used to illustrate these concepts, and additional details can be found in [25].

由于经典变更传播框架[11]维护查询的高时间和空间成本，Wang等人[25]提出了一种改进方法，消除了变更传播[11]中的连接操作。对于自由连接查询，该方法首先在叶节点组织每个输入关系，通过内部节点的关系运算符进行连接。该方法并非直接维护子查询，而是将每个子查询分解为线性大小的半连接视图$\left( {V}_{s}\right)$和投影视图$\left( {V}_{p}\right)$。直观而言，关系$R$的半连接视图包含该关系中能与后代节点元组成功连接的所有元组，而投影视图则用于高效维护这些半连接视图。这两种视图通过连接树递归定义。例2.5用于说明这些概念，更多细节可参阅[25]。

Example 2.5. Consider a non-q-hierarchical CQ ${Q}_{\text{hier }} = {\pi }_{\varnothing }{R}_{1}\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{2}}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{2}\left( {x}_{1}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{3}\left( {x}_{2}\right)$ . Figure 4 illustrates the query plan designed for ${Q}_{\text{hier }}\left\lbrack  {25}\right\rbrack$ . In this query plan,each node $e$ is associated with a semi-join view ${V}_{s}\left( {R}_{e}\right)$ that stores those tuples in ${R}_{e}$ that can be successfully joined with all relations in the subtree rooted at $e$ . For instance,we define the semi-join views as follows: ${V}_{s}\left( {R}_{1}\right)  \mathrel{\text{:=}} {R}_{1} \ltimes  {R}_{2},{V}_{s}\left( {R}_{2}\right)  \mathrel{\text{:=}} {R}_{2}$ and ${V}_{s}\left( {R}_{3}\right)  \mathrel{\text{:=}}$ ${R}_{3} \ltimes  \left( {{R}_{1} \ltimes  {R}_{2}}\right)$ . Each node $e$ is further associated with a projection view ${V}_{p}\left( {R}_{e}\right)$ that stores the projection of the corresponding semi-join view onto the join attributes between $e$ and its parent node. For instance,we define the projection-views as follows: ${V}_{p}\left( {R}_{1}\right)  \mathrel{\text{:=}} {\pi }_{{x}_{2}}{V}_{s}\left( {R}_{1}\right)$ , ${V}_{p}\left( {R}_{2}\right)  \mathrel{\text{:=}} {\pi }_{{x}_{1}}{V}_{s}\left( {R}_{2}\right)$ and ${V}_{s}\left( {R}_{3}\right)  \mathrel{\text{:=}} {\pi }_{\varnothing }{V}_{p}\left( {R}_{3}\right)$ . It is noteworthy that the series of semi-joins within the subtree rooted at any node $e$ can be compactly represented by the semi-joins between the node $e$ and the projection views of its child nodes. Consequently,the simplified expressions for the semi-join views become: ${V}_{s}\left( {R}_{1}\right)  \mathrel{\text{:=}} {R}_{1} \ltimes  {V}_{p}\left( {R}_{2}\right)$ and ${V}_{s}\left( {R}_{3}\right)  \mathrel{\text{:=}} {R}_{3} \ltimes  {V}_{p}\left( {R}_{1}\right)$ .

示例2.5. 考虑一个非q-层次化CQ（Conjunctive Query）${Q}_{\text{hier }} = {\pi }_{\varnothing }{R}_{1}\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{2}}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{2}\left( {x}_{1}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{3}\left( {x}_{2}\right)$。图4展示了为${Q}_{\text{hier }}\left\lbrack  {25}\right\rbrack$设计的查询计划。在该查询计划中，每个节点$e$关联着一个半连接视图${V}_{s}\left( {R}_{e}\right)$，该视图存储了${R}_{e}$中能与$e$为根的子树内所有关系成功连接的元组。例如，我们如下定义半连接视图：${V}_{s}\left( {R}_{1}\right)  \mathrel{\text{:=}} {R}_{1} \ltimes  {R}_{2},{V}_{s}\left( {R}_{2}\right)  \mathrel{\text{:=}} {R}_{2}$和${V}_{s}\left( {R}_{3}\right)  \mathrel{\text{:=}}$${R}_{3} \ltimes  \left( {{R}_{1} \ltimes  {R}_{2}}\right)$。每个节点$e$还关联着一个投影视图${V}_{p}\left( {R}_{e}\right)$，该视图存储对应半连接视图在$e$与其父节点间连接属性上的投影。例如，我们如下定义投影视图：${V}_{p}\left( {R}_{1}\right)  \mathrel{\text{:=}} {\pi }_{{x}_{2}}{V}_{s}\left( {R}_{1}\right)$、${V}_{p}\left( {R}_{2}\right)  \mathrel{\text{:=}} {\pi }_{{x}_{1}}{V}_{s}\left( {R}_{2}\right)$及${V}_{s}\left( {R}_{3}\right)  \mathrel{\text{:=}} {\pi }_{\varnothing }{V}_{p}\left( {R}_{3}\right)$。值得注意的是，以任意节点$e$为根的子树内的一系列半连接，可简化为该节点$e$与其子节点投影视图间的半连接。因此，半连接视图的简化表达式为：${V}_{s}\left( {R}_{1}\right)  \mathrel{\text{:=}} {R}_{1} \ltimes  {V}_{p}\left( {R}_{2}\right)$和${V}_{s}\left( {R}_{3}\right)  \mathrel{\text{:=}} {R}_{3} \ltimes  {V}_{p}\left( {R}_{1}\right)$。

Figure 4. Maintaining ${Q}_{\text{hier }}$ by the framework proposed in [25]. Input relations are in black, semi-join views are in blue, and projection views are in red.

图4. 通过文献[25]提出的框架维护${Q}_{\text{hier }}$。输入关系以黑色显示，半连接视图为蓝色，投影视图为红色。

<!-- Media -->

This method guarantees both linear space usage and maintenance time in the worst case; however, the worst-case complexity may not manifest for every update sequence. To provide a more nuanced cost analysis, [25] introduced the notion of enclosureness for update sequences to quantify the amortized update time required. Intuitively,the "status" of a tuple $t$ ,i.e.,whether $t$ is present in ${V}_{s}\left( {R}_{e}\right)$ ,can change multiple times during its lifespan as a result of insertions or deletions of tuples in descendant nodes. Notably,successive insertions (when $t$ is present in ${V}_{s}\left( {R}_{e}\right)$ ) or successive deletions (when $t$ is absent from ${V}_{s}\left( {R}_{e}\right)$ ) in descendant nodes do not further change its status. For example,if an insertion of a tuple ${t}^{\prime }$ in the subtree rooted at $e$ triggers a change in the status of $t$ ,then $t$ will remain in the semi-join view ${V}_{s}\left( {R}_{e}\right)$ until the first subsequent deletion in the subtree occurs. Consequently, the concept of a tuple's minimal lifespan is introduced to capture the minimum temporal interval over which a tuple can effect a change in the status of another tuple.

该方法在最坏情况下既能保证线性空间使用率，又能维持处理时间；然而并非所有更新序列都会出现最坏复杂度。为提供更精细的成本分析，文献[25]引入了更新序列的"包围度"概念来量化所需的摊还更新时间。直观而言，元组$t$的"状态"（即是否存在于${V}_{s}\left( {R}_{e}\right)$中）在其生命周期内可能因后代节点中元组的插入或删除而多次改变。值得注意的是，当$t$存在于${V}_{s}\left( {R}_{e}\right)$时，后代节点的连续插入；或当其不存在时，连续删除都不会再改变其状态。例如，若在以$e$为根的子树中插入元组${t}^{\prime }$导致$t$状态改变，则该元组将保持在半连接视图${V}_{s}\left( {R}_{e}\right)$中，直至子树中首次发生删除操作。因此引入元组最小生命周期的概念，用以捕捉一个元组能够影响另一个元组状态变化的最小时间区间。

Definition 2.6 (Minimal lifespan). Given a free-connex query $Q$ ,a free-connex $\mathcal{T}$ of $Q$ ,an update sequence $S$ , and a tuple ${t}_{1}$ in an input relation ${R}_{e}$ ,the two minimal lifespans of ${t}_{1}$ with respect to its lifespan $I\left( {t}_{1}\right)  = \left\lbrack  {{t}_{1}^{ + },{t}_{1}^{ - }}\right\rbrack$ are defined as:

定义2.6（最小生命周期）。给定自由连接查询$Q$、其自由连接树$\mathcal{T}$、更新序列$S$及输入关系${R}_{e}$中的元组${t}_{1}$，该元组相对于其生命周期$I\left( {t}_{1}\right)  = \left\lbrack  {{t}_{1}^{ + },{t}_{1}^{ - }}\right\rbrack$的两个最小生命周期定义为：

$$
\widehat{I}\left( {t}_{1}\right)  = \left\lbrack  {{t}_{1}^{ + },\min \left( {{t}_{1}^{ - },\mathop{\min }\limits_{{{t}_{2} \in  {R}_{{e}^{\prime }} : {e}^{\prime } \in  {\mathcal{T}}_{e}-\{ e\} ,{t}_{2}^{ - } > {t}_{1}^{ + }}}{t}_{2}^{ - }}\right) }\right\rbrack  ;
$$

$$
\breve{I}\left( {t}_{1}\right)  = \left\lbrack  {\max \left( {{t}_{1}^{ + },\mathop{\max }\limits_{{{t}_{2} \in  {R}_{{e}^{\prime }} : {e}^{\prime } \in  {\mathcal{T}}_{e}-\{ e\} ,{t}_{2}^{ + } < {t}_{1}^{ - }}}{t}_{2}^{ + }}\right) ,{t}_{1}^{ - }}\right\rbrack  
$$

Figure 5. An update sequence for ${Q}_{\text{hier }}$ . Red intervals are lifespans of tuples in ${R}_{1}$ . Blue intervals are lifespans of tuples in ${R}_{2}$ . Purple intervals are lifespans of tuples in ${R}_{3}$ . Green intervals are minimal lifespans for tuples in ${R}_{1}$ and ${R}_{2}$ . Black intervals are the insertions and deletions of $\left( {b}_{1}\right)  \in  {V}_{s}\left( {R}_{3}\right)$ .

图5. ${Q}_{\text{hier }}$的更新序列。红色区间表示${R}_{1}$中元组的生命周期，蓝色区间为${R}_{2}$中的元组生命周期，紫色区间是${R}_{3}$中的元组生命周期，绿色区间表示${R}_{1}$和${R}_{2}$中元组的最小生命周期，黑色区间表示$\left( {b}_{1}\right)  \in  {V}_{s}\left( {R}_{3}\right)$的插入与删除操作。

<!-- Media -->

Definition 2.7 (Enclosureness). Given a free-connex CQ $Q$ ,a free-connex join tree $\mathcal{T}$ of $Q$ ,and an update sequence $S$ ,for a node $e \in  \mathcal{T}$ and a tuple $t \in  {R}_{e}$ ,its enclosureness is

定义2.7（包围度）。给定自由连接查询$Q$、其自由连接连接树$\mathcal{T}$及更新序列$S$，对于节点$e \in  \mathcal{T}$和元组$t \in  {R}_{e}$，其包围度为

${\lambda }_{\mathcal{T}}\left( t\right)  = \mathop{\max }\limits_{{\text{For all }{t}^{\prime } \in  \mathcal{J}\text{,there exists }{e}^{\prime } \in  {\mathcal{T}}_{e}-\{ e\} ,{t}^{\prime } \in  {R}_{{e}^{\prime }}}}\left| \mathcal{J}\right|$

For all ${t}_{1} \in  \mathcal{J},I\left( {t}_{1}\right)  \subseteq  I\left( t\right)$

对于所有${t}_{1} \in  \mathcal{J},I\left( {t}_{1}\right)  \subseteq  I\left( t\right)$

$$
\text{For all}{t}_{2},{t}_{3} \in  \mathcal{J},\dot{I}\left( {t}_{2}\right)  \cap  \dot{I}\left( {t}_{3}\right)  = \varnothing 
$$

where each $\dot{I}$ is either $\widehat{I}$ or $\breve{I}$ ,i.e.,the largest number of disjoint minimal lifespans of tuples in the descendants of $e$ ,which are contained in the lifespan of $t$ . Then,the enclosureness of the update sequence is still average: ${\lambda }_{\mathcal{T}}\left( S\right)  \mathrel{\text{:=}} \max \left( {\frac{\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{t \in  S}}{\lambda }_{\mathcal{T}}\left( t\right) }{\left| S\right| },1}\right) .$

其中每个$\dot{I}$要么是$\widehat{I}$要么是$\breve{I}$，即包含在$t$生命周期内的、$e$后代节点中元组不相交最小生命周期的最大数量。此时更新序列的包围度仍取平均值：${\lambda }_{\mathcal{T}}\left( S\right)  \mathrel{\text{:=}} \max \left( {\frac{\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{t \in  S}}{\lambda }_{\mathcal{T}}\left( t\right) }{\left| S\right| },1}\right) .$

This concept enables a more refined analysis of update sequences that exhibit varying patterns. For instance, in an insertion-only sequence, the enclosureness is 1 on a free-connex join tree. Consequently,any free-connex conjunctive query can be maintained in $O\left( 1\right)$ time over insertion-only update sequences.

该概念支持对呈现不同模式的更新序列进行更精准分析。例如在纯插入序列中，自由连接树上的包围度为1。因此任何自由连接查询在纯插入更新序列上都可在$O\left( 1\right)$时间内完成维护。

Example 2.8. Subsequently,tuple $\left( {b}_{1}\right)$ is reinserted into ${V}_{s}\left( {R}_{3}\right)$ at timestamp 10 and removed at timestamp 12. During this phase,the tuple $\left( {{a}_{3},{b}_{1}}\right)  \in  {R}_{1}$ has a minimal

## 3 MAINTAIN CQS OVER FIFO SEQUENCES

## 3 在FIFO序列上维护连续查询

### 3.1 Weak-q-hierarchical CQs

### 3.1 弱q层次化连续查询

We begin by introducing two key concepts for CQs: reducible relations and skeleton. These notions play a crucial role in characterizing the class of weak-q-hierarchical CQs. In particular, by understanding how relations within a query can be reduced relative to one another and how the remaining non-reducible relations form a skeleton, we can gain further insight into the properties and efficient maintenance of these queries.

我们首先介绍CQ（可计算查询）的两个核心概念：可约化关系与骨架结构。这些概念在界定弱q层次化CQ类别时具有关键作用。具体而言，通过理解查询中关系如何相互约简，以及剩余不可约化关系如何形成骨架，我们能更深入地把握这类查询的特性与高效维护方法。

Definition 3.1 (Reducible Relation). In a free-connex CQ $Q = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E},\mathbf{y}}\right)$ ,a relation $e \in  \mathcal{E}$ is reducible if there exists another relation ${e}^{\prime } \in  \mathcal{E}$ such that (i) $e - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } \subseteq  {e}^{\prime }$ and $e \cap  {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } \subseteq  \overline{\mathrm{y}}$ ; or (ii) $e - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } \subseteq  {e}^{\prime } \cap  \mathbf{y}$ . In this context, ${e}^{\prime }$ is called an anchor of $e$ .

定义3.1（可约化关系）。在自由连接CQ $Q = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E},\mathbf{y}}\right)$ 中，若存在另一关系$e \in  \mathcal{E}$满足：(i)$e - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } \subseteq  {e}^{\prime }$且$e \cap  {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } \subseteq  \overline{\mathrm{y}}$；或(ii)$e - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } \subseteq  {e}^{\prime } \cap  \mathbf{y}$，则称关系${e}^{\prime } \in  \mathcal{E}$为可约化的。此时，${e}^{\prime }$被称为$e$的锚点。

It is worth noting that one relation could have multiple anchors, and one relation could serve as an anchor for multiple other relations. Moreover,the anchor ordering is transitive. Specifically,if ${e}^{\prime }$ is an anchor of $e$ ,and ${e}^{\prime \prime }$ is an anchor of ${e}^{\prime }$ ,then ${e}^{\prime \prime }$ is an anchor of $e$ .

需注意，单个关系可能拥有多个锚点，而单个关系也可作为多个其他关系的锚点。此外，锚点排序具有传递性：若${e}^{\prime }$是$e$的锚点，且${e}^{\prime \prime }$是${e}^{\prime }$的锚点，则${e}^{\prime \prime }$也是$e$的锚点。

Lemma 3.2. If ${e}_{1}$ is reducible by ${e}_{2}$ ,and ${e}_{2}$ is reducible by ${e}_{3}$ ,then ${e}_{1}$ is reducible by ${e}_{3}$ .

引理3.2。若${e}_{1}$可通过${e}_{2}$约化，且${e}_{2}$可通过${e}_{3}$约化，则${e}_{1}$可通过${e}_{3}$约化。

Proof. We distinguish the following four cases. Case 1: ${e}_{1} - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } \subseteq  {e}_{2},{e}_{1} \cap  {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } \subseteq  \overline{\mathbf{y}}$ ,and ${e}_{2} - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } \subseteq  {e}_{3},{e}_{2} \cap  {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } \subseteq  \overline{\mathrm{y}}$ : from ${e}_{1} - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } \subseteq  {e}_{2},{e}_{2} - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } \subseteq  {e}_{3}$ ,and ${e}_{1} \cap  \left( {{e}_{2} \cap  {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  }}\right)  = \varnothing$ ,we have ${e}_{1} - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } \subseteq  {e}_{2} - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } \subseteq  {e}_{3}$ ,therefore ${e}_{1}$ and ${e}_{3}$ satisfies the condition (i) in Definition 3.1. Case 2: ${e}_{1} - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } \subseteq  {e}_{2},{e}_{1} \cap  {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } \subseteq  \overline{\mathbf{y}}$ ,and ${e}_{2} - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } \subseteq  {e}_{3} \cap  \mathbf{y}$ : similarly,from ${e}_{1} - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } \subseteq  {e}_{2}$ and ${e}_{2} - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } \subseteq  {e}_{3} \cap  \mathbf{y}$ , we can get ${e}_{1} - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } \subseteq  {e}_{2} - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } \subseteq  {e}_{3} \cap  \mathrm{y}$ ,therefore ${e}_{1}$ and ${e}_{3}$ satisfies the condition (ii) in Definition 3.1. Case 3: ${e}_{1} - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } \subseteq  {e}_{2} \cap  \mathrm{y}$ ,and ${e}_{2} - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } \subseteq  {e}_{3},{e}_{2} \cap  {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } \subseteq  \overline{\mathrm{y}}$ : since ${e}_{2} \cap  {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } \subseteq  \overline{\mathrm{y}},{e}_{2} \cap  \mathrm{y} \subseteq  {e}_{2} - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  }$ . Therefore, ${e}_{1} - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } \subseteq  {e}_{2} \cap  \mathbf{y} \subseteq  {e}_{2} - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } \subseteq  {e}_{3}$ ,which leads to ${e}_{1} - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } \subseteq  {e}_{3} \cap  \mathbf{y}$ that satisfies the condition (ii) in Definition 3.1. Case $4 : {e}_{1} - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } \subseteq  {e}_{2} \cap  \mathbf{y}$ ,and ${e}_{2} - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } \subseteq  {e}_{3} \cap  \mathbf{y}$ : similarly, ${e}_{1} - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } \subseteq  {e}_{2} \cap  \mathbf{y} \subseteq  {e}_{2} - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } \subseteq  {e}_{3} \cap  \mathbf{y}$ ,making ${e}_{1}$ and ${e}_{3}$ satisfies condition (ii) in Definition 3.1.

证明。我们区分以下四种情况。情况1：${e}_{1} - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } \subseteq  {e}_{2},{e}_{1} \cap  {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } \subseteq  \overline{\mathbf{y}}$且${e}_{2} - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } \subseteq  {e}_{3},{e}_{2} \cap  {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } \subseteq  \overline{\mathrm{y}}$：由${e}_{1} - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } \subseteq  {e}_{2},{e}_{2} - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } \subseteq  {e}_{3}$和${e}_{1} \cap  \left( {{e}_{2} \cap  {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  }}\right)  = \varnothing$可得${e}_{1} - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } \subseteq  {e}_{2} - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } \subseteq  {e}_{3}$，因此${e}_{1}$与${e}_{3}$满足定义3.1中的条件(i)。情况2：${e}_{1} - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } \subseteq  {e}_{2},{e}_{1} \cap  {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } \subseteq  \overline{\mathbf{y}}$且${e}_{2} - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } \subseteq  {e}_{3} \cap  \mathbf{y}$：类似地，根据${e}_{1} - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } \subseteq  {e}_{2}$和${e}_{2} - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } \subseteq  {e}_{3} \cap  \mathbf{y}$可推出${e}_{1} - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } \subseteq  {e}_{2} - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } \subseteq  {e}_{3} \cap  \mathrm{y}$，故${e}_{1}$与${e}_{3}$满足定义3.1中的条件(ii)。情况3：${e}_{1} - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } \subseteq  {e}_{2} \cap  \mathrm{y}$且${e}_{2} - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } \subseteq  {e}_{3},{e}_{2} \cap  {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } \subseteq  \overline{\mathrm{y}}$：由于${e}_{2} \cap  {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } \subseteq  \overline{\mathrm{y}},{e}_{2} \cap  \mathrm{y} \subseteq  {e}_{2} - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  }$，因此${e}_{1} - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } \subseteq  {e}_{2} \cap  \mathbf{y} \subseteq  {e}_{2} - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } \subseteq  {e}_{3}$，这使得${e}_{1} - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } \subseteq  {e}_{3} \cap  \mathbf{y}$满足定义3.1中的条件(ii)。情况$4 : {e}_{1} - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } \subseteq  {e}_{2} \cap  \mathbf{y}$且${e}_{2} - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } \subseteq  {e}_{3} \cap  \mathbf{y}$：同理可得${e}_{1} - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } \subseteq  {e}_{2} \cap  \mathbf{y} \subseteq  {e}_{2} - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } \subseteq  {e}_{3} \cap  \mathbf{y}$，故${e}_{1}$与${e}_{3}$满足定义3.1中的条件(ii)。

We next introduce the TRIM procedure, as described in Algorithm 1, which takes as input a CQ $Q = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E},\mathbf{y}}\right)$ and returns a subset of relations,referred to as the skeleton of $Q$ . This procedure iteratively identifies a reducible relation and its anchor in the remaining relations (if possible), and removes this reducible relation. When no more relations can be removed, the remaining subset of relations is returned. The skeleton returned (denoted as ${\mathcal{E}}_{ * }$ ) may not be unique. Below,we use ${\mathcal{Q}}_{ * }$ to denote the CQ induced by ${\mathcal{E}}_{ \downarrow  }$ .

接下来我们介绍TRIM过程（如算法1所述），该过程以CQ $Q = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E},\mathbf{y}}\right)$作为输入，返回一个关系子集（称为$Q$的骨架）。该过程迭代地识别可约关系及其在剩余关系中的锚点（若存在），并移除该可约关系。当无法继续移除关系时，返回剩余的关系子集。返回的骨架（记为${\mathcal{E}}_{ * }$）可能不唯一。下文使用${\mathcal{Q}}_{ * }$表示由${\mathcal{E}}_{ \downarrow  }$导出的CQ。

Example 3.3. Consider a CQ ${\mathcal{Q}}_{5} = {R}_{1}\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{2}}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{2}\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{2},{x}_{3}}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{3}\left( {{x}_{2},{x}_{3},{x}_{4}}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{4}\left( {{x}_{2},{x}_{3},{x}_{5}}\right)  \bowtie$ ${R}_{5}\left( {{x}_{4},{x}_{6}}\right)$ . We have ${\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } = \left\{  {{x}_{5},{x}_{6}}\right\}$ . A skeleton is $\left\{  {{e}_{2},{e}_{3}}\right\}$ ,since ${e}_{1} \subsetneq  {e}_{2},{e}_{4} - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } \subsetneq  {e}_{2}$ ,and ${e}_{5} - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } \subsetneq  {e}_{3}$ . On the other hand,consider another CQ ${\mathcal{Q}}_{6} = {R}_{1}\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{2}}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{2}\left( {{x}_{2},{x}_{3}}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{3}\left( {{x}_{2},{x}_{4}}\right)$ . We have ${\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } = \left\{  {{x}_{1},{x}_{3},{x}_{4}}\right\}$ . One possible skeleton is $\left\{  {{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}\right\}$ and the other possible skeleton is $\left\{  {{e}_{1},{e}_{3}}\right\}$ .

示例3.3 考虑一个CQ ${\mathcal{Q}}_{5} = {R}_{1}\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{2}}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{2}\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{2},{x}_{3}}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{3}\left( {{x}_{2},{x}_{3},{x}_{4}}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{4}\left( {{x}_{2},{x}_{3},{x}_{5}}\right)  \bowtie$${R}_{5}\left( {{x}_{4},{x}_{6}}\right)$。我们有${\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } = \left\{  {{x}_{5},{x}_{6}}\right\}$。其中一个骨架是$\left\{  {{e}_{2},{e}_{3}}\right\}$，因为${e}_{1} \subsetneq  {e}_{2},{e}_{4} - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } \subsetneq  {e}_{2}$且${e}_{5} - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } \subsetneq  {e}_{3}$。另一方面，考虑另一个CQ ${\mathcal{Q}}_{6} = {R}_{1}\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{2}}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{2}\left( {{x}_{2},{x}_{3}}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{3}\left( {{x}_{2},{x}_{4}}\right)$。我们有${\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } = \left\{  {{x}_{1},{x}_{3},{x}_{4}}\right\}$。一个可能的骨架是$\left\{  {{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}\right\}$，另一个可能的骨架是$\left\{  {{e}_{1},{e}_{3}}\right\}$。

Definition 3.4 (weak-q-hierarchical). A free-connex CQ $Q$ is weak-q-hierarchical if it has a skeleton that induces a q-hierarchical CQ.

定义3.4（弱q分层性）。若自由连接CQ $Q$存在能导出q分层CQ的骨架，则称其具有弱q分层性。

Example 3.5. For a weak-q-hierarchical CQ ${\mathcal{Q}}_{3} = {\pi }_{{x}_{2}}{R}_{1}\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{2}}\right)  \bowtie$ ${R}_{2}\left( {{x}_{2},{x}_{4}}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{3}\left( {{x}_{2},{x}_{5}}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{4}\left( {x}_{1}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{5}\left( {{x}_{3},{x}_{6}}\right)$ ,Algorithm 1 removes ${R}_{4}$ , which is reducible with ${R}_{1}$ as its anchor and ${R}_{5}$ ,which is reducible with ${R}_{2}$ as its anchor. Remaining relations ${R}_{1},{R}_{2},{R}_{3}$ ,form the skeleton and induce a q-hierarchical CQ ${\pi }_{{x}_{2}}{R}_{1}\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{2}}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{2}\left( {{x}_{2},{x}_{4}}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{3}\left( {{x}_{2},{x}_{5}}\right)$ .

示例3.5. 对于弱q分层CQ查询${\mathcal{Q}}_{3} = {\pi }_{{x}_{2}}{R}_{1}\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{2}}\right)  \bowtie$${R}_{2}\left( {{x}_{2},{x}_{4}}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{3}\left( {{x}_{2},{x}_{5}}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{4}\left( {x}_{1}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{5}\left( {{x}_{3},{x}_{6}}\right)$，算法1移除了${R}_{4}$（该关系以${R}_{1}$为锚点可约简）和${R}_{5}$（该关系以${R}_{2}$为锚点可约简）。剩余关系${R}_{1},{R}_{2},{R}_{3}$构成骨架，并导出一个q分层CQ查询${\pi }_{{x}_{2}}{R}_{1}\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{2}}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{2}\left( {{x}_{2},{x}_{4}}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{3}\left( {{x}_{2},{x}_{5}}\right)$。

### 3.2 Maintaining Weak-q-hierarchical CQs

### 3.2 弱q分层CQ查询的维护

Next,we demonstrate that weak- $q$ -hierarchical CQs can be efficiently maintained over FIFO update sequences. Before introducing the algorithm, we establish two key structural properties of weak-q-hierarchical CQs with respect to their free-connex join trees.

接下来我们证明，弱$q$分层CQ查询可以在FIFO更新序列上高效维护。在介绍算法前，我们首先建立弱q分层CQ查询关于其自由连接树的两个关键结构特性。

LEMMA 3.6. For a free-connex ${CQQ} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E},\mathbf{y}}\right)$ with its free-connex join tree $\mathcal{T}$ ,for any leaf node $e$ with its parent node ${e}^{\prime },e$ is reducible,with ${e}^{\prime }$ serving as its anchor.

引理3.6. 对于具有自由连接树$\mathcal{T}$的自由连接查询${CQQ} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E},\mathbf{y}}\right)$，任何父节点为${e}^{\prime },e$的叶节点$e$都是可约简的，且以${e}^{\prime }$作为其锚点。

Proof of Lemma 3.6. The connect property implies $e - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } = e \cap  {e}^{\prime }$ . If $e \cap  {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } = \varnothing$ ,condition (i) follows. Below,assume $e \cap  {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } \neq  \varnothing$ . If $\left( {e \cap  {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  }}\right)  \cap  \mathbf{y} = \varnothing$ ,condition (i) follows. If $\left( {e \cap  {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  }}\right)  \cap  \mathbf{y} \neq  \varnothing$ , we must have $e \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{\text{con }}$ ; otherwise,no relation in ${\mathcal{E}}_{\text{con }}$ contains the output attribute(s) in $\left( {e \cap  {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  }}\right)  \cap  \mathbf{y}$ . As $e \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{\text{con }}$ ,we must have ${e}^{\prime } \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{\text{con }}$ ; otherwise, ${\mathcal{E}}_{\text{con }}$ does not form a connected subtree including the root of $\mathcal{T}$ . Implied by the property of ${\mathcal{E}}_{\text{con }},e \cap  {e}^{\prime } \subseteq  \mathbf{y}$ ,and condition (ii) follows.

引理3.6证明. 连接属性表明$e - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } = e \cap  {e}^{\prime }$。若$e \cap  {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } = \varnothing$，则条件(i)成立。下设$e \cap  {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } \neq  \varnothing$。若$\left( {e \cap  {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  }}\right)  \cap  \mathbf{y} = \varnothing$，则条件(i)成立。若$\left( {e \cap  {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  }}\right)  \cap  \mathbf{y} \neq  \varnothing$，必有$e \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{\text{con }}$；否则${\mathcal{E}}_{\text{con }}$中不存在包含$\left( {e \cap  {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  }}\right)  \cap  \mathbf{y}$输出属性的关系。由于$e \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{\text{con }}$，必有${e}^{\prime } \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{\text{con }}$；否则${\mathcal{E}}_{\text{con }}$无法形成包含$\mathcal{T}$根节点的连通子树。由${\mathcal{E}}_{\text{con }},e \cap  {e}^{\prime } \subseteq  \mathbf{y}$性质可得，条件(ii)成立。

From Lemma 3.6, when constructing a free-connex join tree, a reducible relation can always be placed as a leaf node and thus, a child node of any of its anchors.

根据引理3.6，在构建自由连接树时，可约简关系总可作为叶节点放置，因而能成为其任一锚点的子节点。

LEMMA 3.7. A CQ is weak-q-hierarchical if and only if it has a free-connex join tree of height $\leq  2$ .

引理3.7. 当且仅当一个CQ查询具有高度为$\leq  2$的自由连接树时，该查询是弱q分层的。

Proof. Only-If Direction. Let $Q$ be a weak-q-hierarchical CQ. As ${Q}_{ * }$ is q-hierarchical,it has a height-1 free-connex join tree ${\mathcal{T}}_{ * }$ ,with a one-to-one correspondence between leaf nodes in ${\mathcal{T}}_{ * }$ and relations in ${\mathcal{E}}_{ * }$ . For each relation $e \in  \mathcal{E} - {\mathcal{E}}_{ * }$ ,it is always feasible to find some ${e}^{\prime } \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{ * }$ such that one of the two conditions of reducible relations is satisfied by the transitive property of anchor. We add $e$ as a child of ${e}^{\prime }$ . The resulting tree $\mathcal{T}$ is a height-2 free-connex join tree for $Q$ .

证明。仅当方向。设$Q$为弱q分层连接查询。由于${Q}_{ * }$是q分层的，其存在高度为1的自由连接树${\mathcal{T}}_{ * }$，其中叶节点与${\mathcal{E}}_{ * }$中的关系一一对应。对于每个关系$e \in  \mathcal{E} - {\mathcal{E}}_{ * }$，总能找到某个${e}^{\prime } \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{ * }$使得锚点的传递性满足可约关系的两个条件之一。我们将$e$添加为${e}^{\prime }$的子节点。最终生成的树$\mathcal{T}$即为$Q$的高度为2的自由连接树。

If Direction. Let $\mathcal{T}$ be a free-connex join tree of $\mathcal{Q}$ . If $\mathcal{T}$ has a height of $1,\mathcal{Q}$ is q-hierarchical by Lemma 2.2. Implied by Lemma 2.3,the ${\mathcal{E}}_{ * }$ -induced CQ is q-hierarchical. We next focus on the case when $\mathcal{T}$ has a height of 2,with the connex subset ${\mathcal{E}}_{\text{con }}$ . Let ${\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  }$ be the set of unique attributes in $Q$ . Implied by Lemma 3.6,each leaf node $e$ is reducible with its parent node ${e}^{\prime }$ as the anchor. Let ${\mathcal{T}}^{\prime }$ be the residual tree by removing all leaf nodes of $\mathcal{T}$ . Let ${\mathcal{E}}^{\prime } \subseteq  \mathcal{E}$ be the set of input relations in ${\mathcal{T}}^{\prime }$ . As $\mathcal{T}$ has height 2, ${\mathcal{T}}^{\prime }$ has height 1. From Lemma 2.2, $Q\left\lbrack  {\mathcal{E}}^{\prime }\right\rbrack$ is $q$ -hierarchical. Let ${\mathcal{E}}_{ * }$ be the skeleton of ${\mathcal{E}}^{\prime }$ by invoking TRIM to $\mathcal{Q}\left\lbrack  {\mathcal{E}}^{\prime }\right\rbrack$ . As every leaf node $e \in  \mathcal{T}$ is reducible with its parent as an anchor, ${\mathcal{E}}_{ * }$ is also a skeleton of $\mathcal{E}$ . From Lemma 2.3, ${\mathcal{Q}}_{ * }$ is also q-hierarchical,since ${\mathcal{E}}_{ * } \subseteq  {\mathcal{E}}^{\prime }$ .

当方向。设$\mathcal{T}$为$\mathcal{Q}$的自由连接树。若$\mathcal{T}$高度为$1,\mathcal{Q}$，则根据引理2.2可知其为q分层。由引理2.3可得，${\mathcal{E}}_{ * }$导出的连接查询是q分层的。接下来考察$\mathcal{T}$高度为2且具有连通子集${\mathcal{E}}_{\text{con }}$的情况。令${\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  }$表示$Q$中的唯一属性集。根据引理3.6，每个叶节点$e$均可以其父节点${e}^{\prime }$为锚点进行约简。设${\mathcal{T}}^{\prime }$为移除$\mathcal{T}$所有叶节点后的残差树，${\mathcal{E}}^{\prime } \subseteq  \mathcal{E}$表示${\mathcal{T}}^{\prime }$中的输入关系集。由于$\mathcal{T}$高度为2，${\mathcal{T}}^{\prime }$高度为1。由引理2.2可知$Q\left\lbrack  {\mathcal{E}}^{\prime }\right\rbrack$是$q$分层的。对$\mathcal{Q}\left\lbrack  {\mathcal{E}}^{\prime }\right\rbrack$调用TRIM得到${\mathcal{E}}^{\prime }$的骨架${\mathcal{E}}_{ * }$。由于每个叶节点$e \in  \mathcal{T}$均可以其父节点为锚点约简，${\mathcal{E}}_{ * }$也是$\mathcal{E}$的骨架。根据引理2.3，因${\mathcal{E}}_{ * } \subseteq  {\mathcal{E}}^{\prime }$，${\mathcal{Q}}_{ * }$也是q分层的。

We highlight a critical observation regarding the enclosureness of FIFO update sequences in Lemma 3.8. Combining it with Lemma 3.7, we prove the first half of Theorem 1.11.

我们强调关于引理3.8中FIFO更新序列封闭性的关键观察。结合引理3.7，由此可证定理1.11的前半部分。

LEMMA 3.8 ([25],LEMMA 6.8). For a free-connex ${CQQ}$ with a height-2 free-connex join tree $\mathcal{T}$ , every FIFO sequence for $Q$ has enclosureness as 1 under $\mathcal{T}$ .

引理3.8（[25]引理6.8）。对于具有高度2自由连接树$\mathcal{T}$的自由连接查询${CQQ}$，在$\mathcal{T}$下每个$Q$的FIFO序列封闭性均为1。

### 3.3 Hardness of Maintaining Non-Weak-q-hierarchical CQs

### 3.3 非弱q分层连接查询的维护难度

To demonstrate the difficulty of maintaining non-weak-q-hierarchical CQs, we begin by considering two small queries that fall into this class:

为说明维护非弱q分层连接查询的困难性，我们首先考察属于该类的两个小型查询：

$$
{Q}_{\text{hook }} = {\pi }_{{x}_{1}}\left( {{R}_{1}\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{2}}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{2}\left( {{x}_{2},{x}_{3}}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{3}\left( {x}_{3}\right) }\right) 
$$

$$
{\mathcal{Q}}_{\text{path }} = {\pi }_{\varnothing }\left( {{R}_{1}\left( {x}_{1}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{2}\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{2}}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{3}\left( {{x}_{2},{x}_{3}}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{4}\left( {{x}_{3},{x}_{4}}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{5}\left( {x}_{4}\right) }\right) 
$$

We prove the hardness for maintaining ${\mathcal{Q}}_{\text{hook }}$ over FIFO sequences in Theorem 3.9 and ${\mathcal{Q}}_{\text{path }}$ in Theorem 3.10 separately.

我们分别在定理3.9中证明${\mathcal{Q}}_{\text{hook }}$、定理3.10中证明${\mathcal{Q}}_{\text{path }}$在FIFO序列上的维护难度。

THEOREM 3.9. No index for ${\mathcal{Q}}_{\text{hook }}$ can be updated in $O\left( {\sqrt{\left| D\right| }{}^{1 - \epsilon }}\right)$ amortized time over FIFO sequences while supporting $O\left( \sqrt{{\left| D\right| }^{1 - \epsilon }}\right)$ -delay enumeration for any $\epsilon  > 0$ ,assuming OMv conjecture.

定理3.9：在假设OMv猜想成立的前提下，对于任何$\epsilon  > 0$，不存在能在FIFO序列上以$O\left( {\sqrt{\left| D\right| }{}^{1 - \epsilon }}\right)$摊还时间更新${\mathcal{Q}}_{\text{hook }}$索引并同时支持$O\left( \sqrt{{\left| D\right| }^{1 - \epsilon }}\right)$-延迟枚举的算法。

Proof. Given an arbitrary OMv-instance, we encode the matrix $M$ by ${R}_{1}\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{2}}\right)$ and the vectors $\left\langle  {{v}_{i} : i \in  \left\lbrack  n\right\rbrack  }\right\rangle$ by a subquery ${\pi }_{{x}_{2}}\left( {{R}_{2}\left( {{x}_{2},{x}_{3}}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{3}\left( {x}_{3}\right) }\right)$ . See Figure 7. We construct an update sequence $S$ for ${Q}_{\text{hook }}$ as follows: (1) we first add a tuple $t = \left( {i,j}\right)$ into ${R}_{1}$ with lifespan $\left\lbrack  {{n}^{2} + {in} - j,3{n}^{2} + {in} - j}\right\rbrack$ if ${M}_{ij} \neq  0$ ; (2) we add a tuple $t = \left( i\right)$ with lifespan $\left\lbrack  {{in},{in} + 2{n}^{2}}\right\rbrack$ into ${R}_{3}$ ,for each $i \in  \left\lbrack  n\right\rbrack$ ; (3) after receiving vector ${v}_{i}$ ,we add a tuple $t = \left( {j,i}\right)$ into ${R}_{2}$ with lifespan $\left\lbrack  {2{n}^{2} + {in} - j,4{n}^{2} + {in} - j}\right\rbrack$ if ${\left( {v}_{i}\right) }_{j} \neq  0$ ; (4) we issue the enumeration query at time ${in} + 2{n}^{2}$ : for each result $j$ enumerated for ${\mathcal{Q}}_{\text{hook }}$ ,we set ${\left( M{v}_{i}\right) }_{j} = 1$ ; (5) we repeat (3)-(4) for the next ${v}_{i + 1}$ until $n$ vectors are processed. Each tuple has the same lifespan; hence, $S$ is a FIFO sequence. If an index can be updated in $O\left( {n}^{1 - \epsilon }\right)$ time while supporting $O\left( {n}^{1 - \epsilon }\right)$ -delay enumeration for ${Q}_{\text{hook }}$ over FIFO sequences,the OMv problem can be solved in $O\left( {{n}^{2} \cdot  {n}^{1 - \epsilon } + {n}^{2} \cdot  {n}^{1 - \epsilon }}\right)  = O\left( {n}^{3 - \epsilon }\right)$ time. As $\left| D\right|  = O\left( {n}^{2}\right)$ ,we have $n \geq  \sqrt{\left| D\right| }$ .

证明：给定任意OMv实例，我们通过${R}_{1}\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{2}}\right)$编码矩阵$M$，并通过子查询${\pi }_{{x}_{2}}\left( {{R}_{2}\left( {{x}_{2},{x}_{3}}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{3}\left( {x}_{3}\right) }\right)$编码向量$\left\langle  {{v}_{i} : i \in  \left\lbrack  n\right\rbrack  }\right\rangle$。见图7。我们为${Q}_{\text{hook }}$构建更新序列$S$如下：(1)若${M}_{ij} \neq  0$，首先向${R}_{1}$插入生命周期为$\left\lbrack  {{n}^{2} + {in} - j,3{n}^{2} + {in} - j}\right\rbrack$的元组$t = \left( {i,j}\right)$；(2)对每个$i \in  \left\lbrack  n\right\rbrack$，向${R}_{3}$插入生命周期为$\left\lbrack  {{in},{in} + 2{n}^{2}}\right\rbrack$的元组$t = \left( i\right)$；(3)接收向量${v}_{i}$后，若${\left( {v}_{i}\right) }_{j} \neq  0$，向${R}_{2}$插入生命周期为$\left\lbrack  {2{n}^{2} + {in} - j,4{n}^{2} + {in} - j}\right\rbrack$的元组$t = \left( {j,i}\right)$；(4)在时刻${in} + 2{n}^{2}$发起枚举查询：对${\mathcal{Q}}_{\text{hook }}$枚举的每个结果$j$，设定${\left( M{v}_{i}\right) }_{j} = 1$；(5)对后续${v}_{i + 1}$重复步骤(3)-(4)直至处理完$n$个向量。所有元组具有相同生命周期，故$S$构成FIFO序列。若存在索引能在FIFO序列上以$O\left( {n}^{1 - \epsilon }\right)$时间更新并支持${Q}_{\text{hook }}$的$O\left( {n}^{1 - \epsilon }\right)$-延迟枚举，则OMv问题可在$O\left( {{n}^{2} \cdot  {n}^{1 - \epsilon } + {n}^{2} \cdot  {n}^{1 - \epsilon }}\right)  = O\left( {n}^{3 - \epsilon }\right)$时间内求解。由于$\left| D\right|  = O\left( {n}^{2}\right)$，可得$n \geq  \sqrt{\left| D\right| }$。

Figure 7. A reduction from an OMv instance to a $\sigma$ -sequence for ${Q}_{\text{hook }}$ , where $\sigma \left( {\{ e\} }\right)$ is FIFO for every $e$ .

图7. 将OMv实例归约到${Q}_{\text{hook }}$的$\sigma$序列，其中对任意$e$，$\sigma \left( {\{ e\} }\right)$均满足FIFO特性。

<!-- Media -->

THEOREM 3.10. No index for ${\mathcal{Q}}_{\text{path }}$ can be updated in $O\left( {\sqrt{\left| D\right| }}^{1 - \epsilon }\right)$ time over FIFO sequences while supporting $O\left( {\sqrt{\left| D\right| }}^{2 - \epsilon }\right)$ -delay enumeration for any $\epsilon  > 0$ ,assuming the OuMv conjecture.

定理3.10. 在假设OuMv猜想成立的前提下，对于任何$\epsilon  > 0$，支持$O\left( {\sqrt{\left| D\right| }}^{2 - \epsilon }\right)$-延迟枚举的同时，无法在FIFO序列上以$O\left( {\sqrt{\left| D\right| }}^{1 - \epsilon }\right)$时间复杂度更新${\mathcal{Q}}_{\text{path }}$的索引。

Proof. Given an arbitrary OuMv-instance, we encode the matrix $M$ by ${R}_{3}\left( {{x}_{2},{x}_{3}}\right)$ and the pairs of vectors $\left\langle  \left( {{u}_{i},{v}_{i}}\right) \right.$ : $i \in  \left\lbrack  n\right\rbrack  \rangle$ by two subqueries ${\pi }_{{x}_{2}}\left( {{R}_{2}\left( {{x}_{2},{x}_{1}}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{1}\left( {x}_{1}\right) }\right)$ and ${\pi }_{{x}_{3}}\left( {{R}_{4}\left( {{x}_{3},{x}_{4}}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{5}\left( {x}_{4}\right) }\right)$ . See Figure 8. We construct an update sequence $S$ for ${Q}_{\text{path }}$ as follows: (1) add a tuple $t = \left( {i,j}\right)$ into ${R}_{3}$ with lifespan $\left\lbrack  {2{n}^{2} + {in} - j,6{n}^{2} + {in} - j}\right\rbrack$ ,for each pair $\left( {i,j}\right)  \in  \left\lbrack  n\right\rbrack   \times  \left\lbrack  n\right\rbrack$ if ${M}_{ij} \neq  0$ ; (2) add a tuple $t = \left( i\right)$ with lifespan $\left\lbrack  {\left( {{2i} - 1}\right) n,\left( {{2i} - 1}\right) n + 4{n}^{2}}\right\rbrack$ into ${R}_{1}$ and $\left\lbrack  {{2in},{2in} + 4{n}^{2}}\right\rbrack$ into ${R}_{5}$ ; (3) for each pair of vectors $\left( {{u}_{i},{v}_{i}}\right)$ ,add a tuple $t = \left( {i,j}\right)$ into ${R}_{2}$ with lifespan $\left\lbrack  {\left( {{2i} - 1}\right) n + 4{n}^{2} - j,\left( {{2i} - 1}\right) n + 8{n}^{2} - j}\right\rbrack$ if ${\left( {u}_{i}\right) }_{j} \neq  0$ ,and similarly add a tuple $t = \left( {j,i}\right)$ into ${R}_{4}$ with lifespan $\left\lbrack  {{2in} + 4{n}^{2} - j,{2in} + 8{n}^{2} - j}\right\rbrack$ if ${\left( {v}_{i}\right) }_{j} \neq  0$ ; (4) issue the enumeration query at time $2\mathrm{{in}} + 4{n}^{2}$ : if the result is true for ${\mathcal{Q}}_{\text{path }}$ ,we output true for ${u}_{i}^{T}M{v}_{i}$ ,and false otherwise; (5) repeat (3)-(4) for the next pair $\left( {{u}_{i + 1},{v}_{i + 1}}\right)$ , until $n$ pairs of vectors are processed. Each tuple has the same lifespan; hence $S$ is a FIFO sequence. If an index for ${Q}_{\text{path }}$ can be updated in $O\left( {n}^{1 - \epsilon }\right)$ time over FIFO sequences while supporting $O\left( {n}^{2 - \epsilon }\right)$ - delay enumeration,OuMv can be solved in $O\left( {{n}^{2} \cdot  {n}^{1 - \epsilon } + n \cdot  {n}^{2 - \epsilon }}\right)  = O\left( {n}^{3 - \epsilon }\right)$ time. As $\left| D\right|  = O\left( {n}^{2}\right)$ , we have $n \geq  \sqrt{\left| D\right| }$ .

证明。给定任意OuMv实例，我们通过${R}_{3}\left( {{x}_{2},{x}_{3}}\right)$对矩阵$M$进行编码，并通过两个子查询${\pi }_{{x}_{2}}\left( {{R}_{2}\left( {{x}_{2},{x}_{1}}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{1}\left( {x}_{1}\right) }\right)$和${\pi }_{{x}_{3}}\left( {{R}_{4}\left( {{x}_{3},{x}_{4}}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{5}\left( {x}_{4}\right) }\right)$对向量对$\left\langle  \left( {{u}_{i},{v}_{i}}\right) \right.$:$i \in  \left\lbrack  n\right\rbrack  \rangle$进行编码。见图8。我们为${Q}_{\text{path }}$构建更新序列$S$如下：(1) 若${M}_{ij} \neq  0$，则为每对$\left( {i,j}\right)  \in  \left\lbrack  n\right\rbrack   \times  \left\lbrack  n\right\rbrack$向${R}_{3}$添加生命周期为$\left\lbrack  {2{n}^{2} + {in} - j,6{n}^{2} + {in} - j}\right\rbrack$的元组$t = \left( {i,j}\right)$；(2) 向${R}_{1}$添加生命周期为$\left\lbrack  {\left( {{2i} - 1}\right) n,\left( {{2i} - 1}\right) n + 4{n}^{2}}\right\rbrack$的元组$t = \left( i\right)$，并向${R}_{5}$添加$\left\lbrack  {{2in},{2in} + 4{n}^{2}}\right\rbrack$；(3) 对每个向量对$\left( {{u}_{i},{v}_{i}}\right)$，若${\left( {u}_{i}\right) }_{j} \neq  0$则向${R}_{2}$添加生命周期为$\left\lbrack  {\left( {{2i} - 1}\right) n + 4{n}^{2} - j,\left( {{2i} - 1}\right) n + 8{n}^{2} - j}\right\rbrack$的元组$t = \left( {i,j}\right)$，类似地若${\left( {v}_{i}\right) }_{j} \neq  0$则向${R}_{4}$添加生命周期为$\left\lbrack  {{2in} + 4{n}^{2} - j,{2in} + 8{n}^{2} - j}\right\rbrack$的元组$t = \left( {j,i}\right)$；(4) 在时间点$2\mathrm{{in}} + 4{n}^{2}$发起枚举查询：若结果对${\mathcal{Q}}_{\text{path }}$为真，则输出${u}_{i}^{T}M{v}_{i}$为真，否则为假；(5) 对下一对$\left( {{u}_{i + 1},{v}_{i + 1}}\right)$重复步骤(3)-(4)，直至处理完$n$个向量对。所有元组具有相同生命周期，故$S$是FIFO序列。若${Q}_{\text{path }}$的索引可在FIFO序列上以$O\left( {n}^{1 - \epsilon }\right)$时间更新并支持$O\left( {n}^{2 - \epsilon }\right)$延迟枚举，则OuMv可在$O\left( {{n}^{2} \cdot  {n}^{1 - \epsilon } + n \cdot  {n}^{2 - \epsilon }}\right)  = O\left( {n}^{3 - \epsilon }\right)$时间内求解。由于$\left| D\right|  = O\left( {n}^{2}\right)$，可得$n \geq  \sqrt{\left| D\right| }$。
