图8. 将OuMv实例归约到$\sigma$-序列的示例（其中${Q}_{\text{path }}$的每个$e$都满足FIFO性质）。

Algorithm 2: MixDETECT $\left( {\mathcal{Q} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E},\mathbf{y}}\right) ,\sigma }\right)$

算法2：MixDETECT $\left( {\mathcal{Q} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E},\mathbf{y}}\right) ,\sigma }\right)$

---

1 if $\exists$ a reducible relation $e$ with its anchor ${e}^{\prime }$ s.t. $\sigma \left( {\{ e\} }\right)$ and $\sigma \left( \left\{  {e}^{\prime }\right\}  \right)$ are insertion-only then

1 若$\exists$存在可归约关系$e$及其锚点${e}^{\prime }$，使得$\sigma \left( {\{ e\} }\right)$和$\sigma \left( \left\{  {e}^{\prime }\right\}  \right)$仅为插入操作时

			return $\operatorname{MIXDETECT}\left( {Q\left\lbrack  {\mathcal{E}-\{ e\} }\right\rbrack  ,{\pi }_{\mathcal{E}-\{ e\} }\sigma }\right)$ ;

					返回 $\operatorname{MIXDETECT}\left( {Q\left\lbrack  {\mathcal{E}-\{ e\} }\right\rbrack  ,{\pi }_{\mathcal{E}-\{ e\} }\sigma }\right)$ ;

	else if $Q$ is non-weak-q-hierarchical then return false;

	否则若$Q$不满足弱q分层性则返回假;

4 else if $Q$ does not have a free-connex join tree compatible with $\sigma$ then return false;

4 否则若$Q$不存在与$\sigma$兼容的自由连接树则返回假;

	return true;

	返回真;

---

We can extend the hardness result to general free-connex but non-weak-q-hierarchical CQs, as established in Theorem 3.12. The proof leverages two key structures identified by Lemma 3.11. All omitted proofs are given in Appendix A.

如定理3.12所述，我们可以将硬度结果推广至自由连接但非弱q分层的CQs。该证明利用了引理3.11识别的两个关键结构，所有省略的证明详见附录A。

LEMMA 3.11. For any free-connex but non-weak-q-hierarchical ${CQ}\mathcal{Q} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E},\mathbf{y}}\right)$ ,it must contain at least one of the following structures:

引理3.11. 任何自由连接但非弱q分层的${CQ}\mathcal{Q} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E},\mathbf{y}}\right)$必然包含以下至少一种结构：

- (hook core) three attributes ${x}_{1} \in  \mathbf{y},{x}_{2},{x}_{3} \in  \overline{\mathbf{y}}$ and three relations ${e}_{1},{e}_{2},{e}_{3} \in  \mathcal{E}$ such that ${x}_{1} \in$ ${e}_{1},{x}_{2} \in  {e}_{1} \cap  {e}_{2} - {e}_{3},{x}_{3} \in  {e}_{2} \cap  {e}_{3}$ ,and no relation $e \in  \mathcal{E}$ contains both ${x}_{1},{x}_{3}$ .

- (钩核) 三个属性${x}_{1} \in  \mathbf{y},{x}_{2},{x}_{3} \in  \overline{\mathbf{y}}$与三个关系${e}_{1},{e}_{2},{e}_{3} \in  \mathcal{E}$，满足${x}_{1} \in$${e}_{1},{x}_{2} \in  {e}_{1} \cap  {e}_{2} - {e}_{3},{x}_{3} \in  {e}_{2} \cap  {e}_{3}$，且不存在同时包含${x}_{1},{x}_{3}$的关系$e \in  \mathcal{E}$。

- (path core) four attributes ${x}_{1},{x}_{2},{x}_{3},{x}_{4} \in  \mathcal{V}$ and five relations ${e}_{1},{e}_{2},{e}_{3},{e}_{4},{e}_{5} \in  \mathcal{E}$ such that ${x}_{1} \in$ ${e}_{1} \cap  {e}_{2},{x}_{2} \in  {e}_{2} \cap  {e}_{3},{x}_{3} \in  {e}_{3} \cap  {e}_{4},{x}_{4} \in  {e}_{4} \cap  {e}_{5}$ ,and no relation $e \in  \mathcal{E}$ contains ${x}_{i},{x}_{j}$ with $\left| {j - i}\right|  > 1$ .

- (路径核) 四个属性${x}_{1},{x}_{2},{x}_{3},{x}_{4} \in  \mathcal{V}$与五个关系${e}_{1},{e}_{2},{e}_{3},{e}_{4},{e}_{5} \in  \mathcal{E}$，满足${x}_{1} \in$${e}_{1} \cap  {e}_{2},{x}_{2} \in  {e}_{2} \cap  {e}_{3},{x}_{3} \in  {e}_{3} \cap  {e}_{4},{x}_{4} \in  {e}_{4} \cap  {e}_{5}$，且不存在包含${x}_{i},{x}_{j}$（其中$\left| {j - i}\right|  > 1$）的关系$e \in  \mathcal{E}$。

THEOREM 3.12. For any free-connex but non-weak-q-hierarchical ${CQ}$ and any $\epsilon  > 0$ ,no index can be updated in $O\left( \sqrt{{\left| D\right| }^{1 - \epsilon }}\right)$ amortized time over FIFO sequences while supporting $O\left( \sqrt{{\left| D\right| }^{1 - \epsilon }}\right)$ -delay enumeration, assuming the OuMv and OMv conjectures.

定理3.12. 在OuMv和OMv猜想成立的前提下，对于任何自由连接但非弱q分层的${CQ}$及任意$\epsilon  > 0$，不存在索引能在FIFO序列上以$O\left( \sqrt{{\left| D\right| }^{1 - \epsilon }}\right)$摊还时间更新的同时支持$O\left( \sqrt{{\left| D\right| }^{1 - \epsilon }}\right)$延迟枚举。

## 4 MAINTAIN CQS OVER MIXED SEQUENCES

## 4 混合序列上的CQS维护

This section presents a procedure, MIxDETECT, which determines the hardness of maintaining a CQ $\mathcal{Q}$ over $\sigma$ -sequences,as described in Algorithm 2. Note that when $\operatorname{MIxDETECT}\left( {\mathcal{Q},\sigma }\right)$ returns true, $Q$ can be maintained in $O\left( 1\right)$ amortized time over $\sigma$ -sequences; otherwise,it cannot be maintained in $O\left( {\sqrt{N}}^{1 - \epsilon }\right)$ amortized time for any $\epsilon  > 0$ . All missing proofs are given in Appendix B.

本节介绍了一种名为MIxDETECT的算法流程，用于判定在$\sigma$序列上维护CQ查询$\mathcal{Q}$的计算复杂度，如算法2所述。需注意当$\operatorname{MIxDETECT}\left( {\mathcal{Q},\sigma }\right)$返回真值时，可在$\sigma$序列上以$O\left( 1\right)$摊还时间维护$Q$；否则，对于任意$\epsilon  > 0$都无法在$O\left( {\sqrt{N}}^{1 - \epsilon }\right)$摊还时间内实现维护。所有缺失的证明详见附录B。

As described in Algorithm 2, the high-level idea is to iteratively simplify the input CQ and update sequences until a base case is reached. First,a reducible relation $e$ is removed if it has an anchor ${e}^{\prime }$ such that both $\sigma \left( {\{ e\} }\right)$ and $\sigma \left( \left\{  {e}^{\prime }\right\}  \right)$ are insertion-only (lines 1-2,Lemma 4.3). Next,the procedure returns false if $Q$ is not weak-q-hierarchical (line 3, Lemma 4.4) or does not have a free-connex join tree compatible with $\sigma$ (line 4,Lemma 4.8). If neither condition is met, the procedure returns true (line 4, Lemma 4.16). Although different orderings may be used for removing reducible relations, they always yield the same final result.

如算法2所示，核心思路是通过迭代简化输入CQ并更新序列直至达到基准情形。首先，若可约关系<b0></b1>存在满足$\sigma \left( {\{ e\} }\right)$和$\sigma \left( \left\{  {e}^{\prime }\right\}  \right)$均为仅插入操作的锚点${e}^{\prime }$，则移除该关系（第1-2行，引理4.3）。接着，若$Q$不满足弱q分层性（第3行，引理4.4）或不存在与$\sigma$兼容的自由连接树（第4行，引理4.8），则返回假值。若均不满足上述条件，则返回真值（第4行，引理4.16）。虽然移除可约关系的顺序可能不同，但最终结果始终保持一致。

Figure 9. A compatible free-connex join tree for ${Q}_{4}$ .

图9. ${Q}_{4}$的兼容自由连接树结构

<!-- Media -->

Definition 4.1 (Compatible Free-Connex Join Tree). For a weak-q-hierarchical CQ $\mathcal{Q} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E},\mathbf{y}}\right)$ and a pattern $\sigma$ of update sequences,a free-connex join tree $\mathcal{T}$ for $Q$ is compatible with $\sigma$ if for each leaf node $e$ in $\mathcal{T}$ with its parent node ${e}^{\prime }$ (where both $e$ and ${e}^{\prime }$ correspond to input relations of $\mathcal{E}$ ),the following conditions hold: (1) ${e}^{\prime }$ is an anchor of $e$ ; and (2) either $\sigma \left( {\{ e\} }\right)$ is insertion-only,or $\sigma \left( \left\{  {e,{e}^{\prime }}\right\}  \right)$ is FIFO.

定义4.1（兼容自由连接树）。对于弱q分层CQ查询$\mathcal{Q} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E},\mathbf{y}}\right)$和更新序列模式$\sigma$，若自由连接树$\mathcal{T}$满足：对于树中每个叶节点$e$及其父节点${e}^{\prime }$（二者均对应$\mathcal{E}$的输入关系），(1) ${e}^{\prime }$是$e$的锚点；(2) $\sigma \left( {\{ e\} }\right)$为仅插入操作或$\sigma \left( \left\{  {e,{e}^{\prime }}\right\}  \right)$符合FIFO原则，则该连接树与$\sigma$兼容。

Example 4.2. Consider Example 2.1 with a pattern $\sigma$ of update sequences where $\sigma \left( \left\{  {e}_{1}\right\}  \right) ,\sigma \left( \left\{  {e}_{8}\right\}  \right)$ and $\sigma \left( \left\{  {e}_{9}\right\}  \right)$ are arbitrary, $\sigma \left( \left\{  {{e}_{2},{e}_{3},{e}_{4}}\right\}  \right)$ is insertion-only and $\sigma \left( \left\{  {{e}_{5},{e}_{6},{e}_{7}}\right\}  \right)$ is FIFO. We remove ${e}_{4}$ since ${e}_{4}$ is reducible with ${e}_{3}$ serving as an anchor,and $\sigma \left( \left\{  {e}_{3}\right\}  \right) ,\sigma \left( \left\{  {e}_{4}\right\}  \right)$ are insertion-only. The resulting CQ ${\mathcal{Q}}_{4} = {R}_{1}\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{2},{x}_{3}}\right)  \ltimes  {R}_{2}\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{2}}\right)  \ltimes  {R}_{3}\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{4}}\right)  \ltimes  {R}_{5}\left( {{x}_{2},{x}_{3}}\right)  \ltimes  {R}_{6}\left( {{x}_{2},{x}_{6}}\right)  \ltimes  {R}_{7}\left( {{x}_{3},{x}_{7}}\right)  \ltimes$ ${R}_{8}\left( {{x}_{3},{x}_{8}}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{9}\left( {{x}_{3},{x}_{9}}\right)$ is weak-q-hierarchical with a compatible free-connex join tree,as illustrated in Figure 9. Consequently, Algorithm 2 returns true.

例4.2. 考虑示例2.1中的更新序列模式$\sigma$，其中$\sigma \left( \left\{  {e}_{1}\right\}  \right) ,\sigma \left( \left\{  {e}_{8}\right\}  \right)$和$\sigma \left( \left\{  {e}_{9}\right\}  \right)$为任意类型，$\sigma \left( \left\{  {{e}_{2},{e}_{3},{e}_{4}}\right\}  \right)$仅为插入操作，$\sigma \left( \left\{  {{e}_{5},{e}_{6},{e}_{7}}\right\}  \right)$遵循先进先出原则。由于${e}_{4}$可通过锚点${e}_{3}$进行归约，且$\sigma \left( \left\{  {e}_{3}\right\}  \right) ,\sigma \left( \left\{  {e}_{4}\right\}  \right)$仅为插入操作，故移除${e}_{4}$。最终得到的CQ查询${\mathcal{Q}}_{4} = {R}_{1}\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{2},{x}_{3}}\right)  \ltimes  {R}_{2}\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{2}}\right)  \ltimes  {R}_{3}\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{4}}\right)  \ltimes  {R}_{5}\left( {{x}_{2},{x}_{3}}\right)  \ltimes  {R}_{6}\left( {{x}_{2},{x}_{6}}\right)  \ltimes  {R}_{7}\left( {{x}_{3},{x}_{7}}\right)  \ltimes$${R}_{8}\left( {{x}_{3},{x}_{8}}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{9}\left( {{x}_{3},{x}_{9}}\right)$是弱q-层次化的，其具有兼容的自由连接树结构如图9所示。因此，算法2返回真值。

Let’s continue with Example 4.2 by considering a different pattern $\sigma$ of update sequences,with the only exception being that $\sigma \left( \left\{  {e}_{4}\right\}  \right)$ is arbitrary. In this case,the simplification step is not applied, and the resulting query is not weak-q-hierarchical. As a result, Algorithm 2 returns false.

延续例4.2的讨论，考虑另一种更新序列模式$\sigma$，唯一区别在于$\sigma \left( \left\{  {e}_{4}\right\}  \right)$为任意类型。此时未应用简化步骤，所得查询不具备弱q-层次性。因此，算法2返回假值。

The remainder of this section is organized as follows. In Section 4.1, we prove the correctness of lines 1-2 in Algorithm 2, i.e., the simplification step does not change the problem's hardness. We then prove the correctness of three base cases of Algorithm 2: line 3 in Section 4.2, line 4 in Section 4.3 and line 5 in Section 4.4.

本节剩余内容安排如下：第4.1节将证明算法2中第1-2行的正确性，即简化步骤不会改变问题复杂度；随后分别在4.2节、4.3节和4.4节证明算法2的三个基础案例：第3行、第4行及第5行的正确性。

### 4.1 Simplification Preserves Hardness: Lines 1-2 of Algorithm 2

### 4.1 简化步骤保持复杂度：算法2第1-2行

LEMMA 4.3. For a free-connex ${CQQ} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E},\mathbf{y}}\right)$ and a pattern $\sigma$ of update sequences,for any reducible relation $e$ and its anchor ${e}^{\prime }$ ,if both $\sigma \left( {\{ e\} }\right)$ and $\sigma \left( \left\{  {e}^{\prime }\right\}  \right)$ are insertion-only,then $Q$ can be maintained in $O\left( 1\right)$ time over $\sigma$ -sequences if and only if $\mathcal{Q}\left\lbrack  {\mathcal{E}-\{ e\} }\right\rbrack$ can be maintained in $O\left( 1\right)$ time over ${\pi }_{\mathcal{E}-\{ e\} }\sigma$ -sequences.

引理4.3. 对于自由连接查询${CQQ} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E},\mathbf{y}}\right)$及更新序列模式$\sigma$，若可归约关系$e$及其锚点${e}^{\prime }$满足$\sigma \left( {\{ e\} }\right)$和$\sigma \left( \left\{  {e}^{\prime }\right\}  \right)$均为仅插入操作，则$Q$在$\sigma$序列上能否以$O\left( 1\right)$时间维护，等价于$\mathcal{Q}\left\lbrack  {\mathcal{E}-\{ e\} }\right\rbrack$在${\pi }_{\mathcal{E}-\{ e\} }\sigma$序列上能否以$O\left( 1\right)$时间维护。

Proof. We give a running example in Appendix B to help illustrate the algorithms used in the proof below. For simplicity,let ${\mathcal{Q}}^{\prime } = \mathcal{Q}\left\lbrack  {\mathcal{E}-\{ e\} }\right\rbrack$ .

证明. 附录B提供了运行示例以辅助理解下述证明所用算法。为简化表述，设${\mathcal{Q}}^{\prime } = \mathcal{Q}\left\lbrack  {\mathcal{E}-\{ e\} }\right\rbrack$。

If Direction. Let ${\mathcal{A}}^{\prime }$ be an algorithm maintaining ${Q}^{\prime }$ in $O\left( 1\right)$ time over ${\pi }_{\mathcal{E}-\{ e\} }\sigma$ -sequences. For a $\sigma$ - sequence $S$ for $Q$ ,we construct a sequence ${S}^{\prime }$ for ${Q}^{\prime }$ as follows. For every update $u = \left( {t,s, + ,{R}_{{e}^{\prime \prime }}}\right)  \in$ $S$ ,if ${e}^{\prime \prime } \in  \mathcal{E} - \left\{  {e,{e}^{\prime }}\right\}$ ,we add it to ${S}^{\prime }$ . We maintain the results of ${R}_{{e}^{\prime }} \ltimes  {R}_{e}$ on the update sequence ${\pi }_{e,{e}^{\prime }}S$ . For every delta result $t \in  {R}_{{e}^{\prime }} \ltimes  {R}_{e}$ that is generated at timestamp $s$ ,we insert $u = \left( {t,s, + ,{R}_{{e}^{\prime }}}\right)$ into the ${S}^{\prime }$ . As all updates to ${R}_{{e}^{\prime }}$ are insertion-only, ${S}^{\prime }$ is a ${\pi }_{\mathcal{E}-\{ e\} }\sigma$ -sequence. We run algorithm ${\mathcal{A}}^{\prime }$ for ${Q}^{\prime }$ over ${S}^{\prime }$ . For each result $t$ enumerated by ${\mathcal{A}}^{\prime }$ ,we enumerate all results in ${\pi }_{\mathrm{y}}\left( {{R}_{e} \bowtie  t}\right)$ for $Q$ within $O\left( 1\right)$ delay if $e \cap  \mathbf{y} \cap  {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } \neq  \varnothing$ ,and enumerate $t$ directly otherwise. As both ${\pi }_{e}S$ and ${\pi }_{{e}^{\prime }}S$ are insertion-only,the delta of ${R}_{{e}^{\prime }} \ltimes  {R}_{e}$ can be maintained in $O\left( 1\right)$ amortized time,and enumerated within $O\left( 1\right)$ delay. Hence, $Q$ can be maintained in $O\left( 1\right)$ time over $\sigma$ -sequences.

方向性假设。设${\mathcal{A}}^{\prime }$为一种在${\pi }_{\mathcal{E}-\{ e\} }\sigma$序列上以$O\left( 1\right)$时间复杂度维护${Q}^{\prime }$的算法。对于$Q$的$\sigma$序列$S$，我们按如下方式构造${Q}^{\prime }$的序列${S}^{\prime }$：每当$S$发生更新$u = \left( {t,s, + ,{R}_{{e}^{\prime \prime }}}\right)  \in$时，若${e}^{\prime \prime } \in  \mathcal{E} - \left\{  {e,{e}^{\prime }}\right\}$成立，则将其加入${S}^{\prime }$。我们在更新序列${\pi }_{e,{e}^{\prime }}S$上维护${R}_{{e}^{\prime }} \ltimes  {R}_{e}$的结果集。对于时间戳$s$生成的每个增量结果$t \in  {R}_{{e}^{\prime }} \ltimes  {R}_{e}$，将$u = \left( {t,s, + ,{R}_{{e}^{\prime }}}\right)$插入${S}^{\prime }$。由于${R}_{{e}^{\prime }}$的所有更新均为只增操作，${S}^{\prime }$构成${\pi }_{\mathcal{E}-\{ e\} }\sigma$序列。我们对${S}^{\prime }$运行${Q}^{\prime }$的算法${\mathcal{A}}^{\prime }$。对于${\mathcal{A}}^{\prime }$枚举的每个结果$t$，若$e \cap  \mathbf{y} \cap  {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } \neq  \varnothing$成立则在$O\left( 1\right)$延迟内枚举$Q$在${\pi }_{\mathrm{y}}\left( {{R}_{e} \bowtie  t}\right)$中的所有结果，否则直接枚举$t$。鉴于${\pi }_{e}S$与${\pi }_{{e}^{\prime }}S$均为只增序列，${R}_{{e}^{\prime }} \ltimes  {R}_{e}$的增量可在$O\left( 1\right)$摊还时间内维护，并在$O\left( 1\right)$延迟内完成枚举。因此，$Q$可在$\sigma$序列上以$O\left( 1\right)$时间复杂度实现维护。

Only-If Direction. Let $\mathcal{A}$ be an algorithm that can maintain $\mathcal{Q}$ in $O\left( 1\right)$ time over any $\sigma$ -sequence. We next show another algorithm ${\mathcal{A}}^{\prime }$ for ${Q}^{\prime }$ . Given a ${\pi }_{\mathcal{E}-\{ e\} }\sigma$ -sequence ${S}^{\prime }$ for ${Q}^{\prime }$ ,we construct a sequence $S$ for $Q$ as follows. For every update $u \in  {S}^{\prime }$ ,we add it to $S$ . For every update $u =$ $\left( {t,s,+,{R}_{{e}^{\prime }}}\right)  \in  {S}^{\prime }$ ,we add an update ${u}^{\prime } = \left( {{t}^{\prime },s,+,{R}_{e}}\right)$ to $S$ ,such that ${\pi }_{e \cap  {e}^{\prime }}{t}^{\prime } = {\pi }_{e \cap  {e}^{\prime }}t$ and ${\pi }_{x}{t}^{\prime } = \{  * \}$ for every attribute $x \in  e - {e}^{\prime }$ . All updates to ${R}_{e}$ are insertion-only,hence, $S$ is a $\sigma$ -sequence. We run algorithm $\mathcal{A}$ for $\mathcal{Q}$ over $S$ . For every result $t$ enumerated for $\mathcal{Q}$ by $\mathcal{A}$ ,we output ${t}^{\prime } = {\pi }_{\mathrm{y} - e \cap  {\mathcal{V}}_{ * }}t$ for ${Q}^{\prime }$ . As ${\pi }_{e \cap  {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  }}t = \{  * \}$ (if $e \cap  {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } \neq  \varnothing$ ) for every tuple $t$ enumerated for $Q$ ,and there is no pair of identical tuples enumerated for $Q$ ,all tuples enumerated for ${Q}^{\prime }$ are also distinct. Putting everything together, ${\mathcal{A}}^{\prime }$ can maintain ${Q}^{\prime }$ in $O\left( 1\right)$ time over any ${\pi }_{\mathcal{E}-\{ e\} }\sigma$ -sequence.

仅当方向。设$\mathcal{A}$为能在$O\left( 1\right)$时间内维护任意$\sigma$序列中$\mathcal{Q}$的算法。接下来我们展示另一个针对${Q}^{\prime }$的算法${\mathcal{A}}^{\prime }$。给定${Q}^{\prime }$的${\pi }_{\mathcal{E}-\{ e\} }\sigma$序列${S}^{\prime }$，我们按如下方式构造$Q$的序列$S$：对于每个更新$u \in  {S}^{\prime }$，将其加入$S$；对于每个更新$u =$$\left( {t,s,+,{R}_{{e}^{\prime }}}\right)  \in  {S}^{\prime }$，向$S$添加更新${u}^{\prime } = \left( {{t}^{\prime },s,+,{R}_{e}}\right)$，使得${\pi }_{e \cap  {e}^{\prime }}{t}^{\prime } = {\pi }_{e \cap  {e}^{\prime }}t$且每个属性$x \in  e - {e}^{\prime }$满足${\pi }_{x}{t}^{\prime } = \{  * \}$。由于对${R}_{e}$的所有更新仅为插入操作，故$S$是$\sigma$序列。我们在$S$上运行算法$\mathcal{A}$维护$\mathcal{Q}$。对于$\mathcal{A}$为$\mathcal{Q}$枚举的每个结果$t$，我们输出${Q}^{\prime }$的${t}^{\prime } = {\pi }_{\mathrm{y} - e \cap  {\mathcal{V}}_{ * }}t$。由于对$Q$枚举的每个元组$t$满足${\pi }_{e \cap  {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  }}t = \{  * \}$（若$e \cap  {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } \neq  \varnothing$），且不存在重复枚举的元组，因此为${Q}^{\prime }$枚举的所有元组也互不相同。综上，${\mathcal{A}}^{\prime }$能在$O\left( 1\right)$时间内维护任意${\pi }_{\mathcal{E}-\{ e\} }\sigma$序列上的${Q}^{\prime }$。

### 4.2 Base Case 1: Line 3 of Algorithm 2

### 4.2 基础情形1：算法2第3行

LEMMA 4.4. For a free-connex ${CQ}$ and a pattern $\sigma$ of update sequences,to which no simplification step can be applied,if $Q$ is non-weak-q-hierarchical, $Q$ cannot be maintained in $O\left( {\sqrt{\left| D\right| }}^{1 - \epsilon }\right)$ time over $\sigma$ -sequences,assuming the OuMv and OMv conjectures.

引理4.4. 对于自由连接查询${CQ}$及无法再简化的更新序列模式$\sigma$，若$Q$非弱q层次结构，则在OuMv和OMv猜想成立的前提下，无法在$O\left( {\sqrt{\left| D\right| }}^{1 - \epsilon }\right)$时间内维护$\sigma$序列上的$Q$。

Let’s start by revisiting two simplest free-connex but non-weak-q-hierarchical CQs, ${Q}_{\text{hook }}$ and ${Q}_{\text{path }}$ . In Theorems 3.9 and 3.10,we already have demonstrated the difficulty of maintaining ${Q}_{\text{hook }}$ and ${\mathcal{Q}}_{\text{path }}$ over $\sigma$ -sequences when $\sigma \left( \mathcal{E}\right)$ is FIFO. Recall that $\mathcal{E}$ is the set of all relations. The condition that $\sigma \left( \mathcal{E}\right)$ is FIFO also implies that $\sigma \left( {\mathcal{E}}^{\prime }\right)$ is FIFO for an arbitrary subset ${\mathcal{E}}^{\prime } \subseteq  \mathcal{E}$ of relations. We now extend this hardness condition in Examples 4.5 and 4.6.

首先重新审视两个最简单的自由连接但非弱q层次结构的CQ：${Q}_{\text{hook }}$和${Q}_{\text{path }}$。在定理3.9和3.10中，我们已证明当$\sigma \left( \mathcal{E}\right)$为FIFO时，在$\sigma$序列上维护${Q}_{\text{hook }}$和${\mathcal{Q}}_{\text{path }}$的困难性。注意$\mathcal{E}$表示所有关系的集合。当$\sigma \left( \mathcal{E}\right)$为FIFO时，也意味着关系子集${\mathcal{E}}^{\prime } \subseteq  \mathcal{E}$对应的$\sigma \left( {\mathcal{E}}^{\prime }\right)$具有FIFO特性。我们将在示例4.5和4.6中扩展这一困难性条件。

Proc. ACM Manag. Data, Vol. 3, No. 2 (PODS), Article 117. Publication date: May 2025.

《ACM数据管理汇刊》第3卷第2期(PODS)，文章117。出版日期：2025年5月。

Example 4.5. For ${Q}_{\text{hook }} = {\pi }_{{x}_{1}}{R}_{1}\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{2}}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{2}\left( {{x}_{2},{x}_{3}}\right)  \bowtie$ ${R}_{3}\left( {x}_{3}\right)$ ,and a pattern $\sigma$ of update sequences where either $\sigma \left( \left\{  {e}_{3}\right\}  \right)$ or $\sigma \left( \left\{  {e}_{2}\right\}  \right)$ is not insertion-only,no index can be updated in $O\left( {\sqrt{\left| D\right| }}^{1 - \epsilon }\right)$ amortized time over $\sigma$ -sequences while supporting $O\left( {\sqrt{\left| D\right| }}^{1 - \epsilon }\right)$ -delay enumeration for any $\epsilon  > 0$ ,assuming the OMv conjecture [7]. Given an OMv instance,we use ${R}_{1}$ to encode the matrix and the following subquery ${R}_{4} = {\pi }_{{x}_{2}}{R}_{2}\left( {{x}_{2},{x}_{3}}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{3}\left( {x}_{3}\right)$ to encode vectors. We essentially maintain ${Q}_{\text{qcore }} = {\pi }_{{x}_{1}}{R}_{1}\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{2}}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{4}\left( {x}_{2}\right)$ over ${\sigma }^{\prime }$ - sequences,where ${\sigma }^{\prime }\left( \left\{  {e}_{4}\right\}  \right)$ is arbitrary. The hardness result follows [7]. See Figures 10 - 11 for illustrations.

示例4.5。对于${Q}_{\text{hook }} = {\pi }_{{x}_{1}}{R}_{1}\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{2}}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{2}\left( {{x}_{2},{x}_{3}}\right)  \bowtie$${R}_{3}\left( {x}_{3}\right)$，以及更新序列模式$\sigma$（其中$\sigma \left( \left\{  {e}_{3}\right\}  \right)$或$\sigma \left( \left\{  {e}_{2}\right\}  \right)$非纯插入类型），在OMv猜想[7]成立的前提下，任何索引都无法在$\sigma$序列上实现$O\left( {\sqrt{\left| D\right| }}^{1 - \epsilon }\right)$均摊时间更新的同时支持$O\left( {\sqrt{\left| D\right| }}^{1 - \epsilon }\right)$延迟枚举（对任意$\epsilon  > 0$）。给定OMv实例时，我们使用${R}_{1}$编码矩阵，并通过子查询${R}_{4} = {\pi }_{{x}_{2}}{R}_{2}\left( {{x}_{2},{x}_{3}}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{3}\left( {x}_{3}\right)$编码向量。本质上我们在${\sigma }^{\prime }$序列上维护${Q}_{\text{qcore }} = {\pi }_{{x}_{1}}{R}_{1}\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{2}}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{4}\left( {x}_{2}\right)$（其中${\sigma }^{\prime }\left( \left\{  {e}_{4}\right\}  \right)$为任意值）。该硬度结论引自[7]。图示详见附图10-11。

Example 4.6. For ${Q}_{\text{path }} = {\pi }_{\varnothing }{R}_{1}\left( {x}_{1}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{2}\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{2}}\right)  \bowtie$ ${R}_{3}\left( {{x}_{2},{x}_{3}}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{4}\left( {{x}_{3},{x}_{4}}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{5}\left( {x}_{4}\right)$ ,and a pattern $\sigma$ of update sequences such that either $\sigma \left( \left\{  {e}_{1}\right\}  \right)$ or $\sigma \left( \left\{  {e}_{2}\right\}  \right)$ is not insertion-only,and either $\sigma \left( \left\{  {e}_{4}\right\}  \right)$ or $\sigma \left( \left\{  {e}_{5}\right\}  \right)$ is not insertion-only,no index can be updated in $O\left( {\sqrt{\left| D\right| }}^{1 - \epsilon }\right)$ amortized time over $\sigma$ -sequences while supporting $O\left( {\sqrt{\left| D\right| }}^{2 - \epsilon }\right)$ -delay enumeration for any $\epsilon  > 0$ ,assuming the OuMv conjecture. Given an instance of OuMv,we use ${R}_{3}$ to encode the matrix, and use the following two subqueries ${R}_{6} = {\pi }_{{x}_{2}}{R}_{1}\left( {x}_{1}\right)  \bowtie$ ${R}_{2}\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{2}}\right) ,{R}_{7} = {\pi }_{{x}_{3}}{R}_{4}\left( {{x}_{3},{x}_{4}}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{5}\left( {x}_{4}\right)$ to encode pairs of vectors separately. We essentially maintain ${\mathcal{Q}}_{\text{hier }} =$ ${\pi }_{\varnothing }\left( {{R}_{6}\left( {x}_{2}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{3}\left( {{x}_{2},{x}_{3}}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{7}\left( {x}_{3}\right) }\right)$ over ${\sigma }^{\prime }$ -sequences,where ${\sigma }^{\prime }\left( \left\{  {e}_{6}\right\}  \right)$ and ${\sigma }^{\prime }\left( \left\{  {e}_{7}\right\}  \right)$ are arbitrary. The hardness result follows [7]. For an illustration, see Figure 12 which handles the case when $\sigma \left( {e}_{1}\right)$ and $\sigma \left( {e}_{4}\right)$ are not insertion-only. The remaining cases can be proved similarly; additional details are given in Appendix B.

示例4.6 对于${Q}_{\text{path }} = {\pi }_{\varnothing }{R}_{1}\left( {x}_{1}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{2}\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{2}}\right)  \bowtie$${R}_{3}\left( {{x}_{2},{x}_{3}}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{4}\left( {{x}_{3},{x}_{4}}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{5}\left( {x}_{4}\right)$，以及更新序列模式$\sigma$——其中$\sigma \left( \left\{  {e}_{1}\right\}  \right)$或$\sigma \left( \left\{  {e}_{2}\right\}  \right)$非纯插入，且$\sigma \left( \left\{  {e}_{4}\right\}  \right)$或$\sigma \left( \left\{  {e}_{5}\right\}  \right)$非纯插入——在OuMv猜想成立的前提下，不存在索引能在$\sigma$序列上以$O\left( {\sqrt{\left| D\right| }}^{1 - \epsilon }\right)$摊还时间完成更新，同时支持任何$\epsilon  > 0$的$O\left( {\sqrt{\left| D\right| }}^{2 - \epsilon }\right)$延迟枚举。给定OuMv实例，我们使用${R}_{3}$编码矩阵，并通过以下两个子查询${R}_{6} = {\pi }_{{x}_{2}}{R}_{1}\left( {x}_{1}\right)  \bowtie$${R}_{2}\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{2}}\right) ,{R}_{7} = {\pi }_{{x}_{3}}{R}_{4}\left( {{x}_{3},{x}_{4}}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{5}\left( {x}_{4}\right)$分别编码向量对。本质上我们维护${\mathcal{Q}}_{\text{hier }} =$${\pi }_{\varnothing }\left( {{R}_{6}\left( {x}_{2}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{3}\left( {{x}_{2},{x}_{3}}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{7}\left( {x}_{3}\right) }\right)$在${\sigma }^{\prime }$序列上的状态，其中${\sigma }^{\prime }\left( \left\{  {e}_{6}\right\}  \right)$与${\sigma }^{\prime }\left( \left\{  {e}_{7}\right\}  \right)$为任意值。该硬度结论引自[7]。示意图见图12，其中处理了$\sigma \left( {e}_{1}\right)$和$\sigma \left( {e}_{4}\right)$非纯插入的情况，其余情况可类似证明，详见附录B。

We can now extend these hardness results to general free-connex but non-weak-q-hierarchical CQs falling in this case, by reducing either ${\mathcal{Q}}_{\text{hook }}$ or ${\mathcal{Q}}_{\text{path }}$ to the given query with the help of Lemma 3.11. See an example below:

借助引理3.11，通过将${\mathcal{Q}}_{\text{hook }}$或${\mathcal{Q}}_{\text{path }}$归约到给定查询，我们可将这些硬度结果推广到本情形中非弱q-层次结构的自由连接CQ，示例如下：

Example 4.7. Let’s continue with the CQ ${Q}_{1}$ as illustrated in Figure 2,with a pattern $\sigma$ of update sequences where $\sigma \left( {e}_{4}\right)$ and $\sigma \left( {e}_{7}\right)$ are arbitrary. Even if the update patterns for all remaining relations are insertion-only, we can still identify the following subquery ${R}_{4}\left( {{x}_{5},{x}_{4}}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{3}\left( {{x}_{4},{x}_{1}}\right)  \bowtie$ ${R}_{1}\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{2},{x}_{3}}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{5}\left( {{x}_{2},{x}_{3}}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{7}\left( {{x}_{3},{x}_{7}}\right)$ to simulate ${\mathcal{Q}}_{\text{path }}$ , after removing ${e}_{2},{e}_{6},{e}_{8},{e}_{9}$ during the simplification step.

例4.7 我们继续讨论图2所示的CQ查询${Q}_{1}$，其更新序列模式$\sigma$中$\sigma \left( {e}_{4}\right)$和$\sigma \left( {e}_{7}\right)$是任意的。即使所有其他关系的更新模式仅为插入操作，在简化步骤移除${e}_{2},{e}_{6},{e}_{8},{e}_{9}$后，仍可识别出子查询${R}_{4}\left( {{x}_{5},{x}_{4}}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{3}\left( {{x}_{4},{x}_{1}}\right)  \bowtie$${R}_{1}\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{2},{x}_{3}}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{5}\left( {{x}_{2},{x}_{3}}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{7}\left( {{x}_{3},{x}_{7}}\right)$来模拟${\mathcal{Q}}_{\text{path }}$。

Similar to Example 4.6, for any given instance of OuMv, we encode the matrix by ${R}_{1}$ and all pairs of vectors using two subqueries ${\pi }_{{x}_{1}}{R}_{4}\left( {{x}_{5},{x}_{4}}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{3}\left( {{x}_{4},{x}_{1}}\right)$ and ${\pi }_{{x}_{2}}{R}_{5}\left( {{x}_{2},{x}_{3}}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{7}\left( {{x}_{3},{x}_{7}}\right)$ ,respectively. We set the domains of ${x}_{1}$ and ${x}_{3}$ to $\left\lbrack  n\right\rbrack$ ,and set the domain of any remaining attributes to $\{  * \}$ for a special value $*$ . For all the remaining relations,we insert all possible tuples across their domains at the beginning. Consequently, there is a one-to-one correspondence between the query result of ${Q}_{1}$ and that of ${Q}_{\text{path }}$ . Hence,the hardness result for ${Q}_{1}$ follows Example 4.6.

类似于例4.6，对于任意给定的OuMv实例，我们分别通过两个子查询${\pi }_{{x}_{1}}{R}_{4}\left( {{x}_{5},{x}_{4}}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{3}\left( {{x}_{4},{x}_{1}}\right)$和${\pi }_{{x}_{2}}{R}_{5}\left( {{x}_{2},{x}_{3}}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{7}\left( {{x}_{3},{x}_{7}}\right)$对矩阵${R}_{1}$及所有向量对进行编码。将${x}_{1}$和${x}_{3}$的域设为$\left\lbrack  n\right\rbrack$，其他属性的域设为特殊值<b7></b6>。所有其他关系在初始化时插入其定义域内所有可能的元组。因此，${Q}_{1}$的查询结果与${Q}_{\text{path }}$存在一一对应关系，故${Q}_{1}$的硬度结论可直接沿用例4.6的结果。

### 4.3 Base Case 2: Line 4 of Algorithm 2

### 4.3 基础情形2：算法2第4行

LEMMA 4.8. For a weak-q-hierarchical ${CQQ}$ and a pattern $\sigma$ of update sequences,to which no simplification step can be applied if $Q$ is weak-q-hierarchical but does not have a free-connex join tree compatible with $\sigma ,Q$ cannot be maintained in $O\left( {\sqrt{\left| D\right| }}^{1 - \epsilon }\right)$ time over $\sigma$ -sequences for arbitrary $\epsilon  > 0$ ,assuming the ${OuMv}$ and ${OMv}$ conjectures.

引理4.8 对于弱q层次化查询${CQQ}$及更新序列模式$\sigma$，若$Q$是弱q层次化但不具有与$\sigma ,Q$兼容的自由连接树时，在${OuMv}$和${OMv}$猜想成立的前提下，该模式无法在$O\left( {\sqrt{\left| D\right| }}^{1 - \epsilon }\right)$时间内维护任意$\epsilon  > 0$的$\sigma$序列。

If $Q$ is q-hierarchical,it has a height-1 free-connex join tree

若$Q$是q层次化查询，则其必存在高度为1的自由连接树

$\mathcal{T}$ in which each leaf node has its parent as a generalized relation. Consequently, $\mathcal{T}$ is compatible with an arbitrary update sequence. In what follows, we focus on weak-q-hierarchical but non-q-hierarchical CQs. We now revisit the three simplest example CQs whose update sequences follow the pattern falling into base case 2 .

$\mathcal{T}$中每个叶节点都以广义关系形式继承其父节点。因此$\mathcal{T}$可适配任意更新序列。下文我们将聚焦于弱q-层次化但非q-层次化的CQs（合取查询）。现在重新审视三个最简示例CQs，其更新序列模式属于基础案例2。

Example 4.9. For ${\mathcal{Q}}_{\text{qcore }} = {\pi }_{{x}_{1}}{R}_{1}\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{2}}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{2}\left( {x}_{2}\right)$ and a pattern $\sigma$ of update sequences,such that $\sigma \left( \left\{  {e}_{2}\right\}  \right)$ is not insertion-only and $\sigma \left( \left\{  {{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}\right\}  \right)$ is not FIFO,no index can be updated in $O\left( {\sqrt{\left| D\right| }}^{1 - \epsilon }\right)$ amortized time over $\sigma$ -sequences, while supporting $O\left( {\sqrt{\left| D\right| }}^{1 - \epsilon }\right)$ -delay enumeration for any $\epsilon  > 0$ ,assuming the OMv conjecture. ${Q}_{\text{qcore }}$ only has one free-connex join tree $\mathcal{T}$ where ${R}_{1}$ is the root with ${R}_{2}$ as the child node. As $\mathcal{T}$ is not compatible with $\sigma ,\sigma \left( \left\{  {e}_{2}\right\}  \right)$ could be FIFO or arbitrary while $\sigma \left( \left\{  {{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}\right\}  \right)$ is arbitrary. The hardness follows [7]. See Figure 13 for an illustration.

示例4.9. 对于${\mathcal{Q}}_{\text{qcore }} = {\pi }_{{x}_{1}}{R}_{1}\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{2}}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{2}\left( {x}_{2}\right)$及更新序列模式$\sigma$（其中$\sigma \left( \left\{  {e}_{2}\right\}  \right)$非仅插入类型且$\sigma \left( \left\{  {{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}\right\}  \right)$不遵循先进先出规则），在OMv猜想成立前提下，任何索引都无法在$O\left( {\sqrt{\left| D\right| }}^{1 - \epsilon }\right)$摊还时间内完成$\sigma$-序列更新，同时支持对任意$\epsilon  > 0$的$O\left( {\sqrt{\left| D\right| }}^{1 - \epsilon }\right)$-延迟枚举。${Q}_{\text{qcore }}$仅存在一个自由连接树$\mathcal{T}$，其中${R}_{1}$为根节点、${R}_{2}$为子节点。由于$\mathcal{T}$与$\sigma ,\sigma \left( \left\{  {e}_{2}\right\}  \right)$不兼容（$\sigma ,\sigma \left( \left\{  {e}_{2}\right\}  \right)$可为先进先出或任意类型，而$\sigma \left( \left\{  {{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}\right\}  \right)$为任意类型），其困难性论证参见文献[7]。示意图见图13。

Example 4.10. For ${\mathcal{Q}}_{\text{hier }} = {\pi }_{\varnothing }{R}_{1}\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{2}}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{2}\left( {x}_{2}\right)  \bowtie$ ${R}_{3}\left( {x}_{1}\right)$ and a pattern $\sigma$ of update sequences such that $\sigma \left( \left\{  {e}_{2}\right\}  \right)$ and $\sigma \left( \left\{  {e}_{3}\right\}  \right)$ are not insertion-only; and $\sigma \left( \left\{  {{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}\right\}  \right)$ and $\sigma \left( \left\{  {{e}_{1},{e}_{3}}\right\}  \right)$ are not FIFO,no index can be updated in $O\left( {\sqrt{\left| D\right| }}^{1 - \epsilon }\right)$ amortized time over $\sigma$ -sequences,while supporting $O\left( {\sqrt{\left| D\right| }}^{2 - \epsilon }\right)$ -delay enumeration for arbitrary $\epsilon  > 0$ , assuming the OuMv conjecture. Note that ${Q}_{\text{hier }}$ only has three free-connex join trees: (1) ${\mathcal{T}}_{1}$ is rooted at ${R}_{1}$ with ${R}_{2},{R}_{3}$ as its child node; (2) ${\mathcal{T}}_{2}$ is rooted at ${R}_{2}$ with ${R}_{1}$ as the child node of ${R}_{2}$ ,and ${R}_{3}$ as the child node of ${R}_{1}$ ; (3) ${\mathcal{T}}_{3}$ is rooted at ${R}_{3}$ with ${R}_{1}$ as the child node of ${R}_{3}$ ,and ${R}_{2}$ as the child node of ${R}_{1}$ . As none of ${\mathcal{T}}_{1},{\mathcal{T}}_{2},{\mathcal{T}}_{3}$ is not compatible with $\sigma$ ,it must be that $\sigma \left( \left\{  {e}_{2}\right\}  \right)$ and $\sigma \left( \left\{  {e}_{3}\right\}  \right)$ are FIFO or arbitrary; and $\sigma \left( \left\{  {{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}\right\}  \right)$ and $\sigma \left( \left\{  {{e}_{1},{e}_{3}}\right\}  \right)$ are arbitrary. The hardness result then follows [7]. See Figure 14 for an illustration.

示例4.10：对于${\mathcal{Q}}_{\text{hier }} = {\pi }_{\varnothing }{R}_{1}\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{2}}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{2}\left( {x}_{2}\right)  \bowtie$${R}_{3}\left( {x}_{1}\right)$及更新序列模式$\sigma$，当$\sigma \left( \left\{  {e}_{2}\right\}  \right)$与$\sigma \left( \left\{  {e}_{3}\right\}  \right)$非纯插入操作，且$\sigma \left( \left\{  {{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}\right\}  \right)$与$\sigma \left( \left\{  {{e}_{1},{e}_{3}}\right\}  \right)$非先进先出时，在OuMv猜想前提下，任何索引都无法在$\sigma$序列上实现$O\left( {\sqrt{\left| D\right| }}^{1 - \epsilon }\right)$均摊时间更新的同时，支持对任意$\epsilon  > 0$的$O\left( {\sqrt{\left| D\right| }}^{2 - \epsilon }\right)$延迟枚举。需注意${Q}_{\text{hier }}$仅存在三种自由连接树结构：(1)以${R}_{1}$为根节点，${R}_{2},{R}_{3}$作为其子节点；(2)以${R}_{2}$为根节点，${R}_{1}$作为${R}_{2}$的子节点，${R}_{3}$又作为${R}_{1}$的子节点；(3)以${R}_{3}$为根节点，${R}_{1}$作为${R}_{3}$的子节点，${R}_{2}$则作为${R}_{1}$的子节点。由于${\mathcal{T}}_{1},{\mathcal{T}}_{2},{\mathcal{T}}_{3}$均与$\sigma$不兼容，故$\sigma \left( \left\{  {e}_{2}\right\}  \right)$与$\sigma \left( \left\{  {e}_{3}\right\}  \right)$必为先进先出或任意类型，$\sigma \left( \left\{  {{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}\right\}  \right)$与$\sigma \left( \left\{  {{e}_{1},{e}_{3}}\right\}  \right)$则为任意类型。该难度结论详见文献[7]，示意图参见图14。

Example 4.11. Consider ${Q}_{\text{swing }} = {\pi }_{\varnothing }{R}_{1}\left( {x}_{1}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{2}\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{2}}\right)$ $\bowtie  {R}_{3}\left( {{x}_{2},{x}_{3}}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{4}\left( {x}_{3}\right)$ and a pattern $\sigma$ of update sequences such that both $\sigma \left( \left\{  {e}_{1}\right\}  \right) ,\sigma \left( \left\{  {e}_{4}\right\}  \right)$ are not insertion-only,and $\sigma \left( \left\{  {{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}\right\}  \right)$ is not FIFO; and (2) both $\sigma \left( \left\{  {{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}\right\}  \right) ,\sigma \left( \left\{  {{e}_{3},{e}_{4}}\right\}  \right)$ are not FIFO. We first point out that no simplification step can be applied, i.e., no relation can be removed. In any free-connex join tree for ${Q}_{\text{swing }}$ ,we observe that either ${R}_{2}$ must be the parent of ${R}_{1}$ ,or ${R}_{3}$ must be the parent of ${R}_{4}$ . Thus,there exists no free-connex join tree compatible with $\sigma$ and no index can be updated in $O\left( {\sqrt{\left| D\right| }}^{1 - \epsilon }\right)$ amortized time over $\sigma$ -sequences,while supporting $O\left( {\sqrt{\left| D\right| }}^{2 - \epsilon }\right)$ -delay enumeration for any $\epsilon  > 0$ ,assuming the OuMv conjecture.

例4.11 考虑${Q}_{\text{swing }} = {\pi }_{\varnothing }{R}_{1}\left( {x}_{1}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{2}\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{2}}\right)$$\bowtie  {R}_{3}\left( {{x}_{2},{x}_{3}}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{4}\left( {x}_{3}\right)$及更新序列模式$\sigma$，其中(1)$\sigma \left( \left\{  {e}_{1}\right\}  \right) ,\sigma \left( \left\{  {e}_{4}\right\}  \right)$均非仅插入类型，且$\sigma \left( \left\{  {{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}\right\}  \right)$不满足先进先出特性；(2)$\sigma \left( \left\{  {{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}\right\}  \right) ,\sigma \left( \left\{  {{e}_{3},{e}_{4}}\right\}  \right)$均不满足先进先出特性。首先指出无法应用任何简化步骤，即无法移除任何关系。在${Q}_{\text{swing }}$的任何自由连接树中，可观察到${R}_{2}$必须作为${R}_{1}$的父节点，或${R}_{3}$必须作为${R}_{4}$的父节点。因此不存在与$\sigma$兼容的自由连接树，且在假设OuMv猜想成立的前提下，无法在$\sigma$序列上以$O\left( {\sqrt{\left| D\right| }}^{1 - \epsilon }\right)$摊还时间更新索引，同时支持对任意$\epsilon  > 0$的$O\left( {\sqrt{\left| D\right| }}^{2 - \epsilon }\right)$延迟枚举。

<!-- Media -->

Algorithm 3: CompatibleTREE $\left( {Q,\sigma }\right)$

算法3：兼容树构建$\left( {Q,\sigma }\right)$

---

$\mathcal{H} \leftarrow$ the query-update graph for $Q$ and $\sigma$ ;

$\mathcal{H} \leftarrow$为$Q$和$\sigma$构建查询-更新图；

${\mathcal{E}}_{ \circ  } \leftarrow$ the set of nodes with in-degree 0 in $\mathcal{H}$ ;

${\mathcal{E}}_{ \circ  } \leftarrow$标记$\mathcal{H}$中入度为0的节点集合；

3 $\mathcal{T} \leftarrow$ a height-1 free-connex join tree for ${\mathcal{E}}_{ \circ  }$ -induced CQ;

3$\mathcal{T} \leftarrow$构建${\mathcal{E}}_{ \circ  }$诱导的CQ的高度-1自由连接树；

All nodes in $\mathcal{H}$ are marked as non-visited;

将$\mathcal{H}$中所有节点标记为未访问；

while there exists some node in ${\mathcal{E}}_{ \circ  }$ non-visited do

当${\mathcal{E}}_{ \circ  }$中存在未访问节点时执行：

	Visit an arbitrary non-visited node $e$ in ${\mathcal{E}}_{ \circ  }$ ;

	访问${\mathcal{E}}_{ \circ  }$中任意未访问节点$e$；

	$Z \leftarrow  \{ e\}$ ;

	while $Z$ changes from last iteration do

	当$Z$较上次迭代发生变化时执行：

		if $\exists \left( {{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}\right)  \in  \mathcal{H},{e}_{1} \in  Z$ and ${e}_{2}$ is non-visited then

		若$\exists \left( {{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}\right)  \in  \mathcal{H},{e}_{1} \in  Z$与${e}_{2}$均为未访问状态则：

			Add ${e}_{2}$ as a child of ${e}_{1}$ in $\mathcal{T}$ ;

			在$\mathcal{T}$中将${e}_{2}$添加为${e}_{1}$的子节点；

			Mark ${e}_{2}$ as visited;

			标记${e}_{2}$为已访问；

			$Z \leftarrow  Z \cup  \left\{  {e}_{2}\right\}  ;$

return $\mathcal{T}$ ;

返回$\mathcal{T}$；

---

<!-- figureText: $\left\lbrack  {x}_{3}\right.$ ${R}_{8}$ ${R}_{9}$ $\left\lbrack  {{x}_{2},{x}_{3}}\right\rbrack$ ${R}_{5}$ -->

图17.${\mathcal{E}}_{0}$的高度-1自由连接树

<!-- Media -->

Given an instance of OuMv,we use ${R}_{3}$ to encode the matrix and use ${R}_{5} = {\pi }_{{x}_{2}}{R}_{1}\left( {x}_{1}\right)  \boxtimes  {R}_{2}\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{2}}\right)$ and ${R}_{4}$ to encode all pairs of vectors. We essentially maintain ${\mathcal{Q}}_{\text{hier }} = {\pi }_{\varnothing }{R}_{5}\left( {x}_{2}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{3}\left( {{x}_{2},{x}_{3}}\right)  \bowtie$ ${R}_{4}\left( {x}_{3}\right)$ over ${\sigma }^{\prime }$ -sequences,where ${\sigma }^{\prime }\left( \left\{  {e}_{5}\right\}  \right)$ and ${\sigma }^{\prime }\left( \left\{  {e}_{4}\right\}  \right)$ are not insertion-only; and ${\sigma }^{\prime }\left( \left\{  {{e}_{5},{e}_{3}}\right\}  \right)$ and ${\sigma }^{\prime }\left( \left\{  {{e}_{4},{e}_{3}}\right\}  \right)$ are not FIFO since $\sigma \left( \left\{  {e}_{3}\right\}  \right)$ is insertion-only. Hence,the hardness result follows Example 4.10. See Figure 15 for an illustration.

给定一个OuMv实例，我们使用${R}_{3}$对矩阵进行编码，并使用${R}_{5} = {\pi }_{{x}_{2}}{R}_{1}\left( {x}_{1}\right)  \boxtimes  {R}_{2}\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{2}}\right)$和${R}_{4}$对所有向量对进行编码。本质上，我们在${\sigma }^{\prime }$序列上维护${\mathcal{Q}}_{\text{hier }} = {\pi }_{\varnothing }{R}_{5}\left( {x}_{2}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{3}\left( {{x}_{2},{x}_{3}}\right)  \bowtie$${R}_{4}\left( {x}_{3}\right)$，其中${\sigma }^{\prime }\left( \left\{  {e}_{5}\right\}  \right)$和${\sigma }^{\prime }\left( \left\{  {e}_{4}\right\}  \right)$不是仅插入的；而${\sigma }^{\prime }\left( \left\{  {{e}_{5},{e}_{3}}\right\}  \right)$和${\sigma }^{\prime }\left( \left\{  {{e}_{4},{e}_{3}}\right\}  \right)$由于$\sigma \left( \left\{  {e}_{3}\right\}  \right)$是仅插入的，因此不符合FIFO原则。因此，该硬度结果遵循示例4.10。图示请参见图15。

By gaining some intuition, we proceed to the general case. We begin by introducing the concept of a query-update graph $\mathcal{H}$ for a weak-q-hierarchical CQ $\mathcal{Q}$ and a pattern $\sigma$ of update sequences. Let ${\mathcal{E}}_{ * }$ be a skeleton of $\mathcal{Q}$ . In a (directed) query-update graph $\mathcal{H}$ ,every relation $e \in  \mathcal{E}$ is a node,and there is an edge from ${e}^{\prime }$ to $e$ if the following conditions hold: (1) $e \in  \mathcal{E} - {\mathcal{E}}_{ * }$ ,(2) ${e}^{\prime }$ is an anchor of $e$ , and (3) either $\sigma \left( {\{ e\} }\right)$ is insertion-only or $\sigma \left( \left\{  {e,{e}^{\prime }}\right\}  \right)$ is FIFO. Let ${\mathcal{E}}_{ \circ  }$ denote the set of relations with in-degree as 0 in $\mathcal{H}$ . Suppose we are given a weak-q-hierarchical CQ $Q$ and a pattern $\sigma$ of update sequences,to which no simplification step can be applied. Moreover, $Q$ is weak-q-hierarchical but does not have a free-connex join tree compatible with $\sigma$ . Below,we prove Lemma 4.8 through two steps: (1) the ${\mathcal{E}}_{ \circ  }$ -induced CQ is not q-hierarchical (as shown in Lemma 4.12); and (2) $Q$ cannot be maintained in $O\left( {\sqrt{\left| D\right| }}^{1 - \epsilon }\right)$ time over $\sigma$ -sequences for any $\epsilon  > 0$ (as shown in Lemma 4.14).

通过建立初步认知后，我们转向一般情况。首先为弱q层次化CQ$\mathcal{Q}$和更新序列模式$\sigma$引入查询-更新图$\mathcal{H}$的概念。设${\mathcal{E}}_{ * }$是$\mathcal{Q}$的骨架结构。在（有向）查询-更新图$\mathcal{H}$中，每个关系$e \in  \mathcal{E}$作为节点，当满足以下条件时存在从${e}^{\prime }$到$e$的边：(1)$e \in  \mathcal{E} - {\mathcal{E}}_{ * }$，(2)${e}^{\prime }$是$e$的锚点，(3)$\sigma \left( {\{ e\} }\right)$是仅插入的或$\sigma \left( \left\{  {e,{e}^{\prime }}\right\}  \right)$是FIFO的。用${\mathcal{E}}_{ \circ  }$表示$\mathcal{H}$中入度为0的关系集合。假设给定一个弱q层次化CQ$Q$和无法应用简化步骤的更新序列模式$\sigma$，且$Q$虽弱q层次化但不具备与$\sigma$兼容的自由连接树。下文通过两个步骤证明引理4.8：(1)由${\mathcal{E}}_{ \circ  }$导出的CQ不具备q层次性（如引理4.12所示）；(2)对于任意$\epsilon  > 0$，$Q$无法在$\sigma$序列上以$O\left( {\sqrt{\left| D\right| }}^{1 - \epsilon }\right)$时间复杂度维护（如引理4.14所示）。

LEMMA 4.12. ${\mathcal{E}}_{ \circ  }$ -induced ${CQ}$ is not $q$ -hierarchical.

引理4.12. 由${\mathcal{E}}_{ \circ  }$导出的${CQ}$不具备$q$层次性。

We prove Lemma 4.12 by contradiction. More specifically,if ${\mathcal{E}}_{ \circ  }$ -induced CQ is q-hierarchical,then $Q$ has a free-connex join tree compatible with $\sigma$ ,leading to contradiction. We present a procedure in Algorithm 3 to construct a free-connex compatible join tree with $\sigma$ for $Q$ . We begin by building a height-1 free-connex join tree $\mathcal{T}$ for ${\mathcal{E}}_{ \circ  }$ (lines 2-3),which is always feasible from Lemma 2.2. Next, we incrementally add relations from $\mathcal{E} - {\mathcal{E}}_{ \circ  }$ to $\mathcal{T}$ . Initially,all relations are non-visited (line 4). We first visit an arbitrary relation $e \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{ \circ  }$ (lines 6-7) and then explore all non-visited nodes reachable from $e$ in $\mathcal{H}$ (lines 8-12). If a node ${e}^{\prime }$ is visited for the first time from ${e}^{\prime \prime }$ ,we add ${e}^{\prime }$ as a child of ${e}^{\prime \prime }$ . Once all nodes reachable from $e$ have been visited,we proceed to the next unvisited relation in ${\mathcal{E}}_{ \circ  }$ , repeating this process until all nodes in ${\mathcal{E}}_{ \circ  }$ have been traversed. For each $e \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{ \circ  }$ ,the subtree of $\mathcal{T}$ rooted at $e$ satisfies the following condition: for any node ${e}^{\prime } \in  \mathcal{E} - \{ e\}$ ,its parent node ${e}^{\prime \prime }$ ,must be an anchor of ${e}^{\prime }$ ,and either $\sigma \left( \left\{  {e}^{\prime }\right\}  \right)$ is insertion-only,or $\sigma \left( \left\{  {{e}^{\prime },{e}^{\prime \prime }}\right\}  \right)$ is FIFO. Consequently, $\mathcal{T}$ is a free-connex join tree compatible with $\sigma$ .

我们通过反证法证明引理4.12。具体而言，若${\mathcal{E}}_{ \circ  }$导出的CQ是q-分层(q-hierarchical)的，则$Q$必然存在与$\sigma$兼容的自由连接树(free-connex join tree)，从而导致矛盾。算法3展示了为$Q$构建$\sigma$兼容自由连接树的过程：首先根据引理2.2构建${\mathcal{E}}_{ \circ  }$的高度为1的自由连接树$\mathcal{T}$(第2-3行)，随后从$\mathcal{E} - {\mathcal{E}}_{ \circ  }$逐步向$\mathcal{T}$添加关系。初始化时所有关系标记为未访问(第4行)，先随机访问一个关系$e \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{ \circ  }$(第6-7行)，然后探索$\mathcal{H}$中从$e$可达的所有未访问节点(第8-12行)。当节点${e}^{\prime }$首次从${e}^{\prime \prime }$被访问时，将其添加为${e}^{\prime \prime }$的子节点。完成$e$可达节点的遍历后，继续处理${\mathcal{E}}_{ \circ  }$中下一个未访问关系，直至遍历所有节点。对于每个$e \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{ \circ  }$，以$e$为根的$\mathcal{T}$子树满足：任意节点${e}^{\prime } \in  \mathcal{E} - \{ e\}$的父节点${e}^{\prime \prime }$必须是${e}^{\prime }$的锚点，且$\sigma \left( \left\{  {e}^{\prime }\right\}  \right)$为仅插入类型或$\sigma \left( \left\{  {{e}^{\prime },{e}^{\prime \prime }}\right\}  \right)$为FIFO类型。因此$\mathcal{T}$构成与$\sigma$兼容的自由连接树。

Example 4.13. Figure 16 illustrates the query-update graph of ${\mathcal{Q}}_{4}$ from Example 4.2. ${\mathcal{E}}_{ \circ  } =$ $\left\{  {{R}_{1},{R}_{5},{R}_{8},{R}_{9}}\right\}$ induces a q-hierarchical CQ ${R}_{1}\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{2},{x}_{3}}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{5}\left( {{x}_{2},{x}_{3}}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{8}\left( {{x}_{3},{x}_{8}}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{9}\left( {{x}_{3},{x}_{9}}\right)$ . We first construct a height-1 free-connex join tree for ${\mathcal{E}}_{ \circ  }$ ,shown in Figure 17. As we traverse $\mathcal{H}$ ,we incrementally add relations: ${R}_{2}$ and ${R}_{3}$ are attached as child nodes of ${R}_{1}$ ,while ${R}_{6}$ and ${R}_{7}$ are added as two child nodes of ${R}_{5}$ . The final free-connex join tree for $Q$ is depicted in Figure 9 .

示例4.13 图16展示了例4.2中${\mathcal{Q}}_{4}$的查询-更新图。${\mathcal{E}}_{ \circ  } =$$\left\{  {{R}_{1},{R}_{5},{R}_{8},{R}_{9}}\right\}$导出了q-分层CQ${R}_{1}\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{2},{x}_{3}}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{5}\left( {{x}_{2},{x}_{3}}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{8}\left( {{x}_{3},{x}_{8}}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{9}\left( {{x}_{3},{x}_{9}}\right)$。我们首先构建${\mathcal{E}}_{ \circ  }$的高度为1自由连接树(图17)，遍历$\mathcal{H}$时逐步添加关系：将${R}_{2}$和${R}_{3}$附加为${R}_{1}$的子节点，${R}_{6}$与${R}_{7}$则作为${R}_{5}$的两个子节点。最终为$Q$构建的自由连接树如图9所示。

LEMMA 4.14. If ${\mathcal{E}}_{ \circ  }$ -induced ${CQ}$ is not $q$ -hierarchical, $Q$ cannot be maintained in $O\left( {\sqrt{\left| D\right| }}^{1 - \epsilon }\right)$ time over $\sigma$ -sequences for any $\epsilon  > 0$ .

引理4.14 若${\mathcal{E}}_{ \circ  }$导出的${CQ}$不满足$q$-分层特性，则对于任意$\epsilon  > 0$，$Q$无法在$\sigma$序列上以$O\left( {\sqrt{\left| D\right| }}^{1 - \epsilon }\right)$时间复杂度维护。

LEMMA 4.15 ([7]). Any non-q-hierarchical ${CQQ} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E},\mathbf{y}}\right)$ must contain one of the following structures:

引理4.15（文献[7]）：任何非q-分层结构${CQQ} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E},\mathbf{y}}\right)$必包含以下结构之一：

- (q-core) relations ${e}_{1},{e}_{2} \in  \mathcal{E}$ and attributes ${x}_{1} \in  \mathbf{y} \cap  \left( {{e}_{1} - {e}_{2}}\right) ,{x}_{2} \in  \overline{\mathbf{y}} \cap  {e}_{1} \cap  {e}_{2}$ .

- (q-核心) 关系${e}_{1},{e}_{2} \in  \mathcal{E}$与属性${x}_{1} \in  \mathbf{y} \cap  \left( {{e}_{1} - {e}_{2}}\right) ,{x}_{2} \in  \overline{\mathbf{y}} \cap  {e}_{1} \cap  {e}_{2}$。

- (hierarchical core) relations ${e}_{1},{e}_{2},{e}_{3} \in  \mathcal{E}$ and attributes ${x}_{1} \in  \left( {{e}_{2} - {e}_{3}}\right)  \cap  {e}_{1},{x}_{2} \in  \left( {{e}_{3} - {e}_{2}}\right)  \cap  {e}_{1}$ .

- (层次化核心) 关系${e}_{1},{e}_{2},{e}_{3} \in  \mathcal{E}$与属性${x}_{1} \in  \left( {{e}_{2} - {e}_{3}}\right)  \cap  {e}_{1},{x}_{2} \in  \left( {{e}_{3} - {e}_{2}}\right)  \cap  {e}_{1}$。

Proof of Lemma 4.14. As the ${\mathcal{E}}_{ \circ  }$ -induced CQ is not q-hierarchical,it contains either a q-core or a hierarchical core by Lemma 4.15. We first assume a q-core with relations ${e}_{1},{e}_{2}$ and attributes ${x}_{1} \in  \mathbf{y} \cap  \left( {{e}_{1} - {e}_{2}}\right) ,{x}_{2} \in  \overline{\mathbf{y}} \cap  {e}_{1} \cap  {e}_{2}$ . As ${Q}_{ * }$ is q-hierarchical,the case with ${e}_{1},{e}_{2} \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{ * }$ cannot happen. We further distinguish the following three cases:

引理4.14的证明。由于${\mathcal{E}}_{ \circ  }$导出的CQ不具备q-层次性，根据引理4.15，它必然包含q-核心或层次化核心。我们首先假设存在具有关系${e}_{1},{e}_{2}$和属性${x}_{1} \in  \mathbf{y} \cap  \left( {{e}_{1} - {e}_{2}}\right) ,{x}_{2} \in  \overline{\mathbf{y}} \cap  {e}_{1} \cap  {e}_{2}$的q-核心。由于${Q}_{ * }$是q-层次化的，${e}_{1},{e}_{2} \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{ * }$的情况不可能发生。我们进一步区分以下三种情形：

- Case 1.1: ${e}_{1} \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{ * }$ and ${e}_{2} \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{ \circ  } - {\mathcal{E}}_{ * }$ . There must exist a relation ${e}_{3} \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{ * }$ that is an anchor of ${e}_{2}$ and contains both attributes ${x}_{1},{x}_{2}$ . If no such relation exists,for any anchor ${e}_{3} \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{ \bullet  }$ of ${e}_{2}$ ,we have ${x}_{2} \in  {e}_{1} - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } \subseteq  {e}_{3}$ and therefore ${x}_{1} \in  {e}_{1} - {e}_{3}$ ,which together forms a q-core with relations ${e}_{1},{e}_{3}$ and attributes ${x}_{1},{x}_{2}$ in ${Q}_{ * }$ . This contradicts the fact that ${Q}_{ * }$ is q-hierarchical. After finding such a relation ${e}_{3}$ with ${x}_{1},{x}_{2} \in  {e}_{3}$ ,we identify another q -core with relations ${e}_{2},{e}_{3}$ and attributes ${x}_{1} \in  \mathbf{y} \cap  \left( {{e}_{3} - {e}_{2}}\right) ,{x}_{2} \in  \overline{\mathbf{y}} \cap  {e}_{2} \cap  {e}_{3}$ . As there is no edge between ${e}_{2},{e}_{3}$ in $\mathcal{H},\sigma \left( \left\{  {e}_{2}\right\}  \right)$ is not insertion-only and $\sigma \left( \left\{  {{e}_{2},{e}_{3}}\right\}  \right)$ is not FIFO. The hardness follows Example 4.9.

- 情形1.1：${e}_{1} \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{ * }$与${e}_{2} \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{ \circ  } - {\mathcal{E}}_{ * }$。必存在一个作为${e}_{2}$锚点的关系${e}_{3} \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{ * }$，且该关系同时包含属性${x}_{1},{x}_{2}$。若无此关系，则对于${e}_{2}$的任意锚点${e}_{3} \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{ \bullet  }$，我们有${x}_{2} \in  {e}_{1} - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } \subseteq  {e}_{3}$从而得到${x}_{1} \in  {e}_{1} - {e}_{3}$，这将与关系${e}_{1},{e}_{3}$及${Q}_{ * }$中的属性${x}_{1},{x}_{2}$构成q-核心。这与${Q}_{ * }$的q-层次性矛盾。找到满足${x}_{1},{x}_{2} \in  {e}_{3}$的关系${e}_{3}$后，我们可识别出另一个具有关系${e}_{2},{e}_{3}$和属性${x}_{1} \in  \mathbf{y} \cap  \left( {{e}_{3} - {e}_{2}}\right) ,{x}_{2} \in  \overline{\mathbf{y}} \cap  {e}_{2} \cap  {e}_{3}$的q-核心。由于${e}_{2},{e}_{3}$在$\mathcal{H},\sigma \left( \left\{  {e}_{2}\right\}  \right)$中无连边，故$\mathcal{H},\sigma \left( \left\{  {e}_{2}\right\}  \right)$非仅插入型且$\sigma \left( \left\{  {{e}_{2},{e}_{3}}\right\}  \right)$非FIFO。其困难性可参照例4.9。

- Case 1.2: ${e}_{1},{e}_{2} \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{ \circ  } - {\mathcal{E}}_{ \bullet  }$ . For any anchor ${e}_{3} \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{ \bullet  }$ of ${e}_{1}$ ,as ${e}_{1}$ is reducible by ${e}_{3},{x}_{2} \in  {e}_{1} - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \circ  } \subseteq  {e}_{3}$ . Moreover, ${x}_{2} \in  \overline{\mathbf{y}}$ ,hence ${x}_{1} \in  \mathbf{y} \cap  {e}_{1} \subseteq  {e}_{3}$ . We can apply the same argument as Case 11. with relations ${e}_{2},{e}_{3}$ and attributes ${x}_{1},{x}_{2}$ .

- 案例1.2：${e}_{1},{e}_{2} \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{ \circ  } - {\mathcal{E}}_{ \bullet  }$。对于${e}_{1}$的任意锚点${e}_{3} \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{ \bullet  }$，由于${e}_{1}$可通过${e}_{3},{x}_{2} \in  {e}_{1} - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \circ  } \subseteq  {e}_{3}$约简。此外，${x}_{2} \in  \overline{\mathbf{y}}$，因此${x}_{1} \in  \mathbf{y} \cap  {e}_{1} \subseteq  {e}_{3}$。我们可以采用与案例11相同的论证方法，结合关系${e}_{2},{e}_{3}$与属性${x}_{1},{x}_{2}$。

- Case 1.3: ${e}_{1} \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{ \circ  } - {\mathcal{E}}_{ \bullet  }$ and ${e}_{2} \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{ \bullet  }$ . By applying the same argument as Case 1.1,we can identify an anchor ${e}_{3} \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{ * }$ of ${e}_{1}$ with ${x}_{1},{x}_{2} \in  {e}_{3}$ . Now,we have identified a q-core in ${Q}_{ * }$ with relation ${e}_{3},{e}_{2}$ and attributes ${x}_{1},{x}_{2}$ ,contradicting the fact that ${Q}_{ * }$ is q-hierarchical.

- 案例1.3：${e}_{1} \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{ \circ  } - {\mathcal{E}}_{ \bullet  }$和${e}_{2} \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{ \bullet  }$。通过应用与案例1.1相同的论证，我们可识别${e}_{1}$中具有${x}_{1},{x}_{2} \in  {e}_{3}$特性的锚点${e}_{3} \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{ * }$。此时，我们在${Q}_{ * }$中发现了一个具有关系${e}_{3},{e}_{2}$和属性${x}_{1},{x}_{2}$的q核，这与${Q}_{ * }$具有q分层性质的事实相矛盾。

Alternatively,we assume a hierarchical core in the ${\mathcal{E}}_{ \circ  }$ -induced CQ with relations ${e}_{1},{e}_{2},{e}_{3}$ and attributes ${x}_{1} \in  \left( {{e}_{2} - {e}_{3}}\right)  \cap  {e}_{1},{x}_{2} \in  \left( {{e}_{3} - {e}_{2}}\right)  \cap  {e}_{1}$ . If ${e}_{1} \notin  {\mathcal{E}}_{ * }$ ,we find an arbitrary anchor ${e}_{1}^{\prime } \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{ * }$ of ${e}_{1}$ . As ${e}_{1}$ is reducible by ${e}_{1}^{\prime }$ ,we have ${e}_{1} - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } \subseteq  {e}_{1}^{\prime }$ and furthermore ${x}_{1},{x}_{2} \in  {e}_{1}^{\prime }$ . We replace ${e}_{1}$ with ${e}_{1}^{\prime }$ . As ${Q}_{ * }$ is q-hierarchical,the case with ${e}_{2},{e}_{3} \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{ * }$ cannot happen. We further distinguish two cases:

另一种情况是，我们假设在${\mathcal{E}}_{ \circ  }$诱导的CQ中存在一个分层核，其包含关系${e}_{1},{e}_{2},{e}_{3}$和属性${x}_{1} \in  \left( {{e}_{2} - {e}_{3}}\right)  \cap  {e}_{1},{x}_{2} \in  \left( {{e}_{3} - {e}_{2}}\right)  \cap  {e}_{1}$。若${e}_{1} \notin  {\mathcal{E}}_{ * }$成立，则可找到${e}_{1}$的任意锚点${e}_{1}^{\prime } \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{ * }$。由于${e}_{1}$可通过${e}_{1}^{\prime }$约简，故有${e}_{1} - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } \subseteq  {e}_{1}^{\prime }$且进一步得到${x}_{1},{x}_{2} \in  {e}_{1}^{\prime }$。我们将${e}_{1}$替换为${e}_{1}^{\prime }$。鉴于${Q}_{ * }$具有q分层特性，${e}_{2},{e}_{3} \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{ * }$的情况不可能发生。我们进一步区分两种子情况：

- Case 2.1: ${e}_{2} \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{ \circ  } - {\mathcal{E}}_{ \bullet  }$ and ${e}_{3} \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{ \bullet  }$ (the case with ${e}_{3} \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{ \circ  } - {\mathcal{E}}_{ \bullet  }$ and ${e}_{2} \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{ \bullet  }$ is symmetric). As ${e}_{1},{e}_{3} \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{ * }$ ,we must have ${e}_{3} - {e}_{1} \neq  \varnothing$ . We can always identify an attribute ${x}_{3} \in  {e}_{3} - {e}_{1}$ satisfying the following two properties:

- 案例2.1：${e}_{2} \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{ \circ  } - {\mathcal{E}}_{ \bullet  }$且${e}_{3} \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{ \bullet  }$（${e}_{3} \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{ \circ  } - {\mathcal{E}}_{ \bullet  }$与${e}_{2} \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{ \bullet  }$的情况对称）。由于${e}_{1},{e}_{3} \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{ * }$，必然存在${e}_{3} - {e}_{1} \neq  \varnothing$。我们总能找到一个满足以下两个特性的属性${x}_{3} \in  {e}_{3} - {e}_{1}$：

- ${\mathcal{E}}_{{x}_{3}} - {\mathcal{E}}_{ \bullet  } \neq  \varnothing$ : By contradiction,assume each attribute ${x}_{3} \in  {e}_{3} - {e}_{1}$ satisfying ${\mathcal{E}}_{{x}_{3}} - {\mathcal{E}}_{ \bullet  } = \varnothing$ , which must be a joint attribute. For each such attribute ${x}_{3} \in  {e}_{3} - {e}_{1} - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  }$ ,as ${\mathcal{E}}_{{x}_{3}} \subseteq  {\mathcal{E}}_{ \bullet  }$ ,there exists a relation ${e}_{{x}_{3}}^{\prime } \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{ * }$ such that ${x}_{3} \subseteq  {e}_{{x}_{3}}^{\prime }$ . Now,if there exists a pair of ${x}_{3},{x}_{4} \in  {e}_{3} - {e}_{1} - {\mathcal{V}}_{ * }$ such that ${e}_{{x}_{3}}^{\prime } \neq  {e}_{{x}_{4}}^{\prime }$ ,we have ${e}_{3} \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{{x}_{3}} \cap  {\mathcal{E}}_{{x}_{4}} \cap  {\mathcal{E}}_{ \bullet  } \neq  \varnothing ,{e}_{{x}_{3}}^{\prime } \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{{x}_{3}} \cap  {\mathcal{E}}_{ \bullet  } - {\mathcal{E}}_{{x}_{4}}$ and ${e}_{{x}_{4}}^{\prime } \in$ ${\mathcal{E}}_{{x}_{4}} \cap  {\mathcal{E}}_{ * } - {\mathcal{E}}_{{x}_{3}}$ ,contradicting the fact that ${Q}_{ * }$ is q-hierarchical. Hence,there is a relation ${e}^{\prime }$ such that ${e}_{3} - {e}_{1} - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } \subseteq  {e}^{\prime }$ . For each attribute ${x}_{5} \in  {e}_{3} \cap  {e}_{1},{x}_{5} \in  {e}^{\prime }$ must hold; otherwise,we have ${e}_{3} \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{{x}_{5}} \cap  {\mathcal{E}}_{{x}_{3}} \cap  {\mathcal{E}}_{ \bullet  } \neq  \varnothing ,{e}^{\prime } \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{{x}_{3}} \cap  {\mathcal{E}}_{ \bullet  } - {\mathcal{E}}_{{x}_{5}}$ and ${e}_{1} \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{{x}_{5}} \cap  {\mathcal{E}}_{ \bullet  } - {\mathcal{E}}_{{x}_{3}}$ ,again contradicting the fact that ${Q}_{ \bullet  }$ is q-hierarchical. Hence, ${e}_{3} - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } \subseteq  {e}^{\prime }$ ,i.e., ${e}_{3}$ is reducible,contradicting that fact that ${e}_{3} \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{ * }$ .

- ${\mathcal{E}}_{{x}_{3}} - {\mathcal{E}}_{ \bullet  } \neq  \varnothing$ ：采用反证法，假设每个满足${\mathcal{E}}_{{x}_{3}} - {\mathcal{E}}_{ \bullet  } = \varnothing$的属性${x}_{3} \in  {e}_{3} - {e}_{1}$都必须是联合属性。对于每个这样的属性${x}_{3} \in  {e}_{3} - {e}_{1} - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  }$，由于${\mathcal{E}}_{{x}_{3}} \subseteq  {\mathcal{E}}_{ \bullet  }$，必然存在关系${e}_{{x}_{3}}^{\prime } \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{ * }$使得${x}_{3} \subseteq  {e}_{{x}_{3}}^{\prime }$。若存在${x}_{3},{x}_{4} \in  {e}_{3} - {e}_{1} - {\mathcal{V}}_{ * }$对使得${e}_{{x}_{3}}^{\prime } \neq  {e}_{{x}_{4}}^{\prime }$，则会导致${e}_{3} \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{{x}_{3}} \cap  {\mathcal{E}}_{{x}_{4}} \cap  {\mathcal{E}}_{ \bullet  } \neq  \varnothing ,{e}_{{x}_{3}}^{\prime } \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{{x}_{3}} \cap  {\mathcal{E}}_{ \bullet  } - {\mathcal{E}}_{{x}_{4}}$与${e}_{{x}_{4}}^{\prime } \in$${\mathcal{E}}_{{x}_{4}} \cap  {\mathcal{E}}_{ * } - {\mathcal{E}}_{{x}_{3}}$，这与${Q}_{ * }$具有q-层次性（q-hierarchical）的事实矛盾。因此存在关系${e}^{\prime }$满足${e}_{3} - {e}_{1} - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } \subseteq  {e}^{\prime }$。对于每个属性${x}_{5} \in  {e}_{3} \cap  {e}_{1},{x}_{5} \in  {e}^{\prime }$必须成立；否则将产生${e}_{3} \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{{x}_{5}} \cap  {\mathcal{E}}_{{x}_{3}} \cap  {\mathcal{E}}_{ \bullet  } \neq  \varnothing ,{e}^{\prime } \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{{x}_{3}} \cap  {\mathcal{E}}_{ \bullet  } - {\mathcal{E}}_{{x}_{5}}$和${e}_{1} \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{{x}_{5}} \cap  {\mathcal{E}}_{ \bullet  } - {\mathcal{E}}_{{x}_{3}}$，再次与${Q}_{ \bullet  }$的q-层次性矛盾。故${e}_{3} - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } \subseteq  {e}^{\prime }$，即${e}_{3}$可约（reducible），这与${e}_{3} \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{ * }$的事实相矛盾。

- ${\mathcal{E}}_{{x}_{1}} \cap  {\mathcal{E}}_{{x}_{3}} = \varnothing$ : Suppose there exists any $e \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{ \bullet  } \cap  {\mathcal{E}}_{{x}_{1}} \cap  {\mathcal{E}}_{{x}_{3}}$ ,then we identify a hierarchical core with relations ${e}_{1},{e}_{3},e$ and attributes ${x}_{1} \in  \left( {{e}_{1} - {e}_{3}}\right)  \cap  e,{x}_{2} \in  \left( {{e}_{3} - {e}_{1}}\right)  \cap  e$ ,contradicting the fact that ${Q}_{ * }$ is q-hierarchical. Otherwise,suppose there exists some $e \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{ \circ  } - {\mathcal{E}}_{ * } \cap  {\mathcal{E}}_{{x}_{1}} \cap  {\mathcal{E}}_{{x}_{3}}$ . For any anchor ${e}^{\prime } \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{ * }$ of $e,{x}_{1},{x}_{3} \in  e - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } \subseteq  {e}^{\prime }$ . However, ${\mathcal{E}}_{ \bullet  } \cap  {\mathcal{E}}_{{x}_{1}} \cap  {\mathcal{E}}_{{x}_{3}} = \varnothing$ by our assumption. Hence, $e$ does not have an anchor,violating the condition that $e$ is reducible.

- ${\mathcal{E}}_{{x}_{1}} \cap  {\mathcal{E}}_{{x}_{3}} = \varnothing$：假设存在任意$e \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{ \bullet  } \cap  {\mathcal{E}}_{{x}_{1}} \cap  {\mathcal{E}}_{{x}_{3}}$，则可识别出一个具有${e}_{1},{e}_{3},e$关系和${x}_{1} \in  \left( {{e}_{1} - {e}_{3}}\right)  \cap  e,{x}_{2} \in  \left( {{e}_{3} - {e}_{1}}\right)  \cap  e$属性的层级核心，这与${Q}_{ * }$为q-层级（q-hierarchical）的事实相矛盾。否则，假设存在某个$e \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{ \circ  } - {\mathcal{E}}_{ * } \cap  {\mathcal{E}}_{{x}_{1}} \cap  {\mathcal{E}}_{{x}_{3}}$。对于$e,{x}_{1},{x}_{3} \in  e - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } \subseteq  {e}^{\prime }$的任何锚点${e}^{\prime } \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{ * }$而言，根据我们的假设，${\mathcal{E}}_{ \bullet  } \cap  {\mathcal{E}}_{{x}_{1}} \cap  {\mathcal{E}}_{{x}_{3}} = \varnothing$成立。因此，$e$不存在锚点，这违反了$e$可约（reducible）的条件。

Let ${e}_{4} \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{ \circ  } - {\mathcal{E}}_{ \bullet  } \cap  {\mathcal{E}}_{{x}_{3}}$ be any relation. We can reduce ${Q}_{\text{swing }}$ to $Q$ with attributes ${x}_{1},{x}_{2},{x}_{3}$ and relations ${e}_{1},{e}_{2},{e}_{3},{e}_{4}$ . As there is no edges $\left( {{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}\right)$ and $\left( {{e}_{3},{e}_{4}}\right)$ in $\mathcal{H}$ ,either $\sigma \left( \left\{  {e}_{1}\right\}  \right)$ or $\sigma \left( \left\{  {e}_{4}\right\}  \right)$ is not insertion-only,and $\sigma \left( \left\{  {{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}\right\}  \right)$ and $\sigma \left( \left\{  {{e}_{3},{e}_{4}}\right\}  \right)$ are not FIFO. The hardness follows Example 4.11.

设${e}_{4} \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{ \circ  } - {\mathcal{E}}_{ \bullet  } \cap  {\mathcal{E}}_{{x}_{3}}$为任意关系。我们可以将${Q}_{\text{swing }}$归约至具有${x}_{1},{x}_{2},{x}_{3}$属性和${e}_{1},{e}_{2},{e}_{3},{e}_{4}$关系的$Q$。由于$\mathcal{H}$中不存在$\left( {{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}\right)$和$\left( {{e}_{3},{e}_{4}}\right)$边，故$\sigma \left( \left\{  {e}_{1}\right\}  \right)$或$\sigma \left( \left\{  {e}_{4}\right\}  \right)$非仅插入（insertion-only），且$\sigma \left( \left\{  {{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}\right\}  \right)$与$\sigma \left( \left\{  {{e}_{3},{e}_{4}}\right\}  \right)$不满足先进先出（FIFO）。其难度如例4.11所示。

- Case 2.2: ${e}_{2},{e}_{3} \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{ \circ  } - {\mathcal{E}}_{ \bullet  }$ . Let ${e}_{4} \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{ \bullet  }$ be an arbitrary anchor of ${e}_{2}$ and ${e}_{5} \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{ \bullet  }$ be an arbitrary anchor of ${e}_{3}$ . Hence, ${x}_{1} \in  {e}_{2} - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } \subseteq  {e}_{4}$ and ${x}_{2} \in  {e}_{3} - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } \subseteq  {e}_{5}$ . Additionally,either ${x}_{2} \in  {e}_{4}$ or ${x}_{1} \in  {e}_{5}$ ; otherwise,we identify a hierarchical core,contradicting the fact that ${Q}_{ * }$ is q-hierarchical. Moreover,if ${x}_{2} \in  {e}_{4}$ but ${x}_{1} \notin  {e}_{5}$ (the case with ${x}_{1} \in  {e}_{5}$ but ${x}_{2} \notin  {e}_{4}$ is symmetric),we can reduce ${e}_{4},{e}_{2},{e}_{5}$ to Case 2.1. Below,we assume ${x}_{2} \in  {e}_{4},{x}_{1} \in  {e}_{5}$ ,and further distinguish the following two cases:

- 案例2.2：${e}_{2},{e}_{3} \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{ \circ  } - {\mathcal{E}}_{ \bullet  }$。设${e}_{4} \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{ \bullet  }$为${e}_{2}$的任意锚点，${e}_{5} \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{ \bullet  }$为${e}_{3}$的任意锚点。因此，${x}_{1} \in  {e}_{2} - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } \subseteq  {e}_{4}$且${x}_{2} \in  {e}_{3} - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } \subseteq  {e}_{5}$。此外，要么${x}_{2} \in  {e}_{4}$要么${x}_{1} \in  {e}_{5}$；否则我们将识别出一个层次化核心，这与${Q}_{ * }$具有q-层次性的事实相矛盾。更进一步，若${x}_{2} \in  {e}_{4}$但${x}_{1} \notin  {e}_{5}$（${x}_{1} \in  {e}_{5}$但${x}_{2} \notin  {e}_{4}$的情况对称），我们可以将${e}_{4},{e}_{2},{e}_{5}$归约为案例2.1。下文假定${x}_{2} \in  {e}_{4},{x}_{1} \in  {e}_{5}$，并进一步区分以下两种情况：

- Case 2.2.1: ${e}_{4} = {e}_{5}$ . In this case,we identify a hierarchical core ${e}_{4},{e}_{2},{e}_{3}$ . Here, $\sigma \left( \left\{  {e}_{2}\right\}  \right)$ and $\sigma \left( \left\{  {e}_{3}\right\}  \right)$ are not insertion-only,and $\sigma \left( \left\{  {{e}_{4},{e}_{2}}\right\}  \right)$ and $\sigma \left( \left\{  {{e}_{4},{e}_{3}}\right\}  \right)$ are not FIFO,as there is no edge between $\left( {{e}_{4},{e}_{2}}\right)$ and $\left( {{e}_{4},{e}_{3}}\right)$ in $\mathcal{H}$ . The hardness follows Example 4.10.

- 案例2.2.1：${e}_{4} = {e}_{5}$。此情形下，我们识别出层次化核心${e}_{4},{e}_{2},{e}_{3}$。其中$\sigma \left( \left\{  {e}_{2}\right\}  \right)$与$\sigma \left( \left\{  {e}_{3}\right\}  \right)$非仅插入型，且$\sigma \left( \left\{  {{e}_{4},{e}_{2}}\right\}  \right)$与$\sigma \left( \left\{  {{e}_{4},{e}_{3}}\right\}  \right)$非FIFO队列，因$\mathcal{H}$中$\left( {{e}_{4},{e}_{2}}\right)$与$\left( {{e}_{4},{e}_{3}}\right)$间无连边。其困难性参见示例4.10。

- Case 2.2.2: ${e}_{2},{e}_{3}$ don’t share a common anchor. We can find ${x}_{3} \in  {e}_{2} - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } - {e}_{5}$ and ${x}_{4} \in  {e}_{3} - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } -$ ${e}_{4}$ . We can reduce ${Q}_{\text{swing }}$ to $Q$ with attributes ${x}_{3},{x}_{1},{x}_{4}$ and relations ${e}_{2},{e}_{4},{e}_{5},{e}_{3}$ . As there is no edge $\left( {{e}_{4},{e}_{2}}\right)$ and $\left( {{e}_{5},{e}_{3}}\right)$ in $\mathcal{H}$ ,either $\sigma \left( \left\{  {e}_{2}\right\}  \right)$ or $\sigma \left( \left\{  {e}_{3}\right\}  \right)$ is not insertion-only,and $\sigma \left( \left\{  {{e}_{2},{e}_{4}}\right\}  \right)$ and $\sigma \left( \left\{  {{e}_{5},{e}_{3}}\right\}  \right)$ are not FIFO. The hardness follows Example 4.11.

- 案例2.2.2：${e}_{2},{e}_{3}$无共用锚点。我们可找到${x}_{3} \in  {e}_{2} - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } - {e}_{5}$与${x}_{4} \in  {e}_{3} - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } -$${e}_{4}$。可将${Q}_{\text{swing }}$归约为具有${x}_{3},{x}_{1},{x}_{4}$属性和${e}_{2},{e}_{4},{e}_{5},{e}_{3}$关系的$Q$。由于$\mathcal{H}$中$\left( {{e}_{4},{e}_{2}}\right)$与$\left( {{e}_{5},{e}_{3}}\right)$无连边，$\sigma \left( \left\{  {e}_{2}\right\}  \right)$或$\sigma \left( \left\{  {e}_{3}\right\}  \right)$非仅插入型，且$\sigma \left( \left\{  {{e}_{2},{e}_{4}}\right\}  \right)$与$\sigma \left( \left\{  {{e}_{5},{e}_{3}}\right\}  \right)$非FIFO队列。其困难性参见示例4.11。

### 4.4 Base Case 3: Line 7 of Algorithm 2

### 4.4 基础案例3：算法2第7行

LEMMA 4.16. For a CQQ and a pattern $\sigma$ of update sequences,to which no simplification step can be applied if $Q$ is weak-q-hierarchical and has a free-connex join tree $\mathcal{T}$ compatible with $\sigma ,Q$ can be maintained in $O\left( 1\right)$ amortized time over $\sigma$ -sequences.

引理4.16. 对于CQQ（连续查询）和更新序列模式$\sigma$，若$Q$是弱q分层且具有与$\sigma ,Q$兼容的自由连接树$\mathcal{T}$，则无法应用简化步骤时，可在$\sigma$序列上以$O\left( 1\right)$摊还时间维护。

In Appendix B,we show that for a $\mathrm{{CQ}}Q$ and a pattern $\sigma$ of update sequences,if $Q$ is weak-q-hierarchical and has a free-connex join tree $\mathcal{T}$ compatible with $\sigma$ ,any $\sigma$ -sequence $S$ has enclosure-ness $O\left( 1\right)$ under $\mathcal{T}$ . Together with Lemma 1.4,we complete the proof of Lemma 4.16.

附录B中我们证明：对于$\mathrm{{CQ}}Q$和更新序列模式$\sigma$，若$Q$是弱q分层且具有与$\sigma$兼容的自由连接树$\mathcal{T}$，则任何$\sigma$序列$S$在$\mathcal{T}$下都具有封闭性$O\left( 1\right)$。结合引理1.4，我们完成了引理4.16的证明。

## 5 CONCLUSION

## 5 结论

In this paper, we study the lower bounds of query maintenance over different classes of update sequences. There are a few exciting open questions in this direction:

本文研究了不同类别更新序列上查询维护的下界。该方向存在若干值得探索的开放性问题：

- Single update v.s. Batch update. So far, we have focused on the maintenance complexity for single-tuple updates. In practice, it is more likely that updates arrive in batch, and query answers are only required to be ready to be enumerated after each batch. A strawman approach is to drop the single-update assumption for updates and update sequences, then apply the current upper bounds. It would be very interesting to study the tradeoff between maintenance complexity, delay, and batch size beyond such an approach.

- 单次更新 vs 批量更新。目前我们聚焦于单元组更新的维护复杂度。实践中更常见批量更新到达，且仅需在每批处理后准备好查询结果枚举。初级方案是放弃单次更新假设，直接应用当前上界。研究维护复杂度、延迟与批量大小间的权衡将极具价值。

- Preprocessing. So far, we simulate the preprocessing steps with an insertion-only update sequence prior to time 0 . The absence of strict update ordering at the start allows for linear-time preprocessing for free-connex queries (also shown by [17]). For non-free-connex or cyclic queries, strategically assigning preprocessing order could improve performance, and exploring the corresponding upper and lower bounds would be an interesting direction for future work. - Better update-independent quantity beyond enclosureness. The current notion of enclosureness is sometimes too loose to capture the hardness of update sequences. In Appendix B, we have to slightly modify the data structure in [25] to derive a tighter analysis. It is open whether one can find a better quantity than enclosureness to characterize the hardness of query maintenance.

- 预处理。当前我们用时序0前的仅插入序列模拟预处理步骤。起始阶段无严格更新顺序允许自由连接查询的线性时间预处理（如文献[17]所示）。对于非自由连接或循环查询，策略性分配预处理顺序可提升性能，探索相应上下界是未来工作的有趣方向。- 超越封闭性的更新无关量。现有封闭性概念有时过于宽松，附录B中我们需微调文献[25]数据结构以获得更严密分析。能否找到比封闭性更好的量来描述查询维护难度仍是开放问题。

- The effect of self-joins. It has been observed [10] in static query enumeration that self-joins will change the existing complexity results established for a long time. As mentioned, there is a natural connection between static query enumeration and query maintenance under insertion-only updates. In general, it remains to investigate how self-joins would change the problem hardness of query maintenance.

- 自连接效应。静态查询枚举中已观察到[10]自连接会改变长期建立的复杂度结论。如前所述，静态查询枚举与仅插入更新的查询维护存在自然关联。自连接如何改变查询维护问题难度仍需深入研究。
