## A MISSING PROOFS IN SECTION 3

## 附录A 第3节缺失证明

LEMMA A.1. For a free-connex but non-weak-q-hierarchical ${CQ}\mathcal{Q} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E},\mathbf{y}}\right)$ with a skeleton ${\mathcal{E}}_{ * }$ , if the ${\mathcal{E}}_{ * }$ -induced ${CQ}$ contains a $q$ -core,then $Q$ must contain a hook core.

引理A.1. 对于具有骨架${\mathcal{E}}_{ * }$的自由连接但非弱q分层${CQ}\mathcal{Q} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E},\mathbf{y}}\right)$，若${\mathcal{E}}_{ * }$导出的${CQ}$包含$q$核，则$Q$必包含钩核。

Proof of Lemma A.1. By definition,there must exist a pair of distinct attributes ${x}_{1} \in  \mathbf{y},{x}_{2} \in  \overline{\mathbf{y}}$ and a pair of distinct relations ${e}_{1},{e}_{2} \subseteq  {\mathcal{E}}_{ * }$ such that ${x}_{1} \in  {e}_{1} - {e}_{2}$ and ${x}_{2} \in  {e}_{1} \cap  {e}_{2}$ . Consider an arbitrary free-connex tree $\mathcal{T}$ for $Q$ ,whose internal (original) nodes are ${\mathcal{E}}_{ * }$ . Let ${\mathcal{E}}_{\text{con }}$ be the connex subtree of $\mathcal{T}$ . We first show that ${e}_{2}$ cannot be the ancestor of ${e}_{1}$ . If this is the case,all nodes containing ${x}_{1}$ must be a subtree of ${x}_{2}$ . Let $e$ be the child node of ${e}_{2}$ lying on the path from ${e}_{2}$ to ${e}_{1}$ . Hence, ${e}_{2},e \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{\text{con }}$ ; otherwise,no node in ${\mathcal{E}}_{\text{con }}$ contains ${x}_{1}$ . However, ${x}_{2} \in  {e}_{2} \cap  e = \operatorname{key}\left( e\right)  \notin  \mathbf{y}$ , coming to a contradiction of the definition of ${\mathcal{E}}_{\text{con }}$ . Let ${e}_{5}$ be the parent node of ${e}_{2}$ . We claim that ${x}_{2} \in  {e}_{5}$ ; otherwise, ${e}_{1}$ must be a descendent of ${e}_{2}$ ,contradicting our observation above. Also, ${x}_{1} \in  {e}_{5}$ ; otherwise,the connect property of ${x}_{1}$ does not hold. As ${e}_{2} \cap  {e}_{5} \cap  \overline{\mathbf{y}} \neq  \varnothing$ ,then ${e}_{2} \notin  {\mathcal{E}}_{\text{con }}$ . Then, $\left( {{e}_{2} - {e}_{5}}\right)  \cap  \mathbf{y} = \varnothing$ ; otherwise,the connect property of any output attribute $x \in  \left( {{e}_{2} - {e}_{5}}\right)  \cap  \mathbf{y}$ does not hold. As ${e}_{2} \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{ * }$ and $\left( {{e}_{2} - {e}_{5}}\right)  \cap  \mathbf{y} = \varnothing$ ,we must have $\left( {{e}_{2} - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } - {e}_{5}}\right)  \cap  \overline{\mathbf{y}} \neq  \varnothing$ ; otherwise, ${e}_{2}$ can be removed from ${\mathcal{E}}_{ * }$ ,due to the existence of ${e}_{5}$ . Let ${x}_{3} \in  \left( {{e}_{2} - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } - {e}_{5}}\right)  \cap  \overline{\mathbf{y}}$ be an arbitrary non-output attribute. As ${x}_{3} \notin  {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  }$ ,there must exist a child node of ${e}_{2}$ say ${e}_{3}$ in $\mathcal{T}$ ,such that ${x}_{3} \in  {e}_{2} \cap  {e}_{3}$ . As ${x}_{1} \notin  {e}_{2},{x}_{1} \notin  {e}_{3}$ . As ${x}_{3} \notin  {e}_{5},{x}_{3} \notin  {e}_{1}$ . Also,there exists no relation containing both ${x}_{1},{x}_{3}$ . Hence,we have identified a hook core with ${x}_{1},{x}_{2},{x}_{3}$ and ${e}_{1},{e}_{2},{e}_{3}$ as desired.

引理A.1的证明。根据定义，必然存在一对不同的属性${x}_{1} \in  \mathbf{y},{x}_{2} \in  \overline{\mathbf{y}}$和一对不同的关系${e}_{1},{e}_{2} \subseteq  {\mathcal{E}}_{ * }$，使得${x}_{1} \in  {e}_{1} - {e}_{2}$且${x}_{2} \in  {e}_{1} \cap  {e}_{2}$。考虑$Q$的任意自由连接树$\mathcal{T}$，其内部（原始）节点为${\mathcal{E}}_{ * }$。令${\mathcal{E}}_{\text{con }}$为$\mathcal{T}$的连接子树。我们首先证明${e}_{2}$不可能是${e}_{1}$的祖先节点。若成立，则所有包含${x}_{1}$的节点都必须是${x}_{2}$的子树。设$e$为${e}_{2}$位于到${e}_{1}$路径上的子节点。因此，${e}_{2},e \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{\text{con }}$；否则${\mathcal{E}}_{\text{con }}$中无节点包含${x}_{1}$。然而${x}_{2} \in  {e}_{2} \cap  e = \operatorname{key}\left( e\right)  \notin  \mathbf{y}$，这与${\mathcal{E}}_{\text{con }}$的定义矛盾。令${e}_{5}$为${e}_{2}$的父节点。我们断言${x}_{2} \in  {e}_{5}$；否则${e}_{1}$必为${e}_{2}$的后代节点，与上述结论矛盾。且${x}_{1} \in  {e}_{5}$；否则${x}_{1}$的连接性质不成立。由于${e}_{2} \cap  {e}_{5} \cap  \overline{\mathbf{y}} \neq  \varnothing$，则${e}_{2} \notin  {\mathcal{E}}_{\text{con }}$。继而$\left( {{e}_{2} - {e}_{5}}\right)  \cap  \mathbf{y} = \varnothing$；否则任何输出属性$x \in  \left( {{e}_{2} - {e}_{5}}\right)  \cap  \mathbf{y}$的连接性质不成立。鉴于${e}_{2} \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{ * }$与$\left( {{e}_{2} - {e}_{5}}\right)  \cap  \mathbf{y} = \varnothing$，必有$\left( {{e}_{2} - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } - {e}_{5}}\right)  \cap  \overline{\mathbf{y}} \neq  \varnothing$；否则由于${e}_{5}$的存在，${e}_{2}$可从${\mathcal{E}}_{ * }$中移除。设${x}_{3} \in  \left( {{e}_{2} - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } - {e}_{5}}\right)  \cap  \overline{\mathbf{y}}$为任意非输出属性。因${x}_{3} \notin  {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  }$，必存在${e}_{2}$的某个子节点${e}_{3}$在$\mathcal{T}$中，使得${x}_{3} \in  {e}_{2} \cap  {e}_{3}$。由于${x}_{1} \notin  {e}_{2},{x}_{1} \notin  {e}_{3}$。又因${x}_{3} \notin  {e}_{5},{x}_{3} \notin  {e}_{1}$。且不存在同时包含${x}_{1},{x}_{3}$的关系。至此，我们已识别出以${x}_{1},{x}_{2},{x}_{3}$和${e}_{1},{e}_{2},{e}_{3}$为要素的钩核结构。

LEMMA A.2. For a free-connex ${CQQ} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E},\mathbf{y}}\right)$ ,if it does not contain a $q$ -core, ${\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } \subseteq  \overline{\mathbf{y}}$ or $\overline{\mathbf{y}} \subseteq  {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  }$ .

引理A.2. 对于自由连接（free-connex）的${CQQ} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E},\mathbf{y}}\right)$，若其不包含$q$核（core），则${\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } \subseteq  \overline{\mathbf{y}}$或$\overline{\mathbf{y}} \subseteq  {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  }$。

Proof of Lemma A.2. It suffices to show that if $y \cap  {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } = \varnothing$ ,then ${\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } \subseteq  \overline{\mathbf{y}}$ . Suppose $x \in  \mathbf{y} \cap  {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  }$ with $x \in  e$ for the unique $e \in  \mathcal{E}$ . We start with relation $e$ ,and add it to $X$ . We keep adding any relation ${e}^{\prime }$ that shares some common attribute with any $e \in  X$ to $X$ ,until we find some ${e}^{\prime }$ with ${e}^{\prime \prime } \cap  {e}^{\prime } \cap  \overline{\mathbf{y}} \neq  \varnothing$ for any ${e}^{\prime \prime } \in  X$ ,or all relations in $\mathcal{E}$ have been added to $X$ . Suppose all relations have been added without finding such a relation ${e}^{\prime }$ . Then, $\mathcal{V} - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } \subseteq  \mathrm{y}$ ,i.e., $\bar{\mathrm{y}} \subseteq  {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  }$ . Suppose such a relation ${e}^{\prime }$ is identified. Let ${e}^{\prime \prime } \in  X$ be such a relation that ${e}^{\prime } \cap  {e}^{\prime \prime } \cap  \overline{\mathbf{y}} \neq  \varnothing$ . Let ${x}_{1} \in  {e}^{\prime \prime } \cap  \mathbf{y} - {e}^{\prime }$ and ${x}_{2} \in  {e}^{\prime } \cap  {e}^{\prime \prime } \cap  \overline{\mathbf{y}}$ . Then,we have found a q-core with ${x}_{1},{x}_{2}$ and ${e}^{\prime \prime },{e}^{\prime }$ ,coming to a contradiction.

引理A.2的证明. 只需证明若$y \cap  {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } = \varnothing$，则${\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } \subseteq  \overline{\mathbf{y}}$。假设存在唯一$e \in  \mathcal{E}$使得$x \in  \mathbf{y} \cap  {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  }$且$x \in  e$。我们从关系$e$出发，将其加入$X$。持续添加任何与$e \in  X$共享属性的关系${e}^{\prime }$至$X$，直至发现某个${e}^{\prime }$对任意${e}^{\prime \prime } \in  X$满足${e}^{\prime \prime } \cap  {e}^{\prime } \cap  \overline{\mathbf{y}} \neq  \varnothing$，或$\mathcal{E}$中所有关系均已加入$X$。若所有关系均已添加而未找到此关系${e}^{\prime }$，则$\mathcal{V} - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } \subseteq  \mathrm{y}$，即$\bar{\mathrm{y}} \subseteq  {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  }$。若识别出此关系${e}^{\prime }$，设${e}^{\prime \prime } \in  X$为满足${e}^{\prime } \cap  {e}^{\prime \prime } \cap  \overline{\mathbf{y}} \neq  \varnothing$的关系，令${x}_{1} \in  {e}^{\prime \prime } \cap  \mathbf{y} - {e}^{\prime }$且${x}_{2} \in  {e}^{\prime } \cap  {e}^{\prime \prime } \cap  \overline{\mathbf{y}}$。此时我们已找到含${x}_{1},{x}_{2}$和${e}^{\prime \prime },{e}^{\prime }$的q核（q-core），导致矛盾。

LEMMA A.3. For a free-connex but non-weak-q-hierarchical ${CQQ}$ with a skeleton ${\mathcal{E}}_{ \bullet  }$ ,if the ${\mathcal{E}}_{ \bullet  }$ - induced ${CQ}$ does not contain a q-core but a hierarchical core, $Q$ must contain a path core.

引理A.3. 对于具有骨架（skeleton）${\mathcal{E}}_{ \bullet  }$的自由连接但非弱q分层（non-weak-q-hierarchical）的${CQQ}$，若${\mathcal{E}}_{ \bullet  }$诱导的${CQ}$不包含q核但存在分层核（hierarchical core），则$Q$必包含路径核（path core）。

Proof of Lemma A.3. As $Q$ contains a hierarchical core,there must exist a pair of attributes ${x}_{2},{x}_{3}$ and three relations ${e}_{2},{e}_{3},{e}_{4} \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{ \Downarrow  }$ such that ${x}_{2} \in  {e}_{2} \cap  {e}_{3} - {e}_{4}$ and ${x}_{3} \in  {e}_{3} \cap  {e}_{4} - {e}_{2}$ . Consider an arbitrary free-connex $\mathcal{T}$ for $Q$ ,whose internal (original) nodes exactly correspond to ${\mathcal{E}}_{ * }$ . Let ${\mathcal{E}}_{\text{con }}$ be the connex subtree of $\mathcal{T}$ . We start with two critical observations: If ${e}_{2}$ is not the root of $\mathcal{T}$ ,let ${e}_{2}^{\prime }$ be the parent of ${e}_{2}$ . We argue that ${e}_{2} - {e}_{2}^{\prime } - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } \neq  \varnothing$ by distinguishing the following two cases:

引理A.3的证明。由于$Q$包含一个层次化核心，必然存在一对属性${x}_{2},{x}_{3}$和三个关系${e}_{2},{e}_{3},{e}_{4} \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{ \Downarrow  }$使得${x}_{2} \in  {e}_{2} \cap  {e}_{3} - {e}_{4}$且${x}_{3} \in  {e}_{3} \cap  {e}_{4} - {e}_{2}$。考虑$Q$的任意自由连接树$\mathcal{T}$，其内部（原始）节点恰好对应于${\mathcal{E}}_{ * }$。令${\mathcal{E}}_{\text{con }}$为$\mathcal{T}$的连接子树。我们从两个关键观察出发：若${e}_{2}$不是$\mathcal{T}$的根节点，设${e}_{2}^{\prime }$为${e}_{2}$的父节点。通过区分以下两种情况论证${e}_{2} - {e}_{2}^{\prime } - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } \neq  \varnothing$：

- if ${e}_{2} \cap  {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } \subseteq  \overline{\mathbf{y}}$ ,then ${e}_{2} - {e}_{2}^{\prime } - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } \neq  \varnothing$ ; otherwise, ${e}_{2}$ can be removed from ${\mathcal{E}}_{ \bullet  }$ due to ${e}_{2}^{\prime }$ ;

- 若${e}_{2} \cap  {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } \subseteq  \overline{\mathbf{y}}$，则${e}_{2} - {e}_{2}^{\prime } - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } \neq  \varnothing$；否则，由于${e}_{2}^{\prime }$，${e}_{2}$可从${\mathcal{E}}_{ \bullet  }$中移除；

- if ${e}_{2} \cap  {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } \cap  \mathbf{y} \neq  \varnothing$ ,then ${e}_{2}^{\prime },{e}_{2} \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{\text{con }}$ ; otherwise,no relation in ${\mathcal{E}}_{\text{con }}$ contains attributes in ${e}_{2} \cap  {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } \cap  \mathrm{y}$ ,contradicting the property of ${\mathcal{E}}_{\text{con }}$ . From the facts that ${e}_{2}^{\prime },{e}_{2} \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{\text{con }}$ ,we observe ${e}_{2}^{\prime } \cap  {e}_{2} \subseteq  \mathbf{y}$ and therefore ${e}_{2} - {e}_{2}^{\prime } - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } \neq  \varnothing$ ; otherwise, ${e}_{2} - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } \subseteq  {e}_{2}^{\prime } \cap  \mathbf{y}$ and ${e}_{2}$ can be removed from ${\mathcal{E}}_{ * }$ due to the existence of ${e}_{2}^{\prime }$ .

- 若${e}_{2} \cap  {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } \cap  \mathbf{y} \neq  \varnothing$，则${e}_{2}^{\prime },{e}_{2} \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{\text{con }}$；否则，${\mathcal{E}}_{\text{con }}$中不存在包含${e}_{2} \cap  {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } \cap  \mathrm{y}$属性的关系，这与${\mathcal{E}}_{\text{con }}$的性质矛盾。根据${e}_{2}^{\prime },{e}_{2} \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{\text{con }}$的事实，我们观察到${e}_{2}^{\prime } \cap  {e}_{2} \subseteq  \mathbf{y}$，因此${e}_{2} - {e}_{2}^{\prime } - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } \neq  \varnothing$；否则，由于${e}_{2}^{\prime }$的存在，${e}_{2} - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } \subseteq  {e}_{2}^{\prime } \cap  \mathbf{y}$和${e}_{2}$可从${\mathcal{E}}_{ * }$中移除。

Similarly,if ${e}_{4}$ is not the root of $\mathcal{T}$ ,let ${e}_{4}^{\prime }$ be the parent of ${e}_{4}$ . We argue that ${e}_{4} - {e}_{4}^{\prime } - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } \neq  \varnothing$ . We are ready to prove Lemma A. 3 by distinguishing two cases: (Case 1) ${e}_{2}$ is not the ancestor of ${e}_{4}$ , nor ${e}_{4}$ is not the ancestor of ${e}_{2}$ . (Case 2) ${e}_{2}$ is the ancestor of ${e}_{4}$ or ${e}_{4}$ is the ancestor of ${e}_{2}$ .

类似地，若${e}_{4}$不是$\mathcal{T}$的根节点，设${e}_{4}^{\prime }$为${e}_{4}$的父节点。我们论证${e}_{4} - {e}_{4}^{\prime } - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } \neq  \varnothing$。现通过区分两种情况完成引理A.3的证明：（情形1）${e}_{2}$不是${e}_{4}$的祖先，且${e}_{4}$也不是${e}_{2}$的祖先；（情形2）${e}_{2}$是${e}_{4}$的祖先，或${e}_{4}$是${e}_{2}$的祖先。

In (Case 1),neither ${e}_{2}$ nor ${e}_{4}$ is the root of $\mathcal{T}$ . In that case, ${e}_{3}$ must not be a decedent of ${e}_{2}$ or ${e}_{4}$ ; otherwise,the connect property of ${x}_{2}$ or ${x}_{3}$ is violated. Let ${e}_{2}^{\prime },{e}_{4}^{\prime }$ be the parent of ${e}_{2},{e}_{4}$ respectively. Implied by the connect property of ${x}_{2}$ and ${x}_{3}$ again, ${x}_{2} \in  {e}_{2}^{\prime }$ and ${x}_{3} \in  {e}_{4}^{\prime }$ . After establishing ${e}_{2} - {e}_{2}^{\prime } - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } \neq  \varnothing$ ,we identify ${x}_{1} \in  {e}_{2} - {e}_{2}^{\prime } - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  }$ as such an arbitrary attribute. Noted that ${x}_{1} \neq  {x}_{2}$ as ${x}_{2} \in  {e}_{2}^{\prime }$ . Some child node ${e}_{1}$ of ${e}_{2}$ must contain ${x}_{1}$ . The ${e}_{5}$ and ${x}_{4}$ can be identified similarly. As ${x}_{3} \notin  {e}_{2}$ ,no relation contains both ${x}_{1},{x}_{3}$ . As ${x}_{4} \notin  {e}_{3}$ ,no relation contains both ${x}_{1},{x}_{4}$ ,or both ${x}_{2},{x}_{4}$ . Hence,we have identified a path core with ${x}_{1},{x}_{2},{x}_{3},{x}_{4}$ and ${e}_{1},{e}_{2},{e}_{3},{e}_{4},{e}_{5}$ .

在(情况1)中，${e}_{2}$和${e}_{4}$都不是$\mathcal{T}$的根节点。此时，${e}_{3}$绝不能是${e}_{2}$或${e}_{4}$的后代节点，否则会违反${x}_{2}$或${x}_{3}$的连接性质。设${e}_{2}^{\prime },{e}_{4}^{\prime }$分别为${e}_{2},{e}_{4}$的父节点。根据${x}_{2}$和${x}_{3}$的连接性质可推知，${x}_{2} \in  {e}_{2}^{\prime }$与${x}_{3} \in  {e}_{4}^{\prime }$。在建立${e}_{2} - {e}_{2}^{\prime } - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } \neq  \varnothing$后，我们确定${x}_{1} \in  {e}_{2} - {e}_{2}^{\prime } - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  }$为任意属性。注意${x}_{1} \neq  {x}_{2}$等同于${x}_{2} \in  {e}_{2}^{\prime }$。${e}_{2}$的某个子节点${e}_{1}$必须包含${x}_{1}$。${e}_{5}$和${x}_{4}$可用类似方法确定。由于${x}_{3} \notin  {e}_{2}$，不存在同时包含${x}_{1},{x}_{3}$的关系。鉴于${x}_{4} \notin  {e}_{3}$，不存在同时包含${x}_{1},{x}_{4}$或同时包含${x}_{2},{x}_{4}$的关系。因此，我们已识别出具有${x}_{1},{x}_{2},{x}_{3},{x}_{4}$和${e}_{1},{e}_{2},{e}_{3},{e}_{4},{e}_{5}$的路径核心。

In (Case 2),assume ${e}_{4}$ is an ancestor of ${e}_{2}$ wlog. Implied by the property of free-connex tree $\mathcal{T}$ , ${e}_{4}$ is an ancestor of ${e}_{3}$ ,and ${e}_{3}$ is an ancestor of ${e}_{2}$ . Let ${e}_{2}^{\prime }$ be the parent of ${e}_{2}$ . Again,we have ${e}_{2} - {e}_{2}^{\prime } - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } \neq  \varnothing$ ,and identify ${x}_{1},{e}_{1}$ as (Case 1). We next focus on how to identify ${e}_{5}$ and ${x}_{4}$ . Let ${e}_{4}^{\prime }$ be the child node of ${e}_{4}$ lying on the path from ${e}_{4}$ to ${e}_{2}$ . Note that ${e}_{4}^{\prime }$ may be the same as ${e}_{3}$ .

在(情况2)中，假设${e}_{4}$是${e}_{2}$的祖先节点（不失一般性）。根据自由连接树$\mathcal{T}$的性质可知，${e}_{4}$是${e}_{3}$的祖先节点，且${e}_{3}$是${e}_{2}$的祖先节点。设${e}_{2}^{\prime }$为${e}_{2}$的父节点。同理可得${e}_{2} - {e}_{2}^{\prime } - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } \neq  \varnothing$，并将${x}_{1},{e}_{1}$识别为(情况1)。接下来我们重点研究如何识别${e}_{5}$和${x}_{4}$。令${e}_{4}^{\prime }$为${e}_{4}$位于到${e}_{2}$路径上的子节点。注意${e}_{4}^{\prime }$可能与${e}_{3}$相同。

We next argue that ${e}_{4} - {e}_{4}^{\prime } - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } \neq  \varnothing$ . If ${e}_{4} \cap  {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } \subseteq  \overline{\mathbf{y}}$ ,we must have ${e}_{4} - {e}_{4}^{\prime } - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } \neq  \varnothing$ ; otherwise, ${e}_{4}$ can be removed from ${\mathcal{E}}_{ * }$ due to the existence of ${e}_{4}^{\prime }$ . Then,it suffices to consider the case with ${e}_{4} \cap  {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } \cap  \mathbf{y} \neq  \varnothing$ . Implied by Lemma A. $2,{x}_{2} \in  \mathbf{y}$ . As ${x}_{2} \notin  {e}_{4}$ ,we must have ${e}_{4},{e}_{4}^{\prime } \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{\text{con }}$ ; otherwise, no relation in ${\mathcal{E}}_{\text{con }}$ contains attribute ${x}_{2}$ ,coming to a contradiction. Implied by the definition of ${\mathcal{E}}_{\text{con }},{e}_{4} \cap  {e}_{4}^{\prime } \subseteq  \mathbf{y}$ . Hence, ${e}_{4} - {e}_{4}^{\prime } - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } \neq  \varnothing$ ; otherwise, ${e}_{4} - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } \subseteq  {e}_{4}^{\prime } \cap  \mathbf{y}$ ,and ${e}_{4}$ can be removed from ${\mathcal{E}}_{ \bullet  }$ ,due to the existence of ${e}_{4}^{\prime }$ ,coming a contradiction.

接下来我们论证${e}_{4} - {e}_{4}^{\prime } - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } \neq  \varnothing$。若${e}_{4} \cap  {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } \subseteq  \overline{\mathbf{y}}$，则必有${e}_{4} - {e}_{4}^{\prime } - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } \neq  \varnothing$；否则，由于${e}_{4}^{\prime }$的存在，${e}_{4}$可从${\mathcal{E}}_{ * }$中移除。因此只需考虑${e}_{4} \cap  {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } \cap  \mathbf{y} \neq  \varnothing$的情况。根据引理A.$2,{x}_{2} \in  \mathbf{y}$可知，当${x}_{2} \notin  {e}_{4}$时必有${e}_{4},{e}_{4}^{\prime } \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{\text{con }}$；否则${\mathcal{E}}_{\text{con }}$中不存在包含属性${x}_{2}$的关系，导致矛盾。根据${\mathcal{E}}_{\text{con }},{e}_{4} \cap  {e}_{4}^{\prime } \subseteq  \mathbf{y}$定义可得，故${e}_{4} - {e}_{4}^{\prime } - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } \neq  \varnothing$；否则因${e}_{4}^{\prime }$存在，${e}_{4} - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } \subseteq  {e}_{4}^{\prime } \cap  \mathbf{y}$且${e}_{4}$可从${\mathcal{E}}_{ \bullet  }$移除，形成矛盾。

After establishing ${e}_{4} - {e}_{4}^{\prime } - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } \neq  \varnothing$ ,we identify ${x}_{4} \in  {e}_{4} - {e}_{4}^{\prime } - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  }$ as such an arbitrary attribute. There must exist some child node ${e}_{5}$ of ${e}_{4}$ ,or the parent node of ${e}_{4}$ that contains ${x}_{4}$ . Noted that ${x}_{2} \notin  {e}_{5}$ ,since ${x}_{2} \in  {e}_{2} \cap  {e}_{3} - {e}_{4}$ ,together with the connect property of ${x}_{2}$ . As ${x}_{3} \notin  {e}_{2}$ and ${x}_{1}$ only appear in relations that are descendants of ${e}_{2}$ ,no relation contains both ${x}_{1},{x}_{3}$ . As ${x}_{4} \notin  {e}_{4}^{\prime }$ ,we have ${x}_{4} \notin  {e}_{3}$ ,and no relation contains both ${x}_{1},{x}_{4}$ ,or both ${x}_{2},{x}_{4}$ . Hence,we have identified a path core with ${x}_{1},{x}_{2},{x}_{3},{x}_{4}$ and ${e}_{1},{e}_{2},{e}_{3},{e}_{4},{e}_{5}$ .

在确立${e}_{4} - {e}_{4}^{\prime } - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } \neq  \varnothing$后，我们将${x}_{4} \in  {e}_{4} - {e}_{4}^{\prime } - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  }$识别为任意属性。必存在${e}_{4}$的某个子节点${e}_{5}$或其父节点包含${x}_{4}$。注意到${x}_{2} \notin  {e}_{5}$，因为${x}_{2} \in  {e}_{2} \cap  {e}_{3} - {e}_{4}$与${x}_{2}$的连接属性共同作用。由于${x}_{3} \notin  {e}_{2}$和${x}_{1}$仅出现在${e}_{2}$后代关系中，故无关系同时包含${x}_{1},{x}_{3}$。根据${x}_{4} \notin  {e}_{4}^{\prime }$可得${x}_{4} \notin  {e}_{3}$，且不存在同时包含${x}_{1},{x}_{4}$或${x}_{2},{x}_{4}$的关系。至此我们已识别出由${x}_{1},{x}_{2},{x}_{3},{x}_{4}$和${e}_{1},{e}_{2},{e}_{3},{e}_{4},{e}_{5}$构成的路径核心。

Proof of Lemma 3.11. Let ${\mathcal{E}}_{ * }$ be the skeleton of $\mathcal{E}$ . Consider a free-connex tree $\mathcal{T}$ for $Q$ ,where ${\mathcal{E}}_{ * }$ is exactly the set of internal nodes of $\mathcal{T}$ . As $Q$ is free-connex but not weak-q-hierarchical,the ${\mathcal{E}}_{ * }$ -induced CQ ${Q}_{ * }$ is not q-hierarchical. If ${Q}_{ * }$ contains a q-core, $Q$ must contain a hook core, implied by Lemma A.1. If ${Q}_{ * }$ does not contain a q-core but a hierarchical core, $Q$ must contain a path core, implied by Lemma A.3.

引理3.11的证明。设${\mathcal{E}}_{ * }$为$\mathcal{E}$的骨架。考虑$Q$的自由连接树$\mathcal{T}$，其中${\mathcal{E}}_{ * }$恰好是$\mathcal{T}$的内部节点集。由于$Q$是自由连接但非弱q-分层的，由${\mathcal{E}}_{ * }$导出的CQ${Q}_{ * }$不具备q-分层性。若${Q}_{ * }$包含q-核，则根据引理A.1可知$Q$必包含钩核；若${Q}_{ * }$不含q-核但存在分层核，则由引理A.3可知$Q$必包含路径核。

Proof of Theorem 3.12. For any FIFO sequence $S$ ,we denote $\tau \left\lbrack  {S,\theta }\right\rbrack$ as the first deletion of any tuple inserted after timestamp $\theta$ . More specially,there exists some tuple $t$ such that $\left( {t,{s}_{1},+, * }\right)  \in  S$ and $\left( {t,\tau \left\lbrack  {S,\theta }\right\rbrack  ,-, * }\right)  \in  S$ with $\theta  \leq  {s}_{1}$ ,but there exists no tuple ${t}^{\prime }$ such that $\left( {{t}^{\prime },{s}_{2},+, * }\right)  \in  S$ with $\theta  \leq  {s}_{2} < \tau \left\lbrack  {S,\theta }\right\rbrack$ . If no tuple is inserted after timestamp $\theta$ ,we set $\tau \left\lbrack  {S,\theta }\right\rbrack   =  + \infty$ . Implied by Lemma 3.11, a free-connex but non-weak-q-hierarchical query must contain Case (1) a path core or Case (2) a hook core.

定理3.12的证明。对任意FIFO序列$S$，记$\tau \left\lbrack  {S,\theta }\right\rbrack$为时间戳$\theta$后插入元组的首次删除。具体而言，存在元组$t$满足$\left( {t,{s}_{1},+, * }\right)  \in  S$且$\left( {t,\tau \left\lbrack  {S,\theta }\right\rbrack  ,-, * }\right)  \in  S$（其中$\theta  \leq  {s}_{1}$），但不存在满足$\left( {{t}^{\prime },{s}_{2},+, * }\right)  \in  S$（其中$\theta  \leq  {s}_{2} < \tau \left\lbrack  {S,\theta }\right\rbrack$）的元组${t}^{\prime }$。若时间戳$\theta$后无插入元组，则设$\tau \left\lbrack  {S,\theta }\right\rbrack   =  + \infty$。根据引理3.11，自由连接但非弱q-分层的查询必包含情形(1)路径核或情形(2)钩核。

For every relation $e \in  \mathcal{E}$ ,we assign $i$ as the unique integer in advance to ensure that at each timestamp, there can be at most one update, and

- if $e \cap  \left\{  {{x}_{1},{x}_{2},{x}_{3},{x}_{4}}\right\}   = \varnothing$ ,we add a tuple (*) at timestamp ${t}_{0} =  - \infty$ and delete it at timestamp ${t}_{1} = \tau \left\lbrack  {{S}^{\prime }, - \infty }\right\rbrack   + {i\epsilon }$ . We reinsert (*) at timestamp ${t}_{1}$ and delete it at timestamp ${t}_{2} = \tau \left\lbrack  {{S}^{\prime },{t}_{1}}\right\rbrack   + {i\epsilon }$ . Again,we reinsert $\left( *\right)$ at timestamp ${t}_{2}$ and delete it at timestamp ${t}_{3} = \tau \left\lbrack  {{S}^{\prime },{t}_{2}}\right\rbrack   + {i\epsilon }$ . We repeat this procedure until $\tau \left\lbrack  {{S}^{\prime },{t}_{k}}\right\rbrack   =  + \infty$ . There are at most $\left| D\right|$ tuples in ${R}_{e}$ .

- 若$e \cap  \left\{  {{x}_{1},{x}_{2},{x}_{3},{x}_{4}}\right\}   = \varnothing$，则在时间戳${t}_{0} =  - \infty$添加元组(*)，并于时间戳${t}_{1} = \tau \left\lbrack  {{S}^{\prime }, - \infty }\right\rbrack   + {i\epsilon }$删除。在时间戳${t}_{1}$重新插入(*)，再于时间戳${t}_{2} = \tau \left\lbrack  {{S}^{\prime },{t}_{1}}\right\rbrack   + {i\epsilon }$删除。此过程循环执行直至$\tau \left\lbrack  {{S}^{\prime },{t}_{k}}\right\rbrack   =  + \infty$，期间在时间戳${t}_{2}$重新插入$\left( *\right)$并于时间戳${t}_{3} = \tau \left\lbrack  {{S}^{\prime },{t}_{2}}\right\rbrack   + {i\epsilon }$删除。${R}_{e}$中最多存在$\left| D\right|$个元组。

For tuples in relations ${R}_{e}$ with $e \cap  \left\{  {{x}_{1},{x}_{2},{x}_{3},{x}_{4}}\right\}   = \varnothing$ , adding these updates won’t violate the FIFO property,since during the lifespan $\left\lbrack  {{t}_{i},{t}_{i + 1}}\right\rbrack$ ,there exists no tuple ${t}^{\prime }$ such that $\left( {{t}^{\prime },{s}_{1},+, * }\right)  \in  {S}^{\prime }$ and $\left( {{t}^{\prime },{s}_{2},-, * }\right)  \in  {S}^{\prime }$ with ${t}_{i} < {s}_{i} < {s}_{2} < {t}_{i + 1}$ ; otherwise $\tau \left\lbrack  {{S}^{\prime },{t}_{i}}\right\rbrack   = {s}_{2}$ ,coming to a contradiction. - If $e \cap  \left\{  {{x}_{1},{x}_{2},{x}_{3}}\right\}   = \varnothing$ ,we add a tuple (*) at timestamp ${t}_{0} =  - \infty$ and delete it at timestamp ${t}_{1} = \tau \left\lbrack  {{S}^{\prime }, - \infty }\right\rbrack   + {i\epsilon }$ . ## B MISSING DETAILS OF SECTION 4

## 附录B 第四章缺失细节

The other three cases for Example 4.6 are illustrated in Figures 18 - 20.

示例4.6的其余三种情况见图18至图20所示。

LEMMA B.1. Algorithm 2 always returns the same answer no matter which ordering is applied to remove reducible relations.

引理B.1. 无论采用何种顺序消除可约关系，算法2始终返回相同结果。

Proof of LEMMA B.1. The reducible relationship can be modeled as a directed graph, where each vertex represents a relation $e$ ,and there exists a direct edge from ${e}_{1}$ to ${e}_{2}$ if ${e}_{1}$ is reducible with ${e}_{2}$ as the anchor,and both $\sigma \left( {e}_{1}\right) ,\sigma \left( {e}_{2}\right)$ are insertion-only. If the graph is acyclic,by running Algorithm 2, the residual query (after removing all possible reducible relations) is unique, with a determined final output. On the other hand,if there exist two relations ${e}_{1},{e}_{2}$ that can be reducible by each other as an anchor,we must have ${e}_{1} - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } = {e}_{2} - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  }$ ,and both $\sigma \left( {e}_{1}\right)$ and $\sigma \left( {e}_{2}\right)$ are insertion-only. We can further distinguish two more cases. If an anchor ${e}_{3}$ exists for both ${e}_{1}$ and ${e}_{2}$ ,such that $\sigma \left( {e}_{3}\right)$ is also insertion-only. In this case, ${e}_{1}$ and ${e}_{2}$ will be removed by ${e}_{3}$ ,no matter in which order, without making any difference to the final output. Otherwise,either ${e}_{1}$ or ${e}_{2}$ remains in the residual query. In this case,the remaining relation will appear as a leaf node of any join tree. As both $\sigma \left( {e}_{1}\right)$ and $\sigma \left( {e}_{2}\right)$ are insertion-only and ${e}_{1} - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } = {e}_{2} - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  }$ ,this remaining relation won’t change the if-condition of Algorithm 2. Hence, Algorithm 2 returns the same answer.

引理B.1的证明。可约关系可建模为有向图，其中每个顶点代表一个关系$e$，若${e}_{1}$以${e}_{2}$为锚点可约且$\sigma \left( {e}_{1}\right) ,\sigma \left( {e}_{2}\right)$均为仅插入类型，则存在从${e}_{1}$指向${e}_{2}$的有向边。当图为无环图时，通过运行算法2，剩余查询（移除所有可约关系后）具有唯一性，且最终输出确定。反之，若存在两个关系${e}_{1},{e}_{2}$能互为锚点约简，则必有${e}_{1} - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } = {e}_{2} - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  }$成立，且$\sigma \left( {e}_{1}\right)$与$\sigma \left( {e}_{2}\right)$均为仅插入类型。此时可进一步区分两种情况：若存在公共锚点${e}_{3}$使$\sigma \left( {e}_{3}\right)$也是仅插入类型，则无论处理顺序如何，${e}_{1}$和${e}_{2}$都将被${e}_{3}$移除，且不影响最终输出；否则，${e}_{1}$或${e}_{2}$会保留在剩余查询中。由于$\sigma \left( {e}_{1}\right)$和$\sigma \left( {e}_{2}\right)$均为仅插入类型且满足${e}_{1} - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  } = {e}_{2} - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  }$，该剩余关系不会改变算法2的条件判断，因此算法2返回结果不变。

Proof of Lemma 4.4. Let ${\mathcal{E}}_{ * }$ be the skeleton of $\mathcal{E}$ . Let ${\mathcal{T}}_{ * }$ be the free-connex join tree of ${\mathcal{E}}_{ * }$ - induced CQ. We construct a free-connex join tree $\mathcal{T}$ for $Q$ as follows. For every relation $e \in  \mathcal{E} - {\mathcal{E}}_{ * }$ , some relation ${e}^{\prime } \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{ * }$ must exist such that ${e}^{\prime }$ is an anchor of $e$ . We just add $e$ as a child of ${e}^{\prime }$ . From Lemma 3.11,as $Q$ is not weak-q-hierarchical,we can always find a path core or hook core. In addition,we aim to identify either a path core where ${e}_{1}$ and ${e}_{5}$ are leaf or root nodes in the join tree $\mathcal{T}$ ,or a hook core where ${e}_{3}$ is a leaf node in $\mathcal{T}$ . Since no simplification steps can be applied,for such a path core,either $\sigma \left( {e}_{1}\right)$ or $\sigma \left( {e}_{2}\right)$ ,and either $\sigma \left( {e}_{4}\right)$ or $\sigma \left( {e}_{5}\right)$ are not insertion-only. Similarly, for such a hook core,either $\sigma \left( {e}_{2}\right)$ or $\sigma \left( {e}_{3}\right)$ is not insertion-only.

引理4.4的证明。设${\mathcal{E}}_{ * }$为$\mathcal{E}$的骨架。令${\mathcal{T}}_{ * }$是${\mathcal{E}}_{ * }$诱导的合取查询的自由连接树。我们按如下方式为$Q$构建自由连接树$\mathcal{T}$：对于每个关系$e \in  \mathcal{E} - {\mathcal{E}}_{ * }$，必存在某个关系${e}^{\prime } \in  {\mathcal{E}}_{ * }$使得${e}^{\prime }$成为$e$的锚点。我们只需将$e$添加为${e}^{\prime }$的子节点。根据引理3.11，由于$Q$非弱q分层结构，总能找到路径核心或钩型核心。此外，我们旨在识别两种情形：在连接树$\mathcal{T}$中${e}_{1}$和${e}_{5}$为叶节点或根节点的路径核心，或${e}_{3}$在$\mathcal{T}$中为叶节点的钩型核心。由于无法应用简化步骤，对此类路径核心而言，$\sigma \left( {e}_{1}\right)$或$\sigma \left( {e}_{2}\right)$与$\sigma \left( {e}_{4}\right)$或$\sigma \left( {e}_{5}\right)$必非仅插入属性；同理，对此类钩型核心，$\sigma \left( {e}_{2}\right)$或$\sigma \left( {e}_{3}\right)$必非仅插入属性。

(Path core). We first consider the path core of five relations ${e}_{1},{e}_{2},{e}_{3},{e}_{4},{e}_{5} \in  \mathcal{E}$ and four attributes ${x}_{1},{x}_{2},{x}_{3},{x}_{4} \in  \mathcal{V}$ with ${x}_{1} \in  {e}_{1} \cap  {e}_{2},{x}_{2} \in  {e}_{2} \cap  {e}_{3},{x}_{3} \in  {e}_{3} \cap  {e}_{4},{x}_{4} \in  {e}_{4} \cap  {e}_{5}$ ,such that there exists no relation $e \in  \mathcal{E}$ containing both ${x}_{i},{x}_{j}$ with $\left| {j - i}\right|  > 1$ . We identify a relation ${e}_{1}^{\prime } \in  \mathcal{E}$ as follows:

(路径核心)。首先考察由五个关系${e}_{1},{e}_{2},{e}_{3},{e}_{4},{e}_{5} \in  \mathcal{E}$和四个属性${x}_{1},{x}_{2},{x}_{3},{x}_{4} \in  \mathcal{V}$构成的路径核心，其中${x}_{1} \in  {e}_{1} \cap  {e}_{2},{x}_{2} \in  {e}_{2} \cap  {e}_{3},{x}_{3} \in  {e}_{3} \cap  {e}_{4},{x}_{4} \in  {e}_{4} \cap  {e}_{5}$满足不存在同时包含${x}_{i},{x}_{j}$与$\left| {j - i}\right|  > 1$的关系$e \in  \mathcal{E}$。按如下方式确定关系${e}_{1}^{\prime } \in  \mathcal{E}$：

- If ${e}_{1}$ is reducible,then ${e}_{1}$ is identified as ${e}_{1}^{\prime }$ ;

- 若${e}_{1}$可约，则将${e}_{1}$标识为${e}_{1}^{\prime }$；

- If there exists a leaf node $e \in  \mathcal{E}$ with $\left\{  {{e}_{2},{e}_{3},{e}_{4},{e}_{5}}\right\}   \cap  \operatorname{path}\left( {{e}_{1},e}\right)  \neq  \varnothing$ ,then $e$ is identified as ${e}_{1}^{\prime }$ ;

- 若存在带有$\left\{  {{e}_{2},{e}_{3},{e}_{4},{e}_{5}}\right\}   \cap  \operatorname{path}\left( {{e}_{1},e}\right)  \neq  \varnothing$的叶节点$e \in  \mathcal{E}$，则将$e$标识为${e}_{1}^{\prime }$；

- Otherwise,the root $r$ of $\mathcal{T}$ is identified as ${e}_{1}^{\prime }$ . For an arbitrary leaf node $e \in  \mathcal{E}$ ,if $\left\{  {{e}_{2},{e}_{3},{e}_{4},{e}_{5}}\right\}   \cap$ $\operatorname{path}\left( {{e}_{1},e}\right)  \neq  \varnothing$ ,we observe that $\left\{  {{e}_{2},{e}_{3},{e}_{4},{e}_{5}}\right\}   \subseteq  \operatorname{path}\left( {{e}_{1},e}\right)$ . Suppose not, $\left\{  {{e}_{2},{e}_{3},{e}_{4},{e}_{5}}\right\}   \nsubseteq$ $\operatorname{path}\left( {{e}_{1},e}\right)$ . There must exist some $i \in  \{ 2,3,4,5\}$ such that ${e}_{i} \in  \operatorname{path}\left( {{e}_{1},e}\right)$ but ${e}_{i + 1} \notin  \operatorname{path}\left( {{e}_{1},e}\right)$ or ${e}_{i - 1} \notin  \operatorname{path}\left( {{e}_{1},e}\right)$ . Wlog,assume ${e}_{i + 1} \notin  \operatorname{path}\left( {{e}_{1},e}\right)$ . Then, ${e}_{1} \in  \operatorname{path}\left( {{e}_{i},{e}_{i + 1}}\right)$ ,violating the connect property of ${x}_{i}$ . As $\left\{  {{e}_{2},{e}_{3},{e}_{4},{e}_{5}}\right\}   \subseteq  \operatorname{path}\left( {{e}_{1},e}\right)$ for every leaf node $e \in  \mathcal{E}$ ,then $e$ must only have one child,as well as the root $r$ . Hence, $r$ is also a reducible relation with its unique child as the anchor. Moreover, $\left\{  {{e}_{2},{e}_{3},{e}_{4},{e}_{5}}\right\}   \cap  \operatorname{path}\left( {{e}_{1},r}\right)  = \varnothing$ .

- 否则，将$\mathcal{T}$的根节点$r$标识为${e}_{1}^{\prime }$。对于任意叶节点$e \in  \mathcal{E}$，若$\left\{  {{e}_{2},{e}_{3},{e}_{4},{e}_{5}}\right\}   \cap$$\operatorname{path}\left( {{e}_{1},e}\right)  \neq  \varnothing$，则可观察到$\left\{  {{e}_{2},{e}_{3},{e}_{4},{e}_{5}}\right\}   \subseteq  \operatorname{path}\left( {{e}_{1},e}\right)$。假设不成立时，$\left\{  {{e}_{2},{e}_{3},{e}_{4},{e}_{5}}\right\}   \nsubseteq$$\operatorname{path}\left( {{e}_{1},e}\right)$。必然存在某个$i \in  \{ 2,3,4,5\}$使得${e}_{i} \in  \operatorname{path}\left( {{e}_{1},e}\right)$但${e}_{i + 1} \notin  \operatorname{path}\left( {{e}_{1},e}\right)$或${e}_{i - 1} \notin  \operatorname{path}\left( {{e}_{1},e}\right)$。不失一般性，假设${e}_{i + 1} \notin  \operatorname{path}\left( {{e}_{1},e}\right)$成立。此时${e}_{1} \in  \operatorname{path}\left( {{e}_{i},{e}_{i + 1}}\right)$将违反${x}_{i}$的连接性质。由于每个叶节点$e \in  \mathcal{E}$都满足$\left\{  {{e}_{2},{e}_{3},{e}_{4},{e}_{5}}\right\}   \subseteq  \operatorname{path}\left( {{e}_{1},e}\right)$，则$e$必定仅有一个子节点，根节点$r$亦然。因此，$r$也是可约关系，其唯一子节点作为锚点。此外，$\left\{  {{e}_{2},{e}_{3},{e}_{4},{e}_{5}}\right\}   \cap  \operatorname{path}\left( {{e}_{1},r}\right)  = \varnothing$。

Note that ${e}_{1}^{\prime }$ is reducible and $\left\{  {{e}_{2},{e}_{3},{e}_{4},{e}_{5}}\right\}   \cap  \operatorname{path}\left( {{e}_{1},{e}_{1}^{\prime }}\right)  = \varnothing$ . We similarly find a reducible relation ${e}_{5}^{\prime } \in  \mathcal{E}$ such that $\left\{  {{e}_{1},{e}_{2},{e}_{3},{e}_{4}}\right\}   \cap  \operatorname{path}\left( {{e}_{5},{e}_{5}^{\prime }}\right)  = \varnothing$ . Moreover,path $\left( {{e}_{1},{e}_{1}^{\prime }}\right)  \cap  \operatorname{path}\left( {{e}_{5},{e}_{5}^{\prime }}\right)  = \varnothing$ . Let $I \subseteq  \mathcal{V}$ be the set of join attributes appearing in any pair of relations in path $\left( {{e}_{1},{e}_{1}^{\prime }}\right)$ ,and let $J \subseteq  \mathcal{V}$ be the set of join attributes appearing in any pair of relations in path $\left( {{e}_{5},{e}_{5}^{\prime }}\right)$ . For every attribute $x \in  \mathcal{V} - I - J - \left\{  {{x}_{1},{x}_{2},{x}_{3},{x}_{4}}\right\}$ ,we set a special value $\{  * \}$ in its domain.

注意${e}_{1}^{\prime }$是可约关系且$\left\{  {{e}_{2},{e}_{3},{e}_{4},{e}_{5}}\right\}   \cap  \operatorname{path}\left( {{e}_{1},{e}_{1}^{\prime }}\right)  = \varnothing$。我们类似地找到一个可约关系${e}_{5}^{\prime } \in  \mathcal{E}$使得$\left\{  {{e}_{1},{e}_{2},{e}_{3},{e}_{4}}\right\}   \cap  \operatorname{path}\left( {{e}_{5},{e}_{5}^{\prime }}\right)  = \varnothing$成立。此外，路径$\left( {{e}_{1},{e}_{1}^{\prime }}\right)  \cap  \operatorname{path}\left( {{e}_{5},{e}_{5}^{\prime }}\right)  = \varnothing$。设$I \subseteq  \mathcal{V}$为路径$\left( {{e}_{1},{e}_{1}^{\prime }}\right)$中任意关系对出现的连接属性集合，$J \subseteq  \mathcal{V}$为路径$\left( {{e}_{5},{e}_{5}^{\prime }}\right)$中任意关系对出现的连接属性集合。对于每个属性$x \in  \mathcal{V} - I - J - \left\{  {{x}_{1},{x}_{2},{x}_{3},{x}_{4}}\right\}$，我们在其定义域中设置特殊值$\{  * \}$。

After identifying the path core and ${e}_{1}^{\prime },{e}_{5}^{\prime }$ ,we can use $\mathcal{Q}$ to simulate ${\mathcal{Q}}_{\text{path }}$ . If $\mathcal{Q}$ can be maintained in $O\left( 1\right)$ ,then we can maintain ${Q}_{\text{path }}$ under $\sigma \left( \left\{  {{e}_{1},{e}_{2},{e}_{3},{e}_{4},{e}_{5}}\right\}  \right)$ ,which contradicts to Example 4.6.

在确定路径核心与${e}_{1}^{\prime },{e}_{5}^{\prime }$后，可采用$\mathcal{Q}$来模拟${\mathcal{Q}}_{\text{path }}$。若$\mathcal{Q}$能在$O\left( 1\right)$中维持，则我们可在$\sigma \left( \left\{  {{e}_{1},{e}_{2},{e}_{3},{e}_{4},{e}_{5}}\right\}  \right)$条件下维持${Q}_{\text{path }}$，这与示例4.6相矛盾。

(Hook core). We consider the hook core of three relations ${e}_{1},{e}_{2},{e}_{3}$ and three distinct attributes ${x}_{1} \in  \mathbf{y},{x}_{2},{x}_{3} \in  \overline{\mathbf{y}}$ ,with ${x}_{1} \in  {e}_{1} - {e}_{2} - {e}_{3},{x}_{2} \in  {e}_{1} \cap  {e}_{2} - {e}_{3},{x}_{3} \in  {e}_{2} \cap  {e}_{3} - {e}_{1}$ such that there exists no relation $e \in  \mathcal{E}$ both ${x}_{1}$ and ${x}_{3}$ . Note that ${e}_{3}$ must be a descendent of ${e}_{1}$ . Suppose not,we consider ${e}^{\prime }$ as the lowest common ancestor of ${e}_{1}$ and ${e}_{3}$ . Note that ${x}_{2} \in  e$ or ${x}_{3} \in  e$ . It could be the case that ${e}^{\prime } = {e}_{3}$ . Let $e$ be the child of ${e}^{\prime }$ that is also an ancestor of ${e}_{1}$ . If ${x}_{2} \in  {e}^{\prime },{x}_{2} \in  e$ ; otherwise,the connect property of ${x}_{2}$ is not preserved in $\mathcal{T}$ . Similarly,if ${x}_{3} \in  {e}^{\prime },{x}_{3} \in  e$ ; otherwise,the connect property of ${x}_{3}$ is not preserved in $\mathcal{T}$ . As $e \cap  {e}^{\prime } \cap  \widetilde{\mathbf{y}} \neq  \varnothing ,e \notin  {\mathcal{E}}_{\text{con }}$ . Hence,no relation in ${\mathcal{E}}_{\text{con }}$ contains ${x}_{1}$ , coming to a contradiction. We identify a relation ${e}_{3}^{\prime } \in  \mathcal{E}$ as follows:

(钩状核心)。我们考察由三个关系${e}_{1},{e}_{2},{e}_{3}$和三个不同属性${x}_{1} \in  \mathbf{y},{x}_{2},{x}_{3} \in  \overline{\mathbf{y}}$构成的钩状核心，其中${x}_{1} \in  {e}_{1} - {e}_{2} - {e}_{3},{x}_{2} \in  {e}_{1} \cap  {e}_{2} - {e}_{3},{x}_{3} \in  {e}_{2} \cap  {e}_{3} - {e}_{1}$满足不存在同时包含${x}_{1}$和${x}_{3}$的关系$e \in  \mathcal{E}$。注意${e}_{3}$必须是${e}_{1}$的后代节点。假设不成立，则将${e}^{\prime }$视为${e}_{1}$与${e}_{3}$的最低公共祖先。需注意${x}_{2} \in  e$或${x}_{3} \in  e$可能存在${e}^{\prime } = {e}_{3}$的情况。令$e$作为${e}^{\prime }$的子节点且同时是${e}_{1}$的祖先节点。若${x}_{2} \in  {e}^{\prime },{x}_{2} \in  e$成立；否则${x}_{2}$的连接属性无法在$\mathcal{T}$中保持。同理，若${x}_{3} \in  {e}^{\prime },{x}_{3} \in  e$成立；否则${x}_{3}$的连接属性无法在$\mathcal{T}$中保持。由于$e \cap  {e}^{\prime } \cap  \widetilde{\mathbf{y}} \neq  \varnothing ,e \notin  {\mathcal{E}}_{\text{con }}$，故${\mathcal{E}}_{\text{con }}$中不存在包含${x}_{1}$的关系，导致矛盾。我们按以下方式识别关系${e}_{3}^{\prime } \in  \mathcal{E}$：

- If ${e}_{3}$ is reducible,then ${e}_{3}$ is identified as ${e}_{3}^{\prime }$ ;

- 若${e}_{3}$可约，则将${e}_{3}$标识为${e}_{3}^{\prime }$；

- If there exists a leaf node $e \in  \mathcal{E}$ with $\left\{  {{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}\right\}   \cap  \operatorname{path}\left( {{e}_{3},e}\right)  \neq  \varnothing$ ,then $e$ is identified as ${e}_{3}^{\prime }$ .

- 若存在带$\left\{  {{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}\right\}   \cap  \operatorname{path}\left( {{e}_{3},e}\right)  \neq  \varnothing$的叶节点$e \in  \mathcal{E}$，则将$e$标识为${e}_{3}^{\prime }$。

Let $I \subseteq  \mathcal{V}$ be the set of join attributes appearing in any pair of relations in path $\left( {{e}_{3},{e}_{3}^{\prime }}\right)$ ,

设$I \subseteq  \mathcal{V}$为路径$\left( {{e}_{3},{e}_{3}^{\prime }}\right)$中任意关系对出现的连接属性集合，

After identifying the hook core and ${e}_{3}^{\prime }$ ,we can use $Q$ to simulate ${Q}_{\text{hook }}$ . If $Q$ can be maintained in $O\left( 1\right)$ ,then we can maintain ${\mathcal{Q}}_{\text{hook }}$ under $\sigma \left( \left\{  {{e}_{1},{e}_{2},{e}_{3}}\right\}  \right)$ ,which contradicts to Example 4.5.

在确定钩子核心与${e}_{3}^{\prime }$后，我们可以使用$Q$来模拟${Q}_{\text{hook }}$。若$Q$能在$O\left( 1\right)$中保持，则我们可在$\sigma \left( \left\{  {{e}_{1},{e}_{2},{e}_{3}}\right\}  \right)$下维持${\mathcal{Q}}_{\text{hook }}$，这与示例4.5相矛盾。

Proof of Lemma 4.16. We start with the case when the height of $\mathcal{T}$ is 2 . By Definition 2.7,for any leaf node $e \in  \mathcal{T}$ and tuple $t \in  {R}_{e},{\lambda }_{\mathcal{T}}\left( t\right)  = 1$ . Conversely,for any non-leaf node $e \in  \mathcal{T}$ ,for any $t \in  {R}_{e}$ ,given that $\mathcal{T}$ is compatible,then for any child node ${e}^{\prime }$ of $e$ on $\mathcal{T}$ ,if $\sigma \left( \left\{  {e}^{\prime }\right\}  \right)$ is insertion-only or $\sigma \left( \left\{  {e,{e}^{\prime }}\right\}  \right)$ is FIFO,we can select only one disjoint effective lifespan in $I\left( t\right)$ ,making ${\lambda }_{\mathcal{T}}\left( t\right)$ also equal to 1 for all non-child nodes of $\mathcal{T}$ . Therefore,the enclosureness ${\lambda }_{\mathcal{T}} = 1$ for all $\sigma$ -sequences. If the height of $\mathcal{T}$ is $\geq  2$ ,by Definition 2.7, ${\lambda }_{\mathcal{T}}$ may be larger than $O\left( 1\right)$ on some $\sigma$ -sequence $S$ . Let $P \mathrel{\text{:=}} \left\langle  {{e}_{1},{e}_{2},\cdots ,{e}_{n}}\right\rangle$ be a path on $\mathcal{T}$ ,such that: $\left( 1\right) {e}_{i} \in  \mathcal{E} : i \in  \left\lbrack  n\right\rbrack  ;\left( 2\right) {e}_{i}$ is the parent node of ${e}_{i + 1}$ on $\mathcal{T}$ for all $1 \leq  i < n$ ; (3) ${e}_{1}$ is the root of $\mathcal{T}$ ,or $p\left( {e}_{1}\right)$ is a generalized relation; (4) ${e}_{n}$ is a leaf node.

引理4.16的证明。我们首先考虑$\mathcal{T}$高度为2的情况。根据定义2.7，对于任意叶节点$e \in  \mathcal{T}$和元组$t \in  {R}_{e},{\lambda }_{\mathcal{T}}\left( t\right)  = 1$。反之，对于任意非叶节点$e \in  \mathcal{T}$，给定$\mathcal{T}$是兼容的，则对于$\mathcal{T}$上$e$的任意子节点${e}^{\prime }$，若$\sigma \left( \left\{  {e}^{\prime }\right\}  \right)$是仅插入类型或$\sigma \left( \left\{  {e,{e}^{\prime }}\right\}  \right)$是先进先出队列，我们可在$I\left( t\right)$中选择唯一不交的有效生命周期，使得$\mathcal{T}$的所有非子节点的${\lambda }_{\mathcal{T}}\left( t\right)$也等于1。因此，所有$\sigma$序列的包容性${\lambda }_{\mathcal{T}} = 1$。当$\mathcal{T}$高度为$\geq  2$时，根据定义2.7，在某些$\sigma$序列$S$上，${\lambda }_{\mathcal{T}}$可能大于$O\left( 1\right)$。设$P \mathrel{\text{:=}} \left\langle  {{e}_{1},{e}_{2},\cdots ,{e}_{n}}\right\rangle$为$\mathcal{T}$上的路径，满足：(1)对所有$1 \leq  i < n$，$\left( 1\right) {e}_{i} \in  \mathcal{E} : i \in  \left\lbrack  n\right\rbrack  ;\left( 2\right) {e}_{i}$是$\mathcal{T}$上${e}_{i + 1}$的父节点；(3)${e}_{1}$是$\mathcal{T}$的根节点，或$p\left( {e}_{1}\right)$为广义关系；(4)${e}_{n}$是叶节点。

Given any path $P$ on $\mathcal{T}$ with $n \geq  2$ ,only $\sigma \left( \left\{  {e}_{2}\right\}  \right)$ can be insertion-only,as if ${e}_{i},{e}_{j}$ exist that are both insertion-only, one node can be removed because the reducible conditions are transitive. Meanwhile,for ${e}_{i} : i \in  \left\lbrack  {3,n}\right\rbrack  ,\sigma \left( \left\{  {{e}_{i - 1},{e}_{i}}\right\}  \right)$ is FIFO,as the requirement of a compatible join tree.

给定$\mathcal{T}$上任意具有$n \geq  2$的路径$P$，仅$\sigma \left( \left\{  {e}_{2}\right\}  \right)$可为仅插入类型——若存在两个${e}_{i},{e}_{j}$都是仅插入类型，由于可约条件具有传递性，可移除其中一个节点。同时，作为兼容连接树的要求，${e}_{i} : i \in  \left\lbrack  {3,n}\right\rbrack  ,\sigma \left( \left\{  {{e}_{i - 1},{e}_{i}}\right\}  \right)$需满足先进先出特性。

LEMMA B.2. For any relation $e$ ,let $Q \mathrel{\text{:=}} {\pi }_{x}{R}_{e}$ for some attribute $x \subseteq  e$ and let $t$ be a result of $Q$ with lifespan $\left\lbrack  {{t}^{ + },{t}^{ - }}\right\rbrack$ . For any ${t}^{\prime } \in  {R}_{e}$ such that ${\pi }_{x}{t}^{\prime } = t$ and ${t}^{\prime  + } \geq  {t}^{ + }$ ,then ${t}^{ - } \geq  {t}^{\prime  - }$ .

引理 B.2。对于任意关系$e$，设$Q \mathrel{\text{:=}} {\pi }_{x}{R}_{e}$为某属性$x \subseteq  e$的取值，且令$t$表示具有生存期$\left\lbrack  {{t}^{ + },{t}^{ - }}\right\rbrack$的$Q$运算结果。若存在${t}^{\prime } \in  {R}_{e}$满足${\pi }_{x}{t}^{\prime } = t$及${t}^{\prime  + } \geq  {t}^{ + }$，则${t}^{ - } \geq  {t}^{\prime  - }$成立。

The correctness follows that the projection operator only extends the lifespan of any input tuple.

正确性源于投影算子仅会延长输入元组的生存期。

First,we assume that $\sigma \left( \left\{  {e}_{2}\right\}  \right)$ is not insertion-only. For any ${t}_{1} \in  {e}_{1}$ with a lifespan of $\left\lbrack  {{t}_{1}^{ + },{t}_{1}^{ - }}\right\rbrack$ , there can be at most one tuple ${t}_{i}$ in ${e}_{i} - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  }$ for any $i \in  \left\lbrack  {2,n}\right\rbrack$ that can join with ${t}_{1}$ ,as ${e}_{i} - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  }$ is a subset of ${e}_{1}$ . Let’s denote ${t}_{i} \in  {e}_{i} - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  }$ as the tuple that can join with ${t}_{1}$ . The tuple ${t}_{1}$ belongs to ${V}_{s}\left( {R}_{1}\right)$ only if ${t}_{2},\cdots ,{t}_{n}$ exist in ${e}_{2} - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  },\cdots ,{e}_{n} - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  }$ and are not in ${V}_{s}\left( {R}_{1}\right)$ if one or more ${t}_{i}$ missed from ${e}_{i} - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  }$ . The tuple ${t}_{2}$ can be inserted into or deleted from ${\pi }_{{e}_{2} - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  }}$ multiple times,resulting in multiple disjoint lifespans. However,there cannot be a lifespan for ${t}_{2}$ such that ${t}_{2}^{ + } \geq  {t}_{1}^{ + }$ and ${t}_{2}^{ - } \leq  {t}_{1}^{ - }$ . According to Lemma B. 2 and the fact that $\sigma \left( \left\{  {{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}\right\}  \right)$ is FIFO. Moreover,within the time interval $\left\lbrack  {{t}_{2}^{ + },{t}_{1}^{ - }}\right\rbrack$ ,there cannot exist two disjoint lifespans for ${t}_{3}$ that are fully covered by the time interval. Otherwise,we could find three tuples ${t}_{1},{t}_{2},{t}_{3}$ from ${R}_{1},{R}_{2},{R}_{3}$ ,such that ${t}_{1}^{ + } \leq  {t}_{2}^{ + } \leq  {t}_{3}^{ + }$ ,and ${t}_{1}^{ - } \geq  {t}_{3}^{ - }$ ,but it must be ${t}_{1}^{ - } \leq  {t}_{2}^{ - }$ and ${t}_{2}^{ - } \leq  {t}_{3}^{ - }$ to ensure the FIFO property on both $\sigma \left( \left\{  {{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}\right\}  \right)$ and $\sigma \left( \left\{  {{e}_{2},{e}_{3}}\right\}  \right)$ ,which cannot exist.

首先，我们假设$\sigma \left( \left\{  {e}_{2}\right\}  \right)$不是仅插入的。对于任何生命周期为$\left\lbrack  {{t}_{1}^{ + },{t}_{1}^{ - }}\right\rbrack$的${t}_{1} \in  {e}_{1}$，由于${e}_{i} - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  }$是${e}_{1}$的子集，在${e}_{i} - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  }$中对于任何$i \in  \left\lbrack  {2,n}\right\rbrack$最多只能存在一个能与${t}_{1}$连接的元组${t}_{i}$。我们将${t}_{i} \in  {e}_{i} - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  }$表示为可与${t}_{1}$连接的元组。当${t}_{2},\cdots ,{t}_{n}$存在于${e}_{2} - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  },\cdots ,{e}_{n} - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  }$中时，元组${t}_{1}$才属于${V}_{s}\left( {R}_{1}\right)$；若${e}_{i} - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  }$中缺失一个或多个${t}_{i}$，则不属于${V}_{s}\left( {R}_{1}\right)$。元组${t}_{2}$可被多次插入${\pi }_{{e}_{2} - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  }}$或从中删除，从而形成多个不连续的生命周期。然而，${t}_{2}$不可能存在同时满足${t}_{2}^{ + } \geq  {t}_{1}^{ + }$和${t}_{2}^{ - } \leq  {t}_{1}^{ - }$的生命周期。根据引理B.2及$\sigma \left( \left\{  {{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}\right\}  \right)$的先进先出特性。此外，在时间区间$\left\lbrack  {{t}_{2}^{ + },{t}_{1}^{ - }}\right\rbrack$内，不可能存在两个完全被该区间覆盖的${t}_{3}$不连续生命周期。否则，我们可以从${R}_{1},{R}_{2},{R}_{3}$中找到三个元组${t}_{1},{t}_{2},{t}_{3}$，使得${t}_{1}^{ + } \leq  {t}_{2}^{ + } \leq  {t}_{3}^{ + }$且${t}_{1}^{ - } \geq  {t}_{3}^{ - }$，但必须满足${t}_{1}^{ - } \leq  {t}_{2}^{ - }$和${t}_{2}^{ - } \leq  {t}_{3}^{ - }$才能保证$\sigma \left( \left\{  {{e}_{1},{e}_{2}}\right\}  \right)$和$\sigma \left( \left\{  {{e}_{2},{e}_{3}}\right\}  \right)$上的先进先出特性，而这种情况不可能存在。

Following the same idea, we can ascertain that there cannot exist any disjoint lifespans created by ${t}_{i} \in  {e}_{i} - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  }$ within the lifespan $\left\lbrack  {{t}_{1}^{ + },{t}_{1}^{ - }}\right\rbrack$ . Consequently,the status of ${t}_{1}$ can only change $O\left( 1\right)$ times in a $\sigma$ -sequence. A similar conclusion can be made for each ${t}_{i} \in  {e}_{i}$ ,by considering the time interval $\left\lbrack  {{t}_{i}^{ + },{t}_{i}^{ - }}\right\rbrack$ resulting in ${\lambda }_{\mathcal{T}} = O\left( 1\right)$ for any $\sigma$ -sequence.

基于相同原理，我们可以确定在生命周期$\left\lbrack  {{t}_{1}^{ + },{t}_{1}^{ - }}\right\rbrack$内不可能存在由${t}_{i} \in  {e}_{i} - {\mathcal{V}}_{ \bullet  }$创建的任何互斥生命周期。因此，${t}_{1}$的状态在$\sigma$序列中只能改变$O\left( 1\right)$次。通过考察时间间隔$\left\lbrack  {{t}_{i}^{ + },{t}_{i}^{ - }}\right\rbrack$导致${\lambda }_{\mathcal{T}} = O\left( 1\right)$的情况，可以对每个${t}_{i} \in  {e}_{i}$得出类似结论。

On the other hand,if $\sigma \left( \left\{  {e}_{2}\right\}  \right)$ is insertion-only and let’s consider ${t}_{2}$ as the first insertion on ${R}_{2}$ with insertion time ${t}_{2}^{ + }$ ,then for any tuple ${t}_{i} \in  {e}_{i},i \in  \left\lbrack  {3,n}\right\rbrack$ such that ${t}_{i}$ is inserted after time ${t}_{2}^{ + },{t}_{i}$ won’t be deleted from ${e}_{i}$ ,otherwise the FIFO property breaks. Additionally,the status of ${t}_{1}$ won’t change until a corresponding ${t}_{2}$ is inserted. Therefore,once ${t}_{1}$ is inserted into ${V}_{s}\left( {R}_{1}\right)$ ,it will never be deleted from ${V}_{s}\left( {R}_{1}\right)$ until ${t}_{1}$ itself is removed from ${R}_{1}$ . This results in ${\lambda }_{\mathcal{T}}\left( t\right)  = O\left( 1\right)$ for all $t \in  {e}_{1}$ . Similarly,as there won’t be any deletion in ${e}_{j}$ for the tuples that are inserted after ${t}_{i}$ with any $j > i$ , the enclosureness of any $t \in  {e}_{i}$ will be 1,making the total enclosureness for ${\lambda }_{\mathcal{T}} = O\left( 1\right)$ .

另一方面，若$\sigma \left( \left\{  {e}_{2}\right\}  \right)$仅为插入操作且将${t}_{2}$视为${R}_{2}$上首次插入（插入时间为${t}_{2}^{ + }$），则任何在${t}_{2}^{ + },{t}_{i}$时刻后插入的元组${t}_{i} \in  {e}_{i},i \in  \left\lbrack  {3,n}\right\rbrack$都不会从${e}_{i}$中删除，否则将破坏FIFO特性。此外，${t}_{1}$的状态在对应${t}_{2}$插入前不会改变。因此，${t}_{1}$一旦插入${V}_{s}\left( {R}_{1}\right)$，在从${R}_{1}$移除前将永久保留，这使得所有$t \in  {e}_{1}$的${\lambda }_{\mathcal{T}}\left( t\right)  = O\left( 1\right)$成立。同理，由于${e}_{j}$中不会删除任何$j > i$条件下晚于${t}_{i}$插入的元组，每个$t \in  {e}_{i}$的包含度均为1，最终使${\lambda }_{\mathcal{T}} = O\left( 1\right)$的总包含度为该值。

## C DELETION-ONLY UPDATE SEQUENCES

## C 纯删除更新序列

In [25],it is observed that for any insertion-only update sequence $S$ ,we can construct a deletion-only update sequence ${S}^{\prime }$ symmetrically: (1) initializing the database as ${D}_{\infty }$ at negative timestamp with an arbitrary order,where ${D}_{\infty }$ is the database at timestamp $+ \infty$ defined by $S$ ; and (2) changing every update $u = \left( {t,s, + ,{R}_{i}}\right)$ to ${u}^{\prime } = \left( {t, - s, - ,{R}_{i}}\right)$ . Note that ${S}^{\prime }$ has the same enclosureness as $S$ ,so it can be maintained with the same cost as $S$ using the algorithm from [25]. Below,we focus on the hardness of deletion-only update sequences.

文献[25]指出，对于任何纯插入更新序列$S$，可对称构造纯删除更新序列${S}^{\prime }$：(1)在负时间戳以任意顺序将数据库初始化为${D}_{\infty }$（即$S$定义的时间戳$+ \infty$对应的数据库状态）；(2)将每个更新操作$u = \left( {t,s, + ,{R}_{i}}\right)$转换为${u}^{\prime } = \left( {t, - s, - ,{R}_{i}}\right)$。注意${S}^{\prime }$与$S$具有相同包含度，故采用[25]算法时维护成本相同。下文将重点分析纯删除更新序列的复杂性。

THEOREM C.1. For any $\epsilon  > 0$ ,there is no data structure for ${\mathcal{Q}}_{7} \mathrel{\text{:=}} {\pi }_{{x}_{1},{x}_{3}}\left( {{R}_{1}\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{2}}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{2}\left( {{x}_{2},{x}_{3}}\right) }\right)$ that can be updated in $O\left( 1\right)$ time while supporting $O\left( 1\right)$ -delay enumeration over any deletion-only update sequences, assuming the BMM conjecture.

定理C.1. 在BMM猜想成立的前提下，对于任意$\epsilon  > 0$，不存在能同时满足以下条件的数据结构${\mathcal{Q}}_{7} \mathrel{\text{:=}} {\pi }_{{x}_{1},{x}_{3}}\left( {{R}_{1}\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{2}}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{2}\left( {{x}_{2},{x}_{3}}\right) }\right)$：支持在$O\left( 1\right)$时间内完成更新，并对任何纯删除更新序列实现$O\left( 1\right)$延迟枚举。

Proof. Given an instance of BMM $M \times  {M}^{\prime }$ ,where the two matrices $M,{M}^{\prime }$ has size $n \times  n$ ,we can create the following deletion-only update sequence on ${Q}_{6}$ as follows: (1) we insert all tuples $\left( {i,j}\right)  \in  \left\lbrack  n\right\rbrack   \times  \left\lbrack  n\right\rbrack$ into ${R}_{1}$ and ${R}_{2}$ at negative timestamps; (2) for any(i,j)where ${M}_{ij} = 0$ ,we delete (i,j)from ${R}_{1}$ ; similarly,for any(i,j)where ${M}_{ij}^{\prime } = 0$ ,we delete(i,j)from ${R}_{2}$ ; (3) after all deletions are done,we enumerate the query result. It is clear that if this CQ can be updated in $O\left( 1\right)$ time while supporting $O\left( 1\right)$ -delay enumeration over any deletion-only update sequence,then by the BMM problem in time at most $O\left( {{n}^{2} \times  1 + {n}^{2} \times  1}\right)  = O\left( {n}^{2}\right)$ ,which contradicts to BMM conjecture.

证明。给定一个BMM（布尔矩阵乘法）问题实例$M \times  {M}^{\prime }$，其中两个矩阵$M,{M}^{\prime }$的尺寸为$n \times  n$，我们可以在${Q}_{6}$上构造如下仅含删除操作的更新序列：(1) 将所有元组$\left( {i,j}\right)  \in  \left\lbrack  n\right\rbrack   \times  \left\lbrack  n\right\rbrack$在负时间戳插入${R}_{1}$和${R}_{2}$；(2) 对于任意满足${M}_{ij} = 0$的(i,j)，从${R}_{1}$中删除(i,j)；同理，对于任意满足${M}_{ij}^{\prime } = 0$的(i,j)，从${R}_{2}$中删除(i,j)；(3) 所有删除操作完成后，枚举查询结果。显然，若该CQ（合取查询）能在$O\left( 1\right)$时间内完成更新并支持$O\left( 1\right)$延迟枚举任意仅删除更新序列，则可在至多$O\left( {{n}^{2} \times  1 + {n}^{2} \times  1}\right)  = O\left( {n}^{2}\right)$时间内解决BMM问题，这与BMM猜想相矛盾。

THEOREM C.2. For any $\epsilon  > 0$ ,there is no data structure for ${\mathcal{Q}}_{8} \mathrel{\text{:=}} {R}_{1}\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{2}}\right)  \ltimes  {R}_{2}\left( {{x}_{2},{x}_{3}}\right)  \ltimes$ ${R}_{3}\left( {{x}_{3},{x}_{1}}\right)$ that can be updated in $O\left( 1\right)$ time while supporting $O\left( 1\right)$ -delay enumeration over any deletion-only update sequences, assuming HC conjecture.

定理C.2。对于任意$\epsilon  > 0$，在HC（超图团）猜想成立的前提下，不存在${\mathcal{Q}}_{8} \mathrel{\text{:=}} {R}_{1}\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{2}}\right)  \ltimes  {R}_{2}\left( {{x}_{2},{x}_{3}}\right)  \ltimes$${R}_{3}\left( {{x}_{3},{x}_{1}}\right)$的数据结构能在$O\left( 1\right)$时间内完成更新，同时支持$O\left( 1\right)$延迟枚举任意仅删除更新序列。

Proof. Given an instance of(3,2)-HC problem,a special case of $\left( {k + 1,k}\right)$ -HC problem,which tries to find all triangles from a graph. A conjecture is derived from the $\left( {k + 1,k}\right)$ -HC conjecture on this special problem: We can create the following deletion-only update sequence on ${Q}_{8}$ to simulate the triangle problem: (1) we initial ${R}_{1},{R}_{2}$ and ${R}_{3}$ with all tuples $\left( {i,j}\right)  \in  \left\lbrack  n\right\rbrack   \times  \left\lbrack  n\right\rbrack  ;\left( 2\right)$ if $\left( {i,j}\right)  \notin  G$ , we delete(i,j)from ${R}_{1},{R}_{2},{R}_{3};\left( 3\right)$ after all the absent entries are deleted,we enumerate the query result. If any results exist,then a triangle is detected from $G$ . For the construction above,there are at most $O\left( {n}^{2}\right)$ updates,making the total running time of $O\left( {n}^{2}\right)$ if the query can be maintained in $O\left( 1\right)$ time,which is a contradiction to the triangle conjecture as well as the HC conjecture.

证明。给定(3,2)-HC问题（即寻找图中所有三角形这一$\left( {k + 1,k}\right)$-HC问题的特例）的实例。基于该特例问题，从$\left( {k + 1,k}\right)$-HC猜想可导出如下猜想：我们可在${Q}_{8}$上构造如下仅删除更新序列来模拟三角形问题：(1) 初始化${R}_{1},{R}_{2}$和${R}_{3}$时，若$\left( {i,j}\right)  \notin  G$则插入所有元组$\left( {i,j}\right)  \in  \left\lbrack  n\right\rbrack   \times  \left\lbrack  n\right\rbrack  ;\left( 2\right)$；(2) 删除所有缺失项后，枚举查询结果。若存在任何结果，则从$G$中检测到三角形。上述构造至多包含$O\left( {n}^{2}\right)$次更新，若查询能在$O\left( 1\right)$时间内维护，则总运行时间为$O\left( {n}^{2}\right)$，这与三角形检测猜想及HC猜想均矛盾。

THEOREM C.3. For any non-free-connex CQ,no index can be updated in $O\left( 1\right)$ amortized time while supporting $O\left( 1\right)$ -delay enumeration over any deletion-only update sequence,assuming the Boolean Matrix Multiplication, Triangle Detection, and HyperClique conjectures.

定理C.3。在布尔矩阵乘法、三角形检测和超图团猜想成立的前提下，对于任何非自由连接CQ（合取查询），不存在索引能在$O\left( 1\right)$摊还时间内完成更新，同时支持$O\left( 1\right)$延迟枚举任意仅删除更新序列。
